The only way for them to be stronger was to be an obedient dog for the high-level Immortal Cultivators.

While a lot of Immortal Cultivators did not mind being a dog for the experts, even more Immortal Cultivators who were ambitious, disobedient, and intended to dominate the world did not want to bend to somebody else and had their necks choked for their entire lives!

The ordinary people could perhaps hold back the pain of being forever exploited by the Immortal Cultivators. They could not make any voice even if they wanted to.

However, the dissatisfaction of the billions of low-level Immortal Cultivators would certainly affect the entire Imperium!

As Immortal Cultivators, they were born to be superior to others. They were meant to dominate the world and conquer the universe, not to be somebody else's dogs!

The low-level Immortal Cultivators had never craved strength more, but in order to get greater strength, they needed money—the real money that belonged to them, not the toilet paper like the "crystal coins of the Imperium", which was closely monitored in every procedure of the trade!

What they desired was such a currency. First of all, it needed a relatively stable value. It did not mean that the currency could not inflate, but there should be a boundary, allowing it to inflate together with the growth of the economy, unlike the rapid devaluation of the crystal coins of the Imperium. Because of the hard work of the cash printers of the four families, the money that was enough to buy a hen today could only buy an egg tomorrow.

Secondly, and more importantly, such a currency must be untraceable and not be monitorable. Only with such features could they exchange resources without alarming anyone and grow rapidly right under the nose of the high-level Immortal Cultivators, eventually challenging their authority.

Where there was oppression, there was resistance. Where there was demand, there would certainly be corresponding products.

Based on the intense demand of the low-level Immortal Cultivators, "free coin" was born.

The free coin was not a currency in the traditional sense but only a virtual currency for fun at the beginning. It was a decentralized, peer-to-peer trade algorithm that was born on the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus.

The free coin did not have any features of the traditional currencies. It was not even issued by any currency agency. Instead, it was generated through a very special algorithm by digging the potentials of the super crystal processors in the entire Imperium. The trade records were recorded on the scattered databases on the nodes of the Spiritual Nexus. The advantages in the algorithm design and the multiple cryptologic characteristics ensured the absolute security of the currency.

Li Yao was not a student of economics, and he did not know much about the virtual currencies. Therefore, he did not quite understand the essence of the free coins. Perhaps another way of describing it was that the free coin was in fact a virtual, anonymous ledger that recorded all the trades that the user made, but every user in the entire Imperium had such a ledger, and the data on them could be updated synchronically.

In such a way, no hacker could possibly change the trade data of the free coins. It couldn't have been more secure.

Also, the free coins were not issued by any bank but "mined" by digging the potentials of all the super crystal processors in the entire Imperium.

In a given period, the potentials of the super crystal processors were stable, and the number of free coins would be expectable. So, the value of the currency would be relatively stable.

What if the mining potentials of the super crystal processors significantly increased?

It meant that the super crystal processors had grown larger in number and become more powerful. The modern civilization was to a large extent a civilization of crystal processors. The upgrade of super crystal processors meant the development of the economy. Then, the addition of the free coins wouldn't be a problem at all. It was almost perfect!

Of course, there was still a major chasm for the free coin to become a real currency, which had to be jumped over in a most dangerous jump with the wings that were known as credit.

Currency was credit. A currency without credit would be no different from the currency in a virtual game.

The free coin was merely a concept created by a bunch of crystal processor specialists among the space pirates at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium in their boredom.

The purpose of their creation was to use it as a record for the black market trades among the space pirates to avoid the attack of the authorities.

The credit of the currency was the armed forces of the space pirates. Therefore, it did not entail any value outside of the circle of space pirates.

However, in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium, the so-called "space pirates" were in fact just a synonym of "privateers". Most of the powerful, major pirate gangs were the troops of the warlords under a different skin.

As the free coin spread out among the pirate gangs, it immediately entered the eyesight of the local warlords and Sector Master at the edge of the Imperium.

The exploitation of the central worlds of the Imperium on the peripheral worlds was extremely cruel. The dump of goods and the price scissors were almost killing the local dominators. But as it happened, their entire economic system in the past was based on the crystal coins of the Imperium. It was impossible for them to hide anything to themselves.

The appearance of the free coin gave hope to the local dominators. Under their intentional indulgence and even encouragement, the free coin soon became one of the most important trade methods on the black markets in the periphery of the Imperium. Also, it was given new credit that was guaranteed by the local dominators with their guns.

Even so, the newly born free coin was still like a baby in the cradle that was vulnerable and helpless and could be suffocated easily.

However, a serious economic crisis took place in the Imperium a hundred years ago.

A hundred years ago, the Imperium suffered an unprecedented failure in the frontline. The Black Wind Fleet, known as the strongest border defense army, was crushed. Five highly militarized, heavy industrial worlds were lost to the Covenant Alliance, whose army was ready to march toward the heartland of the Imperium.

For a moment, all kinds of rumors were spreading out. Everybody was panicking, as if the world would end soon.

But as it happened, the Black Wind Fleet did not stop there. Knowing that the court would not let go of them after the failure, they simply gathered the defeated soldiers and wandered about in the heartland of the Imperium, shouting that they would correct their mistake by conquering the desolate worlds at the edge of the Imperium. But of course, they could not embark on their expedition with a hungry belly, and the court had to pay them a large sum of compensation and military budget.

Judging from the shameless behavior of the scoundrels, it was obvious that they would surrender to the Covenant Alliance and let everybody die with them.

The emperor at that time was rather weak, and the four families were not united. In the end, they agreed to Black Wind Fleet's blackmail despite their anger, paying a huge amount of money to appease the crushed soldiers.

As it turned out, the crushed soldiers left with the tremendous fees, only to be killed by the Star Glory Federation at the edge of the cosmos a hundred years later.

That was the other part of the story. Focusing on the time of the failure, it was "Marquess Liaohai" Lei Chenghu who stood out and saved the sky, preventing the attacks of the aggressive Covenant Alliance. However, the Imperium suffered a major setback again. The military expenses and compensations were all astronomical. There was also the chain reaction on the financial market due to the loss of five Sectors. It would be a miracle if an economic crisis didn't take place!

The economic crisis led to the collapse of the cabinet of "Silver Fox" Li Jiande, who was the Prime Minister of the Imperium at that time. It also disrupted the last bit of the credit of the crystal coins of the Imperium.

Now, the free coin's credit was approximately zero, but it had a stable value and could be freely traded without being monitored.

The crystal coin's credit, on the other hand, was also close to zero, but it could crazily depreciate at any moment, and every procedure of the trade was tightly monitored.

Even an idiot would know which one to choose!

Merchants always had the keenest senses when it came to money.

Right when the Black Wind Fleet was crushed in the frontline, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, which had been secretly developing, had already foreseen the upcoming economic crisis. Don't forget that Di Feiwen, the deputy commander of the Black Wind Fleet, worked for the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors too. The Black Wind Fleet's blackmail might've been related to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, too.

Through Di Feiwen and other people, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors helped the Black Wind Fleet to blackmail the court, partly boosting the outbreak of the economic crisis. On the other hand, it wagered on the chips it had on the free coin!

Chapter 2516: Internet of Planets

While the most prosperous worlds of the Imperium of True Human Beings were devoted to the meaningless attritions such as power struggles and coups, the seemingly insignificant and harmless Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors rose quietly in the remote lands at the edge of the Imperium.

The situation in the Pangu universe at this moment was entirely different from a hundred thousand years ago.

During the first hundred thousand years after the birth of mankind, every Sector basically met the conditions of self-sufficiency. Every habitable planet boasted enough water, air, minerals, and a steady ecological system. Even without trades with the outside world, it could still maintain an internal circulation of the minimal degree.

However, since the end of the ancient Cultivators, from the war that consumed the three thousand Sectors, to the rise and decline of demons, going down to the "Armageddon Rebellion" fought between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God. The fire of war never truly died out in the Pangu universe.

The self-circulating systems in many worlds were destroyed in the wars. The atmosphere was gone, the mines were emptied, the water was contaminated, and the docile, mild animals turned into ferocious demon beasts.

It was needless to say that technological progress enabled people to conquer the resource planets that were previously unconquerable. In the places absolutely unsuitable for the survival of mankind, massive settlements that could accommodate millions of people had been established.

The result was that at the edge of the Imperium where the resources were limited, most of the habitable planets lost the ability of total self-sufficiency, if they had the ability in the first place at all. They had to count on the busy interstellar trades to bring them the resources necessary for their survival.

Therefore, the importance of interstellar trades couldn't have been more highlighted. Many specialists had even pointed out that the future was not just an age of the Spiritual Nexus but also an age of the logistics network and an age of the "Internet of Planets". Whoever controlled the trades among the habitable planets would be controlling the whole universe!

In the traditional, prosperous areas of the Imperium, the trade routes were dominated by the four families and the merchants affiliated to them.

However, to build a complete logistics system from scratch in a barren, remote, and unprofitable worlds at the edge of the Imperium and to develop the local market until it was profitable, it required tremendous time and effort.

The four families who had been used to earning extraneous profits through monopolization and taxes were too lazy to deal with the hicks and bumpkins of the peripheral worlds. Therefore, the local trade groups and logistics sects grew wildly on their own. After developing for hundreds of years, they weaved a web of trade, logistics, and finance that seemed unattractive but was in fact impenetrable. Later, the loose forces gathered together and surfaced as "Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors".

The peripheral worlds of the Imperium were a chaotic, lawless area. Space pirates, merchants, privateers, and regular armies were totally the same thing. Having fought a way out in such a bloody jungle, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors naturally boasted the most audacious sailors and captains, the most sleazy businessmen, and the most brutal space pirates.

They monopolized the business routes that were like capillary vessels. They could bring incessant resources and talents to a planet, allowing the planet to rise and thrive quickly. They could also turn into space pirates and completely block a planet's sailing routes to the outside world, forcing the planet to submit to them obediently.

Other than anything else, as long as the warships under the command of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors could blow up all the spiritual towers and the remote communication bases around a planet, casting the planet into a network blackout, it would be enough for the civilization on the planet to degenerate for decades in one year.

With such capabilities, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was naturally unwilling to wander around only at the edge of the Imperium as the supplier of the hicks and bumpkins.

Besides, the leader of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors also knew very clearly that in the eyes of the powerful nobles of the Imperium, the merchants such as them were just pigs to be butchered. The fatter they were, the sooner it would be for them to be sent to the slaughterhouse.

In the past, the four families hadn't seen the profits in the trades at the edge of the Imperium, and therefore, they turned a blind eye to them. Now that they had developed the market through all the hard work and it was profitable, would the four families be Immortal Cultivators at all if they did not come and pick the fruits?

It was better to attack than to be attacked. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that was born before long realized intuitively that it had to fight the "old Immortal Cultivators", namely the powerful lords and magnates at the center of the cosmos, who exploited the ordinary people crudely and violently through the traditional and nontechnical approaches, until only one party stood!

The free coin was exactly the sharpest weapon that they had chosen.

They were about to launch a war, one that was not fought with crystal suits, starships, and Colossi but through money with no blood shed. It was a war of currencies!

Taking advantage of the enormous military pressure from the Covenant Alliance and the virulent inflation in the Imperium, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors secretly began the monetization of the free coin in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium.

At first, naturally, nobody viewed the free coin as real money. However inflated, the crystal coin of the Imperium was the "real money" after all. The regime in the Imperium hadn't been changed yet. Who on earth would trade for those worthless free coins with real money?

Naturally, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors could not force the peripheral worlds to use the free coin, nor did they think to replace the crystal coin with the free coin in the very beginning. They merely stipulated that the stores under their name accepted both currencies, but there was a certain discount if the trade was conducted through the free coin. Also, the most demanded goods would be supplied to the buyers who used the free coin first.

Such a measure finally kicked off the process of monetization.

But the real effective moves were sales on account and microcredit.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was best at making profits in the long run. The "South Spirit Planet", Huo Dongling's hometown, was the perfect example.

First of all, they purchased the planets with the potential for preliminary development using their tremendous wealth and connections. Then, by half selling and half offering, they attracted many colonizers to settle on such planets. When the settlements were well-built and the colonizers had devoted their everything, regarding the planets as their new homes with no turning back, they would earn the money back through all kinds of consumables.

In order to develop those "egg-laying hens", the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors provided technical support, microcredit, and even magical equipment on account for the colonizers during the process with enticing conditions, but they did have one requirement, which was that the loans must be paid off with the free coin in the future.

Paying the loans with the free coin would enjoy a discount on the interest rate, while paying the loans with the crystal coin would be charged with a punitive transfer fee. Of course, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors solemnly promised the stability of the value of the free coin, making sure that its exchange rate with the crystal coin would not change much.

During the period when the crystal coin was most devalued, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors even associated the free coin with compressed air, water cleaning pills, synthetic food, and other indispensable assets, stating that one free coin could be exchanged for a designated amount of water and food. It was then known as "air coin", "water coin", and even "meat can coin".

"Meat can coin" was such a rustic name that the erudite economists of the Imperium who knew all the theories would be laughing aloud if they heard it.

However, for the uneducated common folks at the edge of the Imperium, it was an appropriate, readily understandable name. The free coin was the real money that could be exchanged for air, water, and meat cans, bringing the hope of survival!

Such measures earned the first batch of real users and believers of the free coin besides the space pirates. It also empowered the free coin with real credit.

Ever since then, the free coin had been rising and snowballing unstoppably. By the time the Imperium finally caught its breath from the economic crisis caused by the failure of the Black Wind Fleet, the free coin was already spreading out at the edge of the Imperium like a wildfire and couldn't be extinguished at all.

Besides, even though the economic crisis had passed, the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance wasn't over yet. In the first fifty years, the army had been engaged in active defense under the leadership of Lei Chenghu, but in the second fifty years, they were gritting their teeth preparing for a battle of revenge. As the war machine fulminated, the unnecessary taxes were soaring too.

When the central government intended to levy one dollar of special war tax, the bottom-level branches were bold enough to collect twenty dollars, from the hominoids and the low-level Immortal Cultivators alike. In such days, it was impossible for ordinary people to live well or for the low-level Immortal Cultivators to complete their primitive accumulation for advancement without tax evasion. The only possible means of tax evasion was to use the free coin.

The plethora of taxes was the most incredible assist for the free coin. It was almost like pouring fuel on the fire.

Naturally, it was impossible to entirely abandon the crystal coin. Most of the deals in real life had to be conducted through the legal tender. However, the crucial, clandestine deals became the fields that the free coin infiltrated and corrupted.

For a hundred years, the free coin was like a "kindhearted plague" that most of the people and the low-level Cultivators would like to see, corroding the body of the Imperium. At first, it was only spreading on a small scale at the edge of the Imperium. Then, it gradually went deep into the traditional central areas of the Imperium. In the end, even a lot of Immortal Cultivators within the four Kurfürst families began to use the free coin in secret. Could there be any Immortal Cultivator who never killed other people and looted their belongings? Could they trade with the crystal coin when they sold the items on the black market? They would be asking to be caught by doing so!

Trading with the free coin wouldn't leave any trace behind when they were robbing other people of their treasures, working as assassins or bounty hunters, or secretly purchasing tremendous resources preparing to kill the nemesis in the family after successfully advancing into a new level. It couldn't have been more convenient!

Chapter 2517: The Utterly Incompatible Belief!

Just like that, the free coin gradually finished the process of monetization after a hundred years. It was on par with the crystal coin of the Imperium in many fields, if not above it.

That was why the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was capable of supporting the reformists in their cry for reform and sending tremendous capitals to the central worlds right under the nose of the four families, and the four families could do nothing about it.

On the surface, it was the competition between the reformists and the four families, but actually, it was the competition between the free coin and the crystal coin. That was the mainline of the splendid battles in the past hundred years.

Of course, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors would never confess everything dutifully. The conclusions above were come up with by gathering the broken information including the theories of Di Feiwen who used to be the deputy commander of the Black Wind Fleet, Li Yao's communication with Jin Yuyan who was the director of the magical equipment in the past, Queen Li Linghai's public declarations, Boss Bai's deep understanding about the space pirates of the Imperium, as well as Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King's understanding about the free coin.

It was safe to say that not a single person inside the Imperium except Li Yao could get the firsthand information in so many aspects, making it possible to figure out the general picture of everything.

The leadership of the four families might know that the free coin was scary, but they couldn't have known that it was that scary. People who overly worshipped violence would always underestimate the power of money.

Even Li Yao realized that he had been tricked after sorting everything through. Back then, Di Feiwen did not tell the truth to him at all. The man's claims, including that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was just a harmless organization of a bunch of small merchants at the edge of the Imperium who were struggling to survive, and that the Imperium had been exploiting and threatening them, were all bulls*it!

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors a hundred years ago had already monopolized the logistics and trade network in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium and won the support of the billions of people there. They were also preparing to launch a critical strike on the ruling class of the Imperium with the free coin.

The Black Wind Fleet's rampage at the central area of the Imperium after the debacle and their "expedition" after blackmailing abundant compensations had a lot to do with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors too, because the organization at that time was in dire need of a new economic crisis!

Furthermore, was the collapse of the Black Wind Fleet really inevitable? The five worlds including the Black Wind Fleet that had been safe for nine hundred years might not have been forever unassailable, but they must've been heavily guarded. How on earth were they broken through by the Covenant Alliance unstoppably? Was there more to the incident?

Those things had already been buried in the dust of history, but they still reminded Li Yao that the Immortal Cultivators of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were also Immortal Cultivators, who were even more sleazy and cunning. A simple and dutiful Cultivator like Li Yao must be very cautious when dealing with them. One moment of carelessness, and he would be tricked and even counting money for them when he had been sold by them!

Li Yao secretly felt lucky that he resisted the temptation when he just arrived at the Imperium and did not reach out to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in his most difficult time.

If he had truly been 100% convinced by Di Feiwen and went to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, confessing everything frankly, it was true that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors might've helped him, but he would've also been a chess piece for the organization and a slave of money.

Cooperation was certainly an option, but Li Yao must be the leader of it, and the boundaries of the Cultivators must be respected. It was the unshakeable, fundamental principle.

"All in all, this is about the situation right now. While the Imperium's twenty years of counterattacks on the Covenant Alliance have secured a glorious triumph, a new economic crisis is brewing too, and it may be detonated by a random spark. At this moment, the credit of the crystal coin is already entirely gone. The only thing that supports it is violence, the violence that declares the invincibility of the four families."

Making gestures, Li Yao explained to Li Jialing, "That's why Blackstar the Great attacked the capital at great risks!

"The lackluster capital of the Imperium today seems far worse than the heart of the Imperium five hundred years ago. In terms of military and economy, it is not a big deal to lose it.

"However, from the perspective of economy and confidence, the fall of the capital is like an earthquake that shatters the illusion that the four families are invincible.

"The only reason why the economy in the territory of the four families hasn't collapsed, and why the people still accept the crystal coin in their deals, is the absolute violence that the four families control, but right now, the absolute violence has been greatly challenged, and people's faith in it has been seriously destroyed.

"Do you understand? Wuying Qi does not need to attack the territory of the four families at all. All he needs to do is defend the capital and show his power. The longer the reformists occupy the capital, the less credit the four families will have. By the time their credit is entirely gone, they won't be able to pay wages. Will it help at all however many experts in the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage they have? The Immortal Cultivators at the bottom will rebel, and the social order will fall out. The weight of the four families themselves will collapse them.

"Naturally, the great experts in the four families would not just wait to be killed. However, destruction is always easier than construction. It takes only several days to destroy the credit of a currency, but it will take forever and too many approaches to reestablish people's faith in the crystal coin.

"The most direct approach is naturally to take back the capital and to annihilate the 'illegitimate Reform Committee of the Council of Elders'. However, the four families do not dare to take the risk yet. It's certainly not a joke to jump from the outside world and attack such a dangerous place as the capital. If they suffer another failure in their carelessness, losing face in front of the whole universe, the reign of the four families would be completely doomed.

"Therefore, while postponing under the excuse that the new emperor is about to ascend to the throne, the four families have gathered their troops to attack the Seven Seas Grand Market, the nest of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the heart of the free coin!

"Since it's impossible to reestablish people's faith in the crystal coin soon, they can simply blow the free coin into waste. As long as the business routes of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors at the edge of the Imperium are destroyed, making it impossible for people to purchase the air, water, and food that they live on however many free coins they have, too many people will die of hunger, thirst, and suffocation with free coins in their hands. When the news is out, will the free coin be used by anyone at all?

"This is the whole background of the battle of Seven Seas and the strategic significance of it. So, don't just consider the battle as an overture to the conquest of the capital; it's really a battle that will decide the future of the country!"

Li Yao drank up the iced juice and looked at the twisted universe outside through the glass cup.

The shining stars outside seemed to have become glittering coins.

Money was truly something much more loveable than Divinity Transformation Stage experts. After all, money could be exchanged for food, water, and everything else that people lived on, while the Divinity Transformation Stage experts could bring nothing except slaughter and destruction in most cases.

"Although Brother Yao talked about so many advantages of the free coin, which sounded very nice, I still feel that it isn't right…"

Frowning for a long time, Li Jialing shook his head and said slowly, "How should I put it? I still don't like the free coin, intuitively. I think it is an abnormal monster that cannot be counted as a real currency in any case.

"The most crucial problem is that it is absolutely untraceable and cannot be monitored. Every trade procedure is completely invisible. This is too horrible. It is very easy—no, it is destined to be taken advantage of by the bad guys. Even the good guys will be tempted to become bad guys!

"It is an interesting claim that using the free coin can avoid the redundant taxes of the Imperium, but isn't it justifiable for the country to levy taxes? If nobody pays their taxes, how can the government function, who will be responsible for the massive public infrastructure, where does the military fees come from, and what about the compulsory education and public medication? When the Covenant Alliance or other alien species attack us, where will the money be to defend our civilization?

"Despite all the bad things the authorities of the Imperium have done, they at least resisted the aggression of the Covenant Alliance in the past thousand years. Every cannonball shot at the Covenant Alliance is built by the taxes paid by thousands of people. If everybody uses the free coin and does not pay their taxes, will there be such cannonballs at all?

"Hehe. Absolute freedom will only lead to absolute chaos. I don't like the untraceable virtual currency at all."

"You are much smarter now!"

Li Yao remarked in amazement, "Even you cannot tolerate the fatal flaw of the free coin. How can such a great man of the time like Blackstar the Great, who is determined to be an eternal emperor, accept that the foundation of the country is out of his control?

"Therefore, Wuying Qi can't be supporting the free coin for free. He is only using it to undermine the four families.

"On the other hand, Jin Yuyan is the firmest endorser and preacher for the free coin. He was even nominated as the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors because of his contribution to the promotion of the free coin. The free coin is the source of his power and where his belief lies. I believe that he would rather die before he changes his persistence on the free coin.

"Judging from the development of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the free coin in the past hundred years, it can be easily seen that Jin Yuyan is really a tough person. When I got in touch with him before, he struck me as a plain, friendly businessman. I didn't know that he was such a sophisticated expert. Even Queen Li Linghai is some sort of puppet to him.

"However, Jin Yuyan couldn't have foreseen that there would be such an experienced player as Blackstar the Great in the dangerous game. Li Linghai is his puppet on the face, but in fact, she was Wuying Qi's puppet. However, Wuying Qi does not want to end on bad terms with Jin Yuyan yet because he hasn't finished the final preparations.

"Jin Yuyan is as determined as Blackstar the Great is. Their beliefs are highly incompatible, except that they have a mutual enemy that is the four families for now. That's why I'm confident in persuading Jin Yuyan!"

Chapter 2518: The General Picture of the Seven Seas Grand Market!

Li Jialing frowned and asked, "If Brother Yao knows that Jin Yuyan is a determined man, and the free coin is so dangerous, why are you still relieved to cooperate with those cunning merchants?"

"Like I said, it's the lesser of two evils!"

Li Yao opened his hands and said, "I wish that all the Immortal Cultivators at the center of the cosmos somehow had brain damage, turning them into kindhearted, selfless, and altruistic Cultivators like me so that we could build a beautiful tomorrow together, but how is it possible? We can only strike the unpardonable Immortal Cultivators with the slightly better Immortal Cultivators first before we figure out a way to transform the slightly better Immortal Cultivators into Cultivators.

"A real Cultivator is a practical realist, not an idealist who just talks about dreams. Even if we are hoping to change the entire universe, we have to change one planet at a time.

"Besides, I don't think that the free coin is truly untraceable and cannot be monitored. It will still leave abundant information that is very clear during the trades, except that the trades are mostly conducted on the dynamic network, and the parties in the trades are all anonymous.

"But the dynamic network is not really uncapturable. Even though the veil of the free coin cannot be torn apart for now, I believe that the problem will be solved sooner or later with technological progress."

Furrowing his eyebrows, Li Jialing was silent for a long time before he suddenly said, "The universe is too large, and there are too many problems. I suddenly understand Blackstar the Great's anxiety and doggedness now. If I were Wuying Qi, I would've gone crazy in less than three days when faced with all the troubles, including the Covenant Alliance, the reformists, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the Cultivators, the warlords, the free coin, etc."

Li Yao said, "Are you serious? You're feeling compassionate toward Wuying Qi now?"

Li Jialing sniffed and said, "Wuying Qi occupies the shell of the queen and tries to take over my body. We're naturally sworn enemies, and I only wish I could kill him in person!

"However, even if Wuying Qi is killed, so what? Will the chaotic universe resume tranquility? Who can govern the Imperium in the place of Wuying Qi? Would it be a cunning merchant who worships 'absolute freedom' like Jin Yuyan or a Cultivator like Brother Yao? Brother Yao, are you capable of it?"

"Huh…"

Li Yao thought carefully for a moment and replied, "In terms of strength, Blackstar the Great is absolutely no match for me, but everybody has his own gifts. When it comes to the governance of the country, I'm afraid that I'm slightly worse than him, I probably can't replace him."

"That's right. What will the future of the Imperium be?"

The young man was deep in thought while looking at the mysterious stars outside of the window.

Right then, Little Bear shivered and slowed down.

They had passed the inspection, and the ship was pulled to a port at the edge of the Seven Seas Grand Market.

"Alright. As a young man, you shouldn't focus on such things!"

Li Yao patted the back of Li Jialing's head and said, "After all, if the sky falls, those taller than me will be holding it up for you. You are not asked to manage the Imperium. Why do you care about national affairs? It appears to me that you are more and more mature now. I don't even know how I should respond to you. That's not very lovely!"

"This is just who I am."

Li Jialing sniffed. "Li Lingfeng used to raise me as his heir years ago. During the few years, he implanted a plethora of national affairs into my head, except that I sealed them before and did not want to remember them.

"But no matter how I sealed them, they just kept bubbling up from my head, especially after I absorbed the strength of the Imperial Flame Pearl. Whenever I run into any problem, I just can't stop analyzing. What choice do I have?"

While the two of them were talking, Little Bear had been anchored at a port at the edge of the Seven Seas Grand Market.

Ripples of spiritual energy surged into the cabin, connecting Little Bear to the dynamic Spiritual Nexus of the Seven Seas Grand Market. More and more 3D light beams were popping up around them, as if the whole starship were melting and exposing themselves right before the colorful, splendid market in the vacuum.

Li Yao and Li Jialing were both shocked at the enormity and glamor of the Seven Seas Grand Market.

The core of the Seven Seas Grand Market was a planet whose inside had been hollowed. Because the entire Seven Seas Space Zone was full of space cracks, making it one of the most suitable places for space jumps at the edge of the Imperium, the place had been built into a massive transfer base of a military base in the Star Ocean Imperium. The planet was itself a super large arsenal.

After ten thousand years of development, the planet was already beyond recognition. The inside of the planet had been hollowed and built into what appeared to be a beehive or an ant nest. On the surface of the planet, countless circular rails had been built, which grew outward incessantly, extending branches of vacuum tubes. Next to the vacuum tubes were the living cabins in the shape of spheres, and around the living cabins were new vacuum tubes. Further outside, they were connected to the permanent living quarters modified based on the abandoned starships, which were then connected with the circular rails to even more vacuum tubes, abandoned starships, and sphere living cabins. Today, nobody could tell how many floors the Seven Seas Grand Market had, and how many zones and how much population there were in the place.

It was a haven for the space pirates, a resort for the wanted criminals, an arena for the unknown to earn their fame with their life, a paradise for the adventurers, and a graveyard for the cowards. This was a black market of the black markets, a slaughterhouse of the slaughterhouses, and a heaven of the heavens. This was the freest place in the entire universe!

At this moment, the latest catalog of goods and services in the Seven Seas Grand Market, as well as the demo system in the Grand Illusionary Land, had been matched with the mainframe crystal processor of Little Bear, allowing them to be displayed directly.

Through the trade system with the Grand Illusionary Land, the merchants could browse through the commodities vividly. After the trades were completed through the free coin on the Spiritual Nexus, the fully-automatic barges would load the goods on the starships. The merchants did not even need to leave his starship in the entire process. It couldn't have been more convenient.

Of course, regarding the unique Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures whose authenticity must be checked in real life, or when the merchandise was human resources such as mercenaries, assassins, or regular laborers, the merchants still needed to enter the Seven Seas Grand Market to complete the trades.

Li Yao was preparing to secretly get in touch with Jin Yuyan. He naturally needed to get off.

But before leaving Little Bear, it wouldn't hurt to look around in the virtual market and see what was out there.

The two of them were both experts with extraordinary computational abilities. As soon as they made the decision, they were immediately connected to the demo system with the Grand Illusionary Land, and tremendous names of goods spurted out from the 3D light beam like a waterfall.

From the most fundamental water cleaning pills, air circulation chips, and synthetic food in cans, to the refined minerals for industrial purposes and the strengthening drugs for the experts, to the rather sensitive space shuttles, crystal suits, battle puppets, and even the modified, half-armed carriers, anything could be found on the catalog. Both of them felt rather dazzled.

Clicking on the latest half-armed carrier to read the introduction, Li Jialing discovered that it was modified based on a new model of carriers co-developed by the four families. But the questions remained. Where exactly did the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors get so many advanced carriers, and how did they get the most confidential technological manuals of those carriers in order to complete the modification?

Looking at the description of the good, which stated that it was an original product from a certain magical equipment base of the four families, Li Jialing couldn't help but feel absurd.

"The goods provided in the Seven Seas Grand Market are truly numerous."

Li Jialing found it hard to understand. "The coalition fleet of the four families have obviously blocked most of the sailing routes, trapping the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the Seven Seas Space Zone, their base. Yet so many goods are still supplied here without any restriction, and so many business teams have arrived here by passing through the control of the four families. This is utterly unreasonable!"

Li Yao told Li Jialing that it was because he did not know the whole of it. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors did not produce any goods. They even released the shares of many shipping groups, inviting the people who were interested to join the great cause together. They were just the provider of a platform where trades were free and anonymous.

The individuals trading and making a fortune on the platform established by the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were actually the countless corporations.

Who created those corporations, or who controlled such enterprises in secret? Of course, they were the Immortal Cultivators that included those from the four families!

An Immortal Cultivator from a secondary bloodline of the four families could perhaps get a minor job in the government or become an unimportant officer in the fleet. With their salaries and the dividends from their family every year, they would never be starved, but they would never be full either. They were about to live their entire life for nothing when a certain corporation affiliated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors offered them shares in secret, asking for nothing in return except for their "friendship". Who would ever turn that down?

Such things did not happen yesterday but had started decades ago before the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors completely revealed its ambitions. At that time, it was completely legal.

After decades of operations, their shares equaled a rather impressive amount of money if exchanged into the free coin.

From the four families to other noble houses, and from the minor officials in the central worlds to the warlords in the peripheral worlds, who did not have the shares of the corporations under the name of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, who did not have free coins dozens of times higher than their legitimate income in their secret account, and who did not receive the bribery of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors every year? Their "friendship" was certainly worth it!

Right now, those people were asked to besiege the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, cutting out their own way of making money and transforming the free coins in their pockets into meaningless numbers? That was certainly not going to happen!

Chapter 2519: When a Country Is About to Die!

Li Jialing thought for a moment and shook his head solemnly. "That's not entirely the case. Take that Li Wuji for example. He seemed rather determined to annihilate the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the beginning, and his conquest on the Blue Sky Market was rather ruthless."

"Li Wuji is a hereditary marquess of the Imperium. The privileges that the country offers to him every year are already astronomical. The money that his small family takes from the court every year is enough for the expenses of a whole sect. That is actually just a minor slice. His main source of income is the mines, farmlands, and monopolization groups that are under his name for hundreds of years."

Li Yao counted his fingers and calculated it for Li Jialing. "He owns hundreds of thousands of acres of most excellent farmlands. The rice he grows is the natural, unpolluted strengthening food. He also has huge ranches where millions of animals are kept. How huge would the profit be? He can't expect to spend all of his money no matter how he tries.

"Therefore, Li Wuji is naturally too proud to accept the bribery of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. He does not need to conspire with the rebels. He is determined to clear all of the rebels to maintain his place as a marquess of the Imperium because it is where his greatest interests lie.

"Aya, I almost forgot. You're from the Li family too. You must've seen a lot of such things. You don't need me to tell you the ratio of the 'lucky dogs' such as Li Wuji in the Li family, which is already one of the most affluent families in the Imperium. What is the life for most of the people in the Li family?"

Li Jialing recalled his life in the past and couldn't help but nod his head. "That's quite right. Of a hundred thousand people in the Li family, not even half has the position and power of Li Wuji. Most of the members of the Li family are no different from the Immortal Cultivators of other surnames. They are exploited even more because the descendants of the main bloodline are highly wary of those of the secondary bloodlines and have been doing everything possible to block the rise of the secondary bloodlines, fearing that their privileges would be stolen once the secondary bloodlines are up. Why did I keep a low profile for years? On one hand, it was because I hated Li Lingfeng's ruthlessness, but on the other hand, it was also because I'd seen too many distinguished talents in training murdered uncannily."

"That's right."

Li Yao said, "The higher class like Li Wuji, having claimed all the benefits, naturally regard the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors as a sworn enemy. But the Immortal Cultivators at the bottom class enjoy nothing and are monitored all the time. When they do not want to succumb to their fate but want to advance, they will need extra resources. The only way to earn the money for the extra resources is the shares offered by the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors or the profits from the ventures based on the anonymous platform of the organization. All the shares and profits will be exchanged into the free coin eventually.

"Even the underclass of the four families have been riddled with holes by the free coin. It is needless to mention the local warlords and leaders who have been on the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' side in the first place and who have been closing deals with the free coin for a hundred years. They have wagered their life savings on the free coin and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors since a long time ago. Isn't asking those people to settle the rebels any different from asking thieves to catch thieves?

"Of course, the four families still boast an extremely high combat ability. The power of their elite fleets is not to be ignored by anyone. Neither the bottom-level Immortal Cultivators nor the local warlords will challenge them openly. They're all declaring their loyalty to the Kurfürsten openly.

"But when the time for real action comes, they will simply procrastinate or pretend that they did not receive the order. Nobody is an idiot. It is often the case that the routes of marching and the intelligence of the configuration have been sent to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors before the fleet leaves the mother port. Some people are even shipping assets to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors under the name of 'settle the rebels', making a huge fortune through it. Tell me about it, how is the war going to be fought?

"So, is it really a surprise that the market here is so prosperous although a war is coming?"

Li Jialing was stunned for a long time, before he said, "It does make sense. So to speak, the few great nobles on the top who are determined to fight can't do anything about those under their command?"

"They don't have any solution in the first place."

Li Yao said, "Let's say that the captain of a warship is a big noble who has many properties in his household. He despises the minor bribery of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and he is an unwavering hawk determined to get rid of the rebels.

"However, below him, the advisors, chief mate, fire control officer, manager of the engine compartment, head of operations… Everybody holds shares of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and they have their own channels to trade intelligence for the free coin. In every major festive, they receive gifts from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Chances are that they even have a few mistresses and illegitimate children in certain secret markets under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Tell me about it. Does such a warship have any combat ability at all no matter how magnificent its performance and firepower are? However awesome a captain is, he cannot expect to control a starship on his own, can he?

"The case for the warship is exactly the case for the so-called 'coalition fleet of the four families'. Otherwise, how can they be in a dilemma with the garrison of a bunch of merchants in the peripheral worlds when they have the toughest fleet at the center of the cosmos?"

Li Jialing was silent and looked rather gloomy.

While he was not fond of the four families, he felt rather bad when he thought how corrupt the previously glorious and unstoppable army of the Imperium had become.

Browsing through the catalog of goods of the Seven Seas Grand Market in silence, especially the most sensitive military assets, he felt more and more upset as he read on. He simply clicked on another item and turned a few pages. His eyes widened, and he couldn't help but shout, "Brother Yao, look, what's the meaning of this?"

Li Yao craned his head and took a look. "Oh, that's the service of back shooting. What's the problem?"

Li Jialing asked, "What is 'back shooting'?"

Li Yao said, "It's a deal between someone in the coalition fleet of the four families and someone in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for the guys of the four families to kill their superiors. Normally, they will settle at a precise time and location. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will be up in the front to attract the firepower, while those guys shoot their superiors in the back. Catching their superiors unprepared, the odds are rather high for them to take down their superiors."

Li Jialing asked, "Why would they do that?"

Li Yao opened his hands and said, "There are plenty of reasons. For example, like what I said just now, most of the low-ranking officers on a warship are associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, but if a pigheaded, inconsiderate captain stops everybody from making money in such a way, wouldn't it be perfectly normal for them to join their hands and kill their superior together?

"Or maybe, they are just coveting their superior's place. The deputy captain wants to be the captain, and the captain wants to be the commander of the fleet. They do not usually have the chance, but in an ever-changing battlefield, if they conspire with the 'enemy' in advance, it would be more than easy for them to kill their superior!"

Li Jialing still found it hard to believe. Pointing at the light beam with his shaking finger, he asked, "But why is such a secret matter so blatantly listed here? Are they not afraid that other people might learn it?"

"What can they do even if they learn it? Just scroll down. Do you see? There are thousands of inquiries about the back shooting service."

Li Yao said, "Let me tell you a public secret. Almost every deputy captain on the warships of the Immortal Cultivators wants to kill the captain and replace him, even if the captain is their biological brother. Sometimes it doesn't help even if the captain is their father!"

Li Jialing mumbled, "How can this happen?"

"This is exactly what I said before. When a world of the Immortal Cultivators develops to the peak, it will be everyone's war against everyone. It can't be helped. If you accept the advantages of the true path of immortality, you will have to accept the disadvantages too."

Li Yao said casually, "Ever since the moment the Immortal Cultivators agreed on the principle of survival of the fittest, the situation today has been inevitable."

Li Jialing thought for a moment and found it impossible to refute Li Yao. He simply changed a menu and read the war bonds and long-term benefits on sale.

The young man's eyes widened again as he read on. "How is it possible? A level-one Grand Pure Mine, with an annual yield of five hundred thousand tons, is only charged for fifty thousand free coins? This… This is almost free. Brother Yao, is it really not a scam?"

Li Yao glanced at it. "Of course it is not. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is rather credible. But look at it more carefully. Where is this mine exactly?"

Li Jialing read it carefully. "It's on the No. 4 planet of the North Peace Space Zone in the North Stone Sector. Huh. The doesn't sound right, isn't the North Stone Sector in the Li family's territory? This mine must have an owner. It should belong to someone in the Li family!"

"Exactly. It is currently owned. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is always known for its honesty, at least on the surface. Look, there are detailed files about the current owner here. Oh, it does belong to an elder of the Li family."

Li Yao said with a smile, "However, if the reformists win in the end and completely control the Imperium, and the atrocious elder of the Li family is killed, to whom do you think the mine will belong?

"That is exactly what long-term benefit means. It cannot be fulfilled until the comprehensive victory of the reformists. That's why it is being sold at such a low price.

"Speaking of which, it's just fifty thousand free coins, and you will have a chance to be the owner of a major mine. You can't lose anything in the purchase. It's actually worth gambling.

"Oh, it's stated here that the descendants of the Li family are entitled to discounts. You seem qualified. Are you interested in it? This is truly a fair deal."

Chapter 2520: Jin Yuyan's Strategy!

Li Jialing thought for a long time and said, rather creeped out, "It's not a bargain but a shameless scheme! If I were a bottom-level Immortal Cultivator of the Li family and I got such a 'long-term benefit', wouldn't I be hoping that my family fails? Only if my family fails and the elder is dead can my 'long-term benefit' be fulfilled!

"If thousands of bottom-level Immortal Cultivators purchase such 'long-term interests' in secret, how exactly can we fight the war?"

"That's the point."

Li Yao said, "Purchasing such a 'long-term benefit' is the same as cutting the flesh of an elder of your own family. Also, the purchase requires your real name, which equals a declaration of loyalty. It is certainly risky. Should the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors fail and the secret agreements fall to the hands of the leaders of the four families, none of the bottom-level Immortal Cultivators would end well. So, I estimate that 99% of the bottom-level Immortal Cultivators do not dare to take the advantage.

"However, there are always gamblers who are willing to take their chances among the Immortal Cultivators. As long as 1% of the ambitious, ferocious Immortal Cultivators dare to take the shares that belong to the leaders, they will be tied to the tank of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists. Do I need to tell you who they will help and how they will perform in the war?

"What we see right now is just the tip of the iceberg. According to Zuo Tianying, manager of the Blue Sky Market, the really valuable benefits will not be sold in the public market at all. It would be offered for free to the middle class in the four families who are reasonably powerful but who have no hope of being further promoted. For the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists, those mines, farmlands, ranches, and factories are not theirs in the first place, so there isn't any cost. As long as the hopeless middle-class people are bold enough to take it, they will have to work for the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists and undermine the big shots of their family!"

After a brief silence, Li Jialing mumbled, "How many of the middle class in the four families have accepted such options?"

"More than you think."

Li Yao said, "This isn't the first day that those from the middle class of the four families deal with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. They became friends with the organization decades ago, and it was thanks to the gifts from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that they struggled to climb upward in their family. In other words, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors has abundant shreds of evidence about their bribery and conspiracies.

"Under normal circumstances, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will certainly not kill the goose that lays golden eggs. It's easy to threaten someone with those pieces of evidence, but who will cooperate with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors after that?

"However, if the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is truly about to die, they won't care about so many things. Some people have to die with them!

"Considering the brutal internal competition in the four families, once somebody is proved to be associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, will there be any chance of survival for them?

"Since their chances of survival are slim, why would they refuse the prosperous options when they have an opportunity to?

"Just like that, from the bottom class to the middle class, too many Immortal Cultivators of the four families have been dragged into the swamp. By the time they realize what's going on, there is already no turning back!"

Li Jialing sighed. "Do the leaders of the four families know that their subordinates are so corrupt?"

"What can they do even if they know? Can they just shoot all the subordinates who do the actual work? With a war coming near, unity is more needed than ever. The investigation on a single case will make everybody worry about themselves, and the morale will be severely affected. How can the war be fought anymore?"

Li Yao sneered, "You have crystal suits, but I have money. You have starships, but I have options. You have Colossi, but I can beat the whole maintenance team of your Colossi with sugar-coated cannonballs. That is exactly the strategy of Jin Yuyan, the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!"

Li Jialing thought for a long time and was awed by Jin Yuyan's toughness. "I didn't know until today that a non-battle-type expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage could be so dreadful too!"

"The Immortal Cultivators attach so much importance to the principle of survival of the fittest that pure strength is prioritized to everything else. The battle-type Immortal Cultivators are often cocky and despise the non-battle-type Immortal Cultivators."

Li Yao said, "Little do they know that the civilization of mankind has long entered the age of teamwork, cooperation, and division of labor. There are too many forces that are more powerful than pure physical strength, except that they can barely emerge under the system of the true path of immortality. But once such forces surface, the simple warriors who know nothing except their fists and blades can hardly do anything to deal with them.

"Let's go. The inspection is all done. We can go to the market now."

Li Yao patted Li Jialing's shoulder, hinting that it was time for business.

The two of them went to Huo Dongling to bid her farewell. Naturally, they did not forget to ask about the trades of goods on Little Bear. The raw minerals brought by Little Bear were not very valuable and shouldn't have sold much, but since they were bold enough to cross the blockages to reach the Seven Seas Grand Market in such a time of war, they had set a good example for other people. Therefore, the staff of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors said that even though the minerals were not valuable enough for them to purchase the assets they needed, a batch of life necessities could be sold to them on credit so that they could resolve the crisis on the South Spirit Planet for now.

It was a rather nice move. The people from the South Spirit Planet couldn't have been more grateful to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists. After thanking the merchants, they promised that they would spread the news to other colonized planets around the South Spirit Planet, letting everyone know how "benevolent" the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was and that it was the four Kurfürst families who did not give them a chance of living.

Knowing that Little Bear would return in full loads, Li Yao and Li Jialing were both reassured.

Saying goodbye to the girl, they left the port and officially entered the Seven Seas Grand Market.

The Seven Seas Grand Market was divided into the internal district and the external district. Since "absolute freedom" was honored, the examination wasn't too thorough. Nobody cared about who they were or why they were here. The guards of the Business Alliance merely checked their weapons roughly to make sure that there was no massively destructive magical equipment. The guards even turned a blind eye to their fake names and their obvious masks. They were given two cards that had dynamic passwords for trades and let through.

Li Yao and Li Jialing found themselves in the middle of a bunch of mercenaries, merchants, assassins, and loose prisoners. Almost everybody was wearing a brilliant mask and a long robe with a hood that covered their entire body. They squeezed into the giant transparent capsules inside the vacuum tube.

From the bottom of the transparent tubes, certain thick fluids bubbled up and enshrouded all the passengers.

Hum!

As the passengers were entirely submerged in the thick fluids, everybody simply felt a minor quake on their eardrums, before the light around them were pulled into colorful lines.

They rushed toward the depths of the Seven Seas Grand Market like lightning. After a journey of only a dozen minutes, the port and the space were already left far behind, and they entered into the bright stomach of the beast of money that never slept.

When Li Yao and Li Jialing finally broke free from the thick fluids inside the transparent capsules, they found that their bodies were dry and comfortable, and even their hair was not drenched by the fluids.

Before they could be amazed by that, they had already been astounded by the glamorous views made of ten thousand colors in front of them.

It was the most prosperous market that the two of them had ever seen. Every inch of space as far as their eyes could reach was crammed with signs in fancy styles and huge noises. Below the signs were the adventurers from the entire Imperium in the most bizarre clothes and shapes. Even Li Jialing's style that looked like a cheetah was not eye-catching at all in the crowd, because there were people a hundred times more peculiar than him.

All in all, if the "Fist Dragon City", the establishment outside of the Star Glory Federation, were a hundred times larger and more chaotic, it would almost be what the Seven Seas Grand Market looked like.

Even though the army of the four families was pressing close and the life-and-death battle was about to begin, there was still not much tension in the Seven Seas Grand Market. The horse races and the balls were still on, and the city was still ablaze with lights.

The public broadcast, which echoed in the enclosed space now and then, was the only thing that reminded people that it was the time of war.

"Money is freedom, money is life, money is everything! The four Kurfürst families are trying to steal the money that we have accumulated through hard work for a hundred years and kill each and every one of us! Citizens at the edge of the Imperium, summon your courage and defend your money. Money is sacred and inviolable!

"Breaking news! Breaking news! The nine Sector Masters of the Southern Sky Sectors have recently signed the 'Cooperative Protection Agreement of the Southern Sky Sectors', replying to the four Kurfürst families' demand for them to march that they will not obey the illegitimate order! All the starships of the nine Southern Sky Sectors have exited the coalition fleet of the four families, and they will be neutral in the battle in the next phase!"

"Explosive news! Explosive news just now! General Yun Zhengxin, the highest commander of the fourth battle area of the expedition army just announced that, in light of the chaos in the Imperium, to be prepared for the next round of counterattack from the Covenant Alliance, all the fleets in the four battle area of the expedition army will receive the command of Lei Chenghu, Marquess Liaohai, together. He will also send the most excellent, patriotic soldiers to the capital to discuss the issues of a joint cabinet with the 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders' together with Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet. The astonishing news has triggered a chain reaction in other battle areas. In the future Imperium, the real, pure, righteous, and patriotic soldiers will certainly play a bigger role!"

Chapter 2521: I Have Special Skills

Li Jialing listened attentively for a while. Then he pulled Li Yao's sleeve and said in excitement, "Brother Yao, did you hear it? The situation seems to be in favor of the reformists. Many more forces in the expedition army and the peripheral worlds of the Imperium are inclined to the reformists now!"

"Such fake news is only for entertainment. Don't take it too seriously."

Li Yao scorned, "Merchants are best at bragging. When they have a tiny boat, they dare to pretend that it is a main-force warship. As long as you believe half of a hundred sentences they say to you, you may be sold by them without you knowing!"

Li Jialing was slightly stunned. "Is it… fake?"

"It's certainly not entirely true. This is just meant to stabilize the market and boost the morale."

Li Yao said, "I've heard a thing or two about the nine Southern Sky Sectors. They are just nine barren, developing worlds at the edge of the Imperium. In terms of military forces, resources, or experts, are they even close to the four families?

"Even if the nine Southern Sky Sectors intend to keep neutral, they will only cheer for other people without doing any real work at best. How would they dare to compete with the four Kurfürst families openly? Wouldn't it be like a slap in the face of the four Kurfürsten if they claim that they will not follow the 'illegitimate order'? Those Sector Masters are certainly not bold enough to do so.

"As for the second news, all the fleets in the fourth battle area of the expedition army are pledging loyalty to Lei Chenghu, Marquess Liaohai? Huh. In terms of prestige, connections, and combat ability of the fleet, Lei Chenghu is indeed the toughest general in the expedition army. He deserves to be the highest commander of the expedition army.

"Besides, the expedition army's stance is rather obscure. Most of the soldiers in the expedition army are from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium although they receive the command of the four families. But right now, the expedition army is the real neutral one here, neither helping the peripheral worlds nor helping the four families. It is obvious that they intend to claim all the advantages in the end.

"However, I do not believe that the fourth battle area of the expedition army has openly declared loyalty to Lei Chenghu. The most crucial thing here is resources. The reformists have only occupied the Empyreal Terminus Sector. It is impossible to supply the expedition army with only the resources of the capital. The peripheral worlds where the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is at are too far away from the newly-recovered territory. However abundant resources the Seven Seas Grand Market have, it is barely possible to ship them to the frontline and meet the astronomical consumption of the expedition army every day.

"The result is that the battle areas of the expedition army still count on the supplies from the four families to sustain themselves. Their necks have been tied up by the supplies and logistics. How can they break up with the four families easily?"

Tilting his head, Li Jialing thought for a while and said in disappointment, "It does seem the case. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is rather shameless to make up such lies!"

"It may be too far to call them lies. There is something behind them."

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, "I believe that the warlords of the nine Southern Sky Sectors and the leaders of the expedition army must've secretly reached out to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors or even reached agreements with them. That's why the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is bold enough to release such 'fake news' first. Whether or not it is true, the morale of the four families will be affected, and the four families' relationship with other forces will be weakened.

"Speaking of which, 'God of War' Lei Chenghu's movements are rather mysterious. He was pinned into the heartland of the four families like a nail in the beginning. By logic, the four families should pluck the nail in their heartland before they start any massive military operation. However, I did not hear that the Astounding Thunder Fleet encountered any major battle…

"Is Lei Chenghu aware of Blackstar the Great's secret or not? If he does, what's his attitude? What is the veteran who has been fighting wars for two hundred years thinking?"

Li Yao was deep in thought.

His impression of Lei Chenghu was not bad. While the guy was a cold, heartless, model Immortal Cultivator, he was also a respectable opponent who deserved to be fought with strength, unlike the selfish "false Immortal Cultivators" of the four families, who were scumbags like sticky slugs.

The former reminded Li Yao of "Iron Commander Zhou Hengdao", the soldier of iron and blood from the Star Glory Federation. He respected the man even in a life-and-death battle.

As for the latter, Li Yao would consider it a stain to his boots even if he stepped on them.

In Li Yao's plan, Lei Chenghu was a part that was as critical as Jin Yuyan. However, the stubborn Immortal Cultivator was certainly not as easily persuaded as Jin Yuyan might be. Chances were that he would even ardently worship and support Blackstar the Great. So, he needed to make thorough plans…

Li Jialing jabbed him with his elbow and asked, "Brother Yao, let's leave Lei Chenghu behind for now. How exactly are we going to approach Jin Yuyan?"

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and looked around. Pointing at a splendid and crowded building ahead, he said, "Do you see it? It's 'Commodity Market', the center of the Seven Seas Grand Market'."

Li Yao told Li Jialing that the Seven Seas Grand Market was divided into the internal district and the external district. The internal district was for the staff of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the VIPs. Jin Yuyan usually lived and worked in the internal district.

With Li Yao and Li Jialing's skills, it was not difficult to get a VIP card. However, going into the internal district required thorough safety checks. Not only was massive-destruction magical equipment forbidden, but even the Cosmos Rings would also be locked tightly. Their identities would also be investigated. It could be quite troublesome.

Li Yao estimated that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors must've been deeply infiltrated by Blackstar the Great's secret spies, and Wuying Qi's guys must be lurking in the critical posts around Jin Yuyan, who had received orders to kill Jin Yuyan in advance should anything go wrong.

Li Yao was reluctant to place Jin Yuyan in unnecessary danger or to let Wuying Qi know that he had come to the Seven Seas Grand Market. Therefore, he couldn't openly enter the internal district and request to meet Jin Yuyan.

Zuo Tianying, the manager of the Blue Sky Market, gave Li Yao a channel through which he could talk to Jin Yuyan in secret. However, after much deliberation, Li Yao still abandoned the means of communication in the end, because Zuo Tianying was merely a common manager, and it was rather suspicious how "secret" the channel under his control was.

One couldn't be too prudent about Blackstar the Great's methods. Chances were that he had monitored every communication channel around Jin Yuyan, and the first syllable that Li Yao spoke to Jin Yuyan would be heard by Wuying Qi who was thousands of lightyears away.

The safest approach was still to sneak to Jin Yuyan's side so that the two of them could have a face-to-face talk after blocking the soundwaves with rune arrays.

Through Zuo Tianying, Li Yao learned that, while Jin Yuyan usually worked in the internal district, he would come to the "Commodity Market" in the external district every other day in the last few months.

The so-called commodities referred to the trades whose total amount was above fifty million free coins. It was the foundation that supported the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Jin Yuyan would show up every other day to host part of the trades and to talk with his old friends. Even if he did nothing, it would still hold the confidence of the market as a sign that he would live and die with the Seven Seas Grand Market.

With a great war coming near, confidence was more precious than marrow crystals. Unless a very serious crisis took place, Jin Yuyan would certainly not break the norm.

After making clear of that, the rest would be very simple. All Li Yao needed to do was to sneak into the Commodity Market and approach Jin Yuyan without alarming anyone. Then, he would have a chance to communicate with the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors from close up!

"That explains a lot. I got it now!"

Li Jialing stood on tiptoe and vigorously searched around.

Li Yao found it odd. "Hey, what are you doing? Don't act like you are a thief, alright? The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is loose on the outside but tense on the inside. The security examination just now may be sloppy, but there are in fact many plain-clothes policemen on the road and a lot of surveillance cameras that can even see your nose hair. What are you doing exactly?"

"Found them!"

Li Jialing said excitedly, "Brother Yao, a hundred and fifty meters to the east, there is an entrance to a ventilation tube. Forty meters to the south of our back, there is a manhole leading to a gutter. In the mall on our left, the public toilet is also a valuable point of breakthrough. Just go without worries! I'll scout for you!"

Li Yao: "… You little idiot, what are you thinking, and who do you think your Brother Yao is? I am now an extraordinary expert above the Divinity Transformation Stage, only one step away from the Divinity Branching Stage, who is magnificent enough to behead a warship in one attack! Ventilation tube? I didn't crawl into a ventilation tube even when I encountered Blackstar the Great. Now that we have come to such a poor market, with only a couple of battle-type experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage at best, you are asking me to crawl into a ventilation tube? That is too humiliating!"

Li Jialing coughed and lowered his head in embarrassment. "I'm sorry, Brother Yao. I thought that it was your favorite!"

Li Yao sniffed heavily. "Even if I'm fond of crawling into ventilation tubes to eavesdrop and snoop and everything, you should know that your Brother Yao is now in a different level from before, and it is too lame to crawl into the ventilation tubes physically. As a matter of fact, I have grasped the special skills of separating the soul from the body and crawling the ventilation tubes with the soul. Where are the entrances that you found a moment ago? Point them out to me again."

"Huh?"

Li Jialing was dumbfounded. "Are you really going to crawl into the ventilation tubes?"

"Not me. Somebody is there in advance."

Li Yao sneered and said, "Trust me. All the weak parts around the 'Commodity Market' have been long infiltrated. There must be at least five groups of spies and assassins who are ambushing in the most secret places, preparing to kill Jin Yuyan.

"Even if I play the old trick and enter the 'Commodity Market' through a certain ventilation tube, it will only be the buttock of one of the assassins that I will see."

Chapter 2522: A Game of Assassins!

"Assassins?"

Li Jialing asked in surprise, "What assassins?"

"The assassins from the four families, of course!"

Li Yao sniffed and said, "After everything we talked about when we were still on board, you should know how deeply infiltrated the 'coalition fleet of the four families' is, right? Such a 'coalition fleet' where everybody has his own plans has absolutely no or even negative combat ability!

"It's impossible that the leaders of the four families do not know the mess. There are too many uncertain factors until the mess is sorted out. Chances are that certain people in the coalition fleet will intentionally make the fleet fail for their own interests. It is the reason why the main troops of the four families haven't shown up in the Seven Seas Space Zone.

"But the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors has their own weaknesses too. They are seriously short of military forces after all. They are sustaining themselves with strong 'confidence'. That's why they are bragging about the bulls*it such as the Southern Sky Sectors became independent and the expedition army was betraying. The thing about 'confidence' is that it's like a soap bubble. It's just as easy to blow it up as it is to make it big.

"As long as a serious accident takes place in the Seven Seas Grand Market, the morale of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will significantly be struck. As long as the free coin cannot be exchanged for a few days, people's confidence in the free coin, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and the reformists will be gone, and the splendid soap bubble will explode.

"If the coalition fleet of the four families presses the charge by then, they will truly be 'united' and 'unstoppable'.

"How can the people's confidence in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors be reduced to the minimum? Isn't it the most direct approach to assassinate the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?"
