A series of fireballs exploded outside of the spiritual shield that Li Yao had condensed in a haste, as if throwing Jin Yuyan and him into an oven to bake them. They were surrounded by walls of fire that were thousands of degrees high, which were also pressing toward the center. If Li Yao's spiritual energy hadn't been unbelievably abundant, the barrage just now would've been enough to melt every cell on the two of them.

When the fireball slightly dimmed, Li Yao released telepathic thoughts and searched again, but the mirror-like man had already disappeared.

Li Yao secretly cursed. His telepathic thoughts spread out like a tide, activating all the Cosmos Rings that he deserted in the trash cans and the corners of the street to detonate the rest of the smoke grenades, stun grenades, and shriek bombs!

Once again, the Seven Seas Grand Market was shaking as if an earthquake were happening. The whole market was enshrouded in the dense fog that was like ink. Even the searches and communications with telepathic thoughts were blocked. Both the defenders and the assassins from the four families lost their targets. They exclaimed in shock before they shot at the sources of the exclamations.

Li Yao drew a circle on the floor with the tip of his toe. Focusing his spiritual energy on his feet, he stomped on the ground and immediately left a hole on the floor.

The Seven Seas Grand Market was not established on a solid planet. The planet it had been established on was already riddled with holes because of mining and excavation a thousand years ago. Below their feet were actually layers of tubes and decks.

Dragging Jin Yuyan, Li Yao penetrated through seven layers of decks in a row. After every deck he went through, he would release even more smoke grenades from the Cosmos Ring that he carried, turning the seven decks and the ten square kilometers around into a shadowy forest.

"Hey, miss, or should I call you 'bro'?"

Behind him were the storm of bullets, and around him were the roars for killing. Unsatisfied with Jin Yuyan's speed, he simply carried Jin Yuyan on his shoulder and ran at the highest speed. "If you are really Jin Yuyan, you must know some secret tunnels or secret chambers, don't you? Where should we go?"

"… This way!"

The chimpanzee-like female bodyguard hesitated for a long time. Then, she finally made up her mind to guide the way for Li Yao.

Bam! Bam! Bam! Bam!

Li Yao's spiritual energy raged out like a superstorm, destroying all the crystal cameras within ten kilometers. He then scanned the locations of all the defenders and pursuers and set a route that could perfectly dodge everyone before he pushed forward quickly.

It was not until they spent six hours zigzagging in the dilapidated streets, maintenance tubes, and abandoned living areas that were as complicated as mazes that they finally got rid of everyone and arrived at the "safe house" that Jin Yuyan said.

It was an abandoned warehouse, whose thermostatic rune arrays had something wrong. It was so cold that a drop of water would be frozen into ice instantly. Also, there was no light at all, making the dark and gloomy place look like a gigantic iron coffin.

Despite Li Yao's enormous strength, he was unable to go on a killing spree but had to flee for six hours in total under the storm of bullets. He was rather tired despite his Cultivation above the Divinity Transformation Stage. Tossing the chimpanzee-like female bodyguard aside, he breathed hard in a corner and turned on the communication with the young lion. "Jialing, what's the situation out there?"

"It's quite a mess, but the situation is getting under control."

Li Jialing said, "It is said that the four Kurfürst families hired 'Red Lady', the former ace assassin and the leader of Bloody Skull, to be one of the most useful assistants around Jin Yuyan to kill Jin Yuyan.

"Right now, Red Lady and her assassins from Bloody Skull, as well as the other assassins sent by the four families, are on the run and putting up a desperate resistance, but I don't think they can get away."

"Alright…"

Li Yao scratched his chin. He was very clear that, whether or not the four families hired Red Lady, her previous shot had missed the target. Even Jin Yuyan's stand-in was not killed by her. "Then, who's in charge of the Seven Seas Grand Market now? Now that Jin Yuyan has been killed, somebody needs to stand out and lead everybody else, right?"

"There are two people."

Li Jialing said, "One of them is Qi Yuanbao, a senior manager of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. He has been in charge of the businesses concerning mercenaries and weapon deals in the black market, and he is also responsible for the security of the Seven Seas Grand Market. Now that the director has been shockingly assassinated, he certainly needs to stand out and clean the mess.

"The other is Jin Yuyan's wife—well, his widow 'Wuying Qinxin'. She is a manager of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors herself, with remarkable connections and influence. After Jin Yuyan's death, all his shares will go to his widow. So, nobody has any disagreement when she stands out and sustains the situation with Qi Yuanbao."

"Jin Yuyan's… widow?"

Li Yao was stunned for a long time. Looking at the devastated female guard, he somehow felt that the person might have complicated family issues. Pausing for a moment, he complimented, "Job well done. How did you collect so much intelligence after only a few hours?"

"It's not a big deal."

Li Jialing said casually but could not hide the pride in his voice. He explained, "Just now, two assassins of the four families passed by me and tried to kill me, but I captured them instead and interrogated them. That's how I got the critical intelligence."

"Alright. Just keep staying outside. Whenever anything happens, let me know. Also, take care of yourself. The assassins in the Divinity Transformation Stage such as the four gods of death may not have the highest levels, but they are certainly adept at the arts of killing. They are not to be messed around with easily. Should you encounter them, just run away as quickly as possible. A great man knows when to yield. You can always settle the scores later!"

Li Yao reminded Li Jialing again and again before he finally cut off the communication and looked at the gloomy female guard.

He stared at the two clusters on the person's chest that looked like basketballs and scanned for a long time, but he was still not sure.

He thought to scan the person's pants, but it did not seem appropriate.

He did not remember being struck by anything when he carried the person to rush away crazily a moment ago. So, was the weird "woman" Jin Yuyan or not?

Chapter 2530: The Imprisoned Soul!

"Well…"

Li Yao and the thick woman who looked like a chimpanzee looked at each other for a while, both feeling somewhat weird. Li Yao felt that his silver tongue was needed again to break the dilemma. "Are you Old Jin, Jin Yuyan, director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors? You haven't been a woman the whole time, right? No, no, no, I'm not biased against women at all. I believe great women can accomplish astonishing achievements too. However, you have a wife… Right, according to the latest news, your wife is working with Qi Yuanbao, the senior manager of your conglomerate who is responsible for black market weapons, the mercenary platform, and security. They have announced your death and appear to be abandoning you so that they can monopolize the Business Alliance."

The chimpanzee-like woman stared at Li Yao. Her eyes seemed to be covered in frosted glass, making it impossible for Li Yao to tell what was on her mind.

"If my guess is correct, Qi Yuanbao was the man who pretended to be the bald tycoon in the white leather coat and who became as unpredictable as the smoke in a mirror later, right?"

Li Yao opened his hands to show his sincerity. "If you are really Jin Yuyan, even if you are not a battle-type Immortal Cultivator, you must've felt the guy's hostility and determination of killing at the critical moment, didn't you? If I hadn't taken action in time, you would've been chopped into dozens of pieces! One other thing, your stand-in was not shot remotely. That fatal bullet was disrupted by me in time and brushed past your stand-in. Also, I assure you that no external force was imposed on his head when your stand-in exploded.

"In other words, some sort of force from within his head broke it apart. What exactly was that about? I believe you can tell me the answer."

The chimpanzee-like woman was still in a trance, like a hideous black statue dwelling in her own world. Even Li Yao was not sure whether or not she had heard him.

"Are you really a woman who pretended to be a man the whole time?"

Frowning, Li Yao scratched his chin and nodded his head cleverly. "I got it now. The real Jin Yuyan has always been a woman. However, the identity of a female was too inconvenient for you to make a living at the chaotic edge of the Imperium. That's why you pretended to be a man.

"Perhaps it was just an experience in the beginning, but as your position rose higher and higher, or for some other reason, it was impossible for you to reveal your true gender. In order to disguise yourself, you were even forced to find a fake wife—Wuying Qinxin.

"However, the seemingly unparalleled business dominator was a woman at all no matter how much money and power you had. Whatever tools and magical equipment you used, you could not satisfy your fake wife, Wuying Qinxin. We all know that women can be extremely, extremely, extremely terrible if they are not satisfied for a long time. Wuying Qinxin was unwilling to be a widow for the rest of her life. Naturally, she had other plans.

"As for Qi Yuanbao, what's his role in all this? Is he only your fake wife's lover, or is there a more complicated triangle relationship, say, he is also your lover? Otherwise, why did you trust him so much and entrusted the most important safety work to him?

"Could it be that you and your fake wife, Wuying Qinxin, fell in love with Qi Yuanbao at the same time, and the intense fire of jealousy between the two of you led to the great tragedy? Aya, what a drama…"

The frosted glass in the eyes of the chimpanzee-like woman finally broke.

If her eyes could spurt out blood, she would have shot out thousands of blood arrows and blew Li Yao apart.

"Are you done?"

The chimpanzee-like woman stared at Li Yao and gnashed her teeth hard. "What nonsense are you talking about? Lover, triangle relationship, or unsatisfied desires? Of course I am a man, an authentic man. Why do you think I am a woman pretending to be a man? What's falling in love with Qi Yuanbao at the same time about? A third-rate soap opera?"

"Ah, you're finally talking!"

Li Yao chuckled and rubbed his nose. Observing the man carefully again, he said, "I too feel that the real Jin Yuyan is unlikely to be a woman. Telling such a lie carries no benefits at all! However, judging from your… remarkable body curve, it's hard to believe that you are not a woman.

"Of course, the experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage can freely adjust the secretion of hormones inside their bodies. If you forcefully suppress your androgens and stimulate the estrogens, it is indeed possible for your body to go through drastic changes. But still, such changes are too extraordinary. I can't tell any trace of a man from the body at all!"

"You naturally can't see any trace of a man because this body is a 100% authentic woman."

The chimpanzee-like woman said sullenly, "But I, Jin Yuyan, have been a man since I was born. A real man. I've never pretended to be a woman or had any transsexual surgeries."

"Huh?"

Li Yao blinked his eyes quickly. "What do you mean? Jin Yuyan is a man. You are Jin Yuyan. Your body is a woman. Could it be—"

Li Yao thought of an unbelievable possibility, and his face immediately became weird.

"That's right. This is not my real body."

The chimpanzee-like woman—Jin Yuyan—smiled bitterly at first, before he heaved a long sigh and collapsed. He mumbled, "My soul is imprisoned inside the body of a woman, unable to get out!"

Thinking quickly, Li Yao was stunned for a long time before he replied, "So, it is possession and reincarnation? If you have possessed the body, I'd say it's a terrible choice. This is obviously the body of a pure warrior, but not the best warrior. Also, the form of the brain is more suitable for brief battles but not for steady thinking for a long time. It is utterly unfit for your soul. What happened? How were you… imprisoned or deceived into such a body?"

"It's not possession and reincarnation but a secret art named 'soul transfer'. However, it does have a lot of similarities with possession and reincarnation, except that—"

Staring at Li Yao, Jin Yuyan was like a wolf that had fallen into a trap with legs stuck inside. Mixed with brutality, desperation, paranoia, anger, and hatred, he breathed quickly and demanded, "Who are you? Why did you save me? Why should I trust you?"

Li Yao did not intend to hide who he was in front of Jin Yuyan in the first place. Immediately, he rubbed his face with both hands, taking off all the potions and artificial skin, before he slightly readjusted the facial muscles. Then, he smiled at Jin Yuyan and said, "Old Jin, it's me."

"It's you, Vulture Li Yao?"

Jin Yuyan took a deep breath again. His wariness was slightly eased, but he was even more confused than before.

On the reformists' march to the capital, Li Yao and Jin Yuyan were both important subordinates for Queen Li Linghai. Although the two of them were not intimate, they did see each other several times. So, Jin Yuyan recognized Li Yao immediately.

However, he was obviously aware of Li Yao's recent doings, too. Frowning, he mumbled, "I don't understand, according to the intelligence from the capital, you have betrayed the reformists and joined the four families. All the assassin groups under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were asked to hunt you.

"However, if you had joined the four families, wouldn't it have been the best option to simply watch my stand-in and the real me be killed? Why did you save me—twice?

"Was it because you wanted to persuade me to join the four families too?"

Jin Yuyan's eyes grew sharp again.

Even though his soul was not residing in his own body, a fury that was almost concrete was still emanating from his body. His eyes closed, he sighed and said, "When you take a gamble, you have to be braced for failure. I've lost the game that I've carefully planned for a hundred years after all, but it is impossible for me to surrender. I'm too tired, and I don't want to switch to a different side and do this again for another hundred years. Just kill me."

"Old Jin, I'm afraid there's a misunderstanding."

Li Yao still said with a smile, "I was never bought over by the four families. On the contrary, I have always been the firmest reformist since the beginning. I am a real Immortal Cultivator, a righteous Immortal Cultivator, and a patriotic Immortal Cultivator!

"It was exactly because I was so pure, righteous, and patriotic that the scoundrels and schemers who blended into the team of reformists found me intolerable and had to banish me!

"I've come to the Seven Seas Grand Market after such a long journey because I believe that you are also a pure, firm, righteous patriot and Immortal Cultivator, Old Jin. That's why I want to join hands with you and continue the cause to renew the Imperium, utterly transforming the decayed, corrupt Imperium of True Human Beings!"

"What?"

His words made Jin Yuyan who had lost all hope widened his eyes again. The shocking brilliance from his eyes was almost piercing through Li Yao's bones, internal organs, and the soul. He asked tentatively, "If you are a 'real reformist', then who are the 'scoundrels and schemers'? Li Linghai? Everybody knows that Li Linghai wants to be the regent of the Imperium or the president of the Council of Elders. It's also part of our deal. That's not critical."

"That's right, in the future plan of the reformists, whether Li Linghai wants to be the regent of the Imperium, or the president of the Council of Elders, or even… some sort of empress, it won't be a big deal. A title is just a title. The other forces of the reformists are confident to contain him and achieve a certain balance of power. Her prestige is not enough after all. After she dies, her power will naturally thaw."

Li Yao said casually, "But what if she is not Li Linghai but… Wuying Qi? What if the person who jumps out to claim the ultimate throne after the reformists and the four families both suffer heavy losses in the battle is none other than Blackstar the Great?"

Chapter 2531: Can We Be More Candid?

Tick-tock. Tick-tock. Tick-tock.

From the broken tubes, water drops were dripping nonstop, further adding to the cold and creepy atmosphere in the warehouse.

Li Yao spent ten minutes introducing the backstory and the general situation today to Jin Yuyan. In the end, he clenched his fists and looked at the guy with innocent eyes while speaking in the most sincere voice, "Trust me, Old Jin, everything I said just now is true. Right now, the two of us are the last hope of the reformists, the Imperium, and the true path of immortality! No matter how depressed and frustrated you are, do not give up hope. Summon your courage and fight for the true path of immortality together with me!"

It took Jin Yuyan another five minutes to completely digest what Li Yao said.

Closing his eyes, he considered for a long time. When he opened his eyes again, they were already filled with profundity and wisdom.

Jin Yuyan extended one finger. "Let me confirm everything with you. You are saying that Blackstar the Great, the ancestor of all Immortal Cultivators, has been reborn and attached to Queen Li Linghai's body and that he is manipulating everything behind the curtain for the purpose of reclaiming the ultimate throne from the four families?"

Li Yao nodded his head quickly. "That's right!"

Jin Yuyan extended another finger. "Then, because he is all by himself after just being reborn and he is scared that he would be exterminated by the four families should his identity be exposed, he is making use of the strength of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists, and even I have been a pawn for him?"

Li Yao continued nodding. "Correct. Your comprehension is truly remarkable!"

Jin Yuyan extended a third finger. "Blackstar the Great is just taking advantage of me, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and the reformists. Although the four Kurfürst families are our mutual enemy, he is in fact a 'royalist' that is even more conservative and autocratic than the four families. If he seizes the fruit of the reformists, he will certainly strangle the reformists and destroy the free coin as well as the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?"

"That's exactly what I was saying!"

Li Yao was delighted. "Wuying Qi's beliefs are absolutely incompatible with yours or mine. He can't be a reformist at all but a royalist a hundred times more conservative than the four Kurfürsten, because he is the greatest living embodiment of the royal power! His shadow was clearly behind the riot in the Seven Seas Grand Market too. Is that not enough to suggest the problem?"

Jin Yuyan extended his fourth finger. "Therefore, I can only cooperate with you now in order to compete with Blackstar the Great as well as the four Kurfürst families and to carry out the idea of 'renew the Imperium' to the entire sea of stars based on my wealth, my expertise on the capital market, my connections at the periphery of the Imperium, and your unparalleled physical strength?"

"You can't be more correct!"

Li Yao was almost crying. He slapped his thigh and said, "That's absolutely the case. Right now, we are the saviors of the reformists. We are the real reformists and the future dominators of the Imperium. You have wealth, and I have invincible strength. We are naturally best allies! I have high hopes for you, Old Jin. Other people would've been stunned for a long time before they realized what was going on after hearing such a piece of appalling news. I didn't expect that you could sort it through and summarize the key points so soon. Judging from your calmness and your wisdom alone, your future is absolutely promising. After our cause succeeds, not just the financial minister of the Imperium, you will be more than enough to serve as the Prime Minister of the Imperium."

Jin Yuyan said, "Okay."

Li Yao asked, "What do you mean by 'okay'?"

Jin Yuyan said casually, "'Okay' means 'interesting'."

Li Yao said, "What do you mean by 'interesting'?"

Jin Yuyan said coldly, "'Interesting' means I don't believe you."

"With everything coming to this point, you still don't believe me?"

Li Yao's eyes were widened, with shock and frustration all over his face. He smashed his fists hard and said, "Old Jin, just think about it carefully. Even if I want to deceive you, I wouldn't tell such a terrible lie that 'Blackstar the Great has been revived', would I? I even have the videos where I fought Wuying Qi! That's real!"

"Yes, that part is real. I don't believe that you would make up such a ridiculous lie either."

Jin Yuyan said, "However, the most brilliant lies always consist of 90% facts and 10% false statements. Just because you did not lie about that does not mean everything you said was true."

Li Yao said: "… Fine, regarding my statement that 'you have wealth and I have invincible strength', I may have exaggerated it a bit. I truly don't have the ability to kill Blackstar the Great right now. You've seen right through it! What keen eyes you have!"

Jin Yuyan said, "The amount of physical strength is not the key either. I know very well what 'Vulture Li Yao' is capable of. Although you might not be 'invincible', your expertise should be more or less enough. What I cannot think through is something else. I would like you to enlighten me.

"If you can give me a perfect answer, there won't be a problem however you want me to cooperate with you. I am a dying man after all, and I can't be any greater loser whatever wild gambles I am to take again."

While speaking, Jin Yuyan became so intimidating that he truly looked like a gambler who had lost all his belongings and who was about to bring out his life and soul to make a comeback.

Li Yao declared resolutely, "Feel free to ask anything, Director Jin. I'll certainly tell you everything I know!"

"Alright. Since we are about to cooperate sincerely and carry on the banner of the reformists, we should not hide anything from each other. At the very least, we should be aware of each other's backgrounds."

Jin Yuyan said, "Everybody knows how I rose. I'll explain to you why I got stranded inside the body of a woman later too. However, can you tell me your origins, your school, and your lineage, Fellow Cultivator Li?

"You are in the Divinity Transformation Stage and not just a random guy in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Even an experienced Divinity Transformation Stage expert like Qi Yuanbao who was adept at assassination skills chose to escape after having one head-to-head clash with you, which implied that he was quite wary of you. How strong is your combat ability exactly?

"Such an experienced expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage with an extraordinary combat ability can't emerge out of thin air. There must be a powerful force supporting you on your back.

"In the past hundred years, in order to expand the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, I visited almost every planet on every Sector at the edge of the Imperium. I know a thing or two about the local magnates and the hermit families, but none of them has a member like you.

"In order to deal with the four Kurfürst families, I've been paying attention to the intelligence from the center of the cosmos too, particularly that regarding the experts among the nobles, to whom you don't belong to either.

"Then, it has been baffling me for a long time. How exactly did you emerge out of nowhere beyond everyone's expectation?"

Faced with Jin Yuyan's question, Li Yao smiled rather lightheartedly. "As I've already explained it to the fellow reformists earlier, I was a dark warrior that the queen raised in secret to help her carry out the most secretive, dangerous, and clandestine missions. Is it strange at all that people from the outside world never heard my name before?"

Jin Yuyan nodded his head. "That's right. In the past, you explained it in such a way. At that time, although I was not entirely convinced, I was not bothered by it too much, but now that I think of it, something is greatly wrong. If you were truly a dark warrior that the queen carefully trained, you must've followed her for more than fifty years before you advanced into the Divinity Transformation Stage, didn't you? Then, why on earth would you betray her and escape from the capital?"

"Because our beliefs are different!"

Li Yao replied without any hesitation, "Although the queen groomed me, I still grew into a real reformist thanks to the lessons on the ideas of the reformists! When I discovered that the queen was merely realizing her own ambitions with the renewal of the Imperium as a disguise, and the most terrible Blackstar the Great was residing inside her body, I certainly had to split apart from them and even stop them!

"Old Jin, we are both men with beliefs and the real Immortal Cultivators, unlike the fake Immortal Cultivators of the four families who are cowardly and only care about their own interests. If you are willing to be sacrificed for what you believe in, why can't I?"

"That's exactly what baffles me."

Jin Yuyan said, "According to what you said just now, you had followed Li Linghai for more than fifty years, and you were her most trusted subordinate that she trained in person. She invested astronomical resources in you, and you lived up to her expectations and became her toughest dark warrior, making great contributions for her.

"On the other hand, it has been decades, if not longer, since Blackstar the Great's remaining soul was attached to Li Linghai. It was Wuying Qi who raised Li Linghai's ambitions first. That was the reason why Li Linghai recruited subordinates, expanded her influence, and trained the dark warriors like you.

"Then, since the very beginning, the people who trained you shouldn't have been just Li Linghai but should've also included Wuying Qi. Even if it was inconvenient for Wuying Qi to show up in person, he should've inculcated the idea about the supremacy of the royal power into your head. How on earth did you become a reformist?

"Stop telling the bulls*it like you stood uncorrupted and you somehow came up with the ideas of the reformists on your own. You should've been trained by Wuying Qi and Li Linghai when you were at your weakest. Was the almighty Blackstar the Great not able to create a most loyal dog after decades of work?

"If such a dog had random thoughts easily and even got out of control beyond the master's control, I find it hard to believe that the 'Blackstar the Great' was authentic.

"This is exactly my greatest confusion. Please give me a straight answer, Fellow Cultivator Li. Who are you exactly? Where do you come from? Where did you get such enormous capabilities? What is it that you are really after?"

Chapter 2532: I Have to Confess Everything Now

After Jin Yuyan finished his speech coldly, the atmosphere somehow became awkward.

Puffing his cheeks like a globefish that surfaced on the water, Li Yao thought for a long time and finally surrendered. "It's… It's indeed hard to explain. Or rather, I can come up with ten thousand explanations, but every one of them will be an insult to your wisdom. So, is it possible that we can neglect the unimportant details for now and focus on our mutual enemy in good faith?"

"You tell me."

Jin Yuyan said, "I've just been betrayed by my wife and my most trusted subordinate, who reduced me into such ghastly conditions. How can I cooperate with you if you cannot explain your identity and your purpose?"

"Fair enough."

Li Yao sighed and took out a glittering jade chip from his pocket. Delivering it to Jin Yuyan, he said, "Seeing is believing. About my real identity, please take a look at this, Director Jin!"

"This is…"

Jin Yuyan narrowed his eyes, turning extremely solemn and weird. Taking over Li Yao's jade chip with both hands, he rubbed it softly in his palm. The more he touched it, the more surprised he became. Sweat the size of beans was falling from his forehead nonstop.

"Since Director Jin has managed the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for years, you must be knowledgeable enough to tell the background of this item."

Li Yao closed his eyes and sighed, before he said gloomily, "It's been a thousand years since this item appeared at the center of the cosmos."

Jin Yuyan glanced at Li Yao in disbelief and then observed the jade chip in his hands carefully.

He was indeed a knowledgeable businessman. Surprisingly, he knew how to activate the item. Placing his hands into a gesture that looked like a blooming lotus flower, he restricted his vitality field into a special unlocking form, which made the upper half of the jade chip blossom too.

In the next moment, the torrent of light that entailed infinite brilliance flowed out like a glittering spring, soaking the entire warehouse in the vast sea of stars. From among the infinite stars, the most splendid silver lines emerged and entwined into a complicated, splendid stamp.

Although it was just an illusionary stamp, it felt like a whole planet was pressing down. Jin Yuyan was dumbfounded under the blast.

"This is… the Cosmic Stamp! The Cosmic Stamp that was passed on from the Star Ocean Imperium and brought away by Firefly after the collapse of the Star Ocean Republic!"

Jin Yuyan's voice was shivering. The thousands of streams of information inside the stamp congregated into an overwhelming torrent that flooded into his brain, making a magnificent, grave voice echo inside his brain. "We hereby appoint Li Yao as the Special Representative of the Speaker of the Star Ocean Republic…"

The voice announced almost twenty mighty, awesome-sounding titles in a row. Basically, Li Yao was a representative of all the advanced officials in the Star Ocean Republic. He was also asked to be fully responsible for the diplomacy, trade, or even war between the Star Ocean Republic and the Imperium of True Human Beings.

All in all, endorsed by the Cosmic Stamp, Li Yao was a living embodiment of the Star Ocean Republic!

"You are a Cultivator? One of the remnants of the former regime who escaped from the center of the cosmos a thousand years ago?"

Never could Jin Yuyan have thought that Li Yao had such an identity, which was truly a shock for him. He examined the jade chip again and again and even released his own soul to investigate the remaining power on the signature. He then scanned the soul marks left on the official document dozens of times, but he still found it hard to believe.

"Old Jin, you don't have to be so cautious. A remnant of the former regime is not an honorable identity. Is there any need for me to tell such a lie?"

Li Yao smiled bitterly. "In fact, I am a very low-key man, and I do not want to intimidate other people with those things unless it is necessary. I proposed to neglect the details just now, but you were too petty and paranoid to believe me. That's why I had to confess my real identity. Just look at the jade chip with the signature of the Cosmic Stamp. Could such a document have been fabricated in advance? Also, I happened to land on the Martial Meritocrats Sector when I just came to the Imperium, and I blew up that 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' by the way. Yes, the mysterious Cultivator who went on a rampage in front of all the netizens of the Imperium was exactly me. I didn't want to bring up such trivia at all. Really. It almost sounds like I'm bragging about myself. But you simply didn't trust me. What choice did I have?"

"It was you!"

Jin Yuyan stood up, his voice shivering. "It was you who blew up 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' and destroyed 'slaughter stream' on your own!"

Li Yao sighed again. "Yes, that was me. Now, my trump cards have been fully exposed. Hmm, why do you look so awful, Director Jin? You can't be such a man of principles that you will kill every Cultivator you see, right?"

"Not exactly. I don't hate Cultivators any more than other Immortal Cultivators do."

Jin Yuyan narrowed his eyes. His face looked somewhat dark and green as he said, one word after another, "However, Wuying Lan borrowed more than three trillion dollars from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors during the construction of 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky'. It was one of the greatest loans that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors granted."

Li Yao: "…"

Jin Yuyan: "Also, I personally thought highly of Wuying Lan's 'slaughter stream' and believed that it was a great money-making program that could also promote our ideas. So, I invested more than three hundred million onto the program from my personal venture platform. That was all my own money and had nothing to do with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors at all."

Li Yao: "…"

Jin Yuyan: "Furthermore, some of the talented weapon developers of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, whom we had been training with tremendous resources, were sent to 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' to cooperate with Wuying Lan's team on the cutting-edge magical equipment. They were supposed to give the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors a hand in the future with their products. As it turned out, all those weapon developers were killed, resulting in the greatest loss of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in terms of weapon development. We might not be able to establish a new team in another twenty years."

Li Yao: "About that, Old Jin, what if I say that I was bragging to show off myself and the job in 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' was not done by me at all? Well, it doesn't sound very persuasive, does it?"

Jin Yuyan nodded his head slowly. "It does not."

Li Yao: "On the bright side, even if I hadn't destroyed 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky', and your investment and your team were still there, wouldn't your wife and her lover be taking all the advantage today? It's better to be given to a Cultivator than to your vicious wife and her lover. While I am a Cultivator, I have never cheated on you, don't I? Look, if you think about it that way, you would feel much better, wouldn't you?"

Jin Yuyan: "… I swear to god that if you don't shut your mouth, I will abandon my future, my career, and my beliefs and fight you, shameless scoundrel!"

"Don't be rash, Old Jin! Let's just talk nicely. It's just about money, alright?"

Li Yao hurried to surrender. "The Star Ocean Republic has nothing except money! Calm down, you have to calm down. Everything is negotiable. I'll double compensate for your losses, alright? Let's talk! Let's just calm down and talk!"

Money was the best sedative. Jin Yuyan immediately resumed icy tranquility from fury.

Staring at Li Yao for a long time, he finally said, "What's a Cultivator, and a special representative of the remnants of the Star Ocean Republic, doing in such a treacherous time in the Imperium?"

"We mean no harm at all but only want to come home."

Li Yao raised his hands, his voice mellow with infinite affection for the hometown. "Since we escaped from the center of the cosmos a thousand years ago, Firefly has drifted for too long in the vast, dark, and cold universe. Our starship is beyond maintenance, our resources are running out, and our people are tired of incessant escaping and exploration.

"There is no hope ahead of us at all. Even the firmest beliefs have been corroded in the disappointing day after day and year after year. Right now, we only want to come home and find a minor sanctuary in our previous home.

"After all, we have been fighting for the Star Ocean Republic for a thousand years. No matter how loyal our ancestors were to the country that should've died a long time, their offspring have done their best. It is not a disgrace to surrender after a thousand years of resistance, is it?

"However, we did not know the Imperium of True Human Beings' attitude toward the—well, refugees of the former regime. Were they determined to annihilate all of us, without any room of negotiation, or was negotiation an option under certain circumstances, and a deal could be made for us to return to the center of the cosmos?

"Therefore, as a special representative of the Star Ocean Republic, I snuck back into the Imperium by myself to investigate the situation of the Imperium and the stance of the authorities. Of course, it would be even better if I could reach out to the influential dignitaries first."

"That explains a lot. After struggling for a thousand years, you finally realized that you had nowhere to go and decided to go back and surrender?"

Jin Yuyan sneered, "It seems that the Cultivators' beliefs are just fancy disguises that are used to trick people too."

"The darkness and cruelty at the edge of the cosmos are far beyond your imagination."

Li Yao said quietly, "Too many things that I would rather not talk about happened in the thousand years after Firefly left the Imperium, which… significantly changed our beliefs and even allowed me to view problems from the perspective of the Immortal Cultivators."

"No wonder you reached out to Li Linghai. You must've thought that Li Linghai, as a reformist, was more likely to tolerate the Cultivators than the four Kurfürst families, and that if the reformists could control the politics of the Imperium, you would have a chance to come home or even play a role in the politics of the future Imperium, right?"

Flashes of lightning were accumulating inside Jin Yuyan's eyes. "But why did Li Linghai trust you and regard you as a trusted subordinate, even keeping it a secret from the other major leaders of the reformists?"

Chapter 2533: I'm Only Short of the Initial Funding!

"Li Linghai had never really trusted me, no more than she ever trusted you. It was just a deal. I made her an offer that she could not refuse."

Holding his arms, Li Yao licked his lips and said with an enticing voice, "Money, glittering coins, astronomical resources, immeasurable Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures… The government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic is short of many things, but money is certainly not among them!

"However Li Linghai hated the Cultivators, she did not hate the Cultivators' money. It required money either to overthrow the four families or to reestablish order in the Imperium. In the new order of the new Imperium, it would still take money for her to fight for the supreme power with the 'allies' like you. Was there any reason for her not to cooperate with me?"

"Fair enough."

Greatly enlightened, Jin Yuyan nodded his head and said, "If it were me, I would've cooperated with anyone if they had come to make deals with me with crystals that could fill a planet, no matter they were Cultivators, extraterrestrial devils, or other bizarre alien lives.

"If the crystals they brought for the deal were enough to fill a Sector, then f*ck it. I could become an avid Cultivator in the next second!

"There's still one problem though. Didn't you say that you were planning to surrender because Firefly was beyond maintenance and short of resources, and it was impossible for you to persist? Let me ask you, how did the desperate beggars like you get so much money to convince the queen of the Imperium?"

Li Yao sweated hard. "Well, about that—"

Jin Yuyan narrowed his eyes. "Huh?"

Li Yao swallowed. "Don't be hasty, and stop looking at me so suspiciously and giving me so much pressure. Just listen to my explanation. The whole thing is very complicated, really. It can only be explained slowly and gradually… Yes, it's true that Firefly was beyond maintenance and too destitute for us to live on anymore, but in the meantime, we were also very rich, if not the wealthiest in the universe. They—they are actually not contradictory! Aya, Director Jin, you are such a smart person with such prowess in business, and yet you can't understand such self-evident logic?"

Jin Yuyan shook his head. "I don't. Please continue your slow explanation."

"We… We were short of cash, the resources to repair the starship, and the food to feed all the people, but it didn't mean that we didn't have other things!"

Li Yao slapped his thigh and said, "Coordinates! We have coordinates!"

Jin Yuyan's eyes glittered. "What coordinates?"

"The coordinates of Sectors!"

Thinking quickly, Li Yao felt that his brain cells were exploding into a raging river as he went on colloquially, "You should know that mankind occupied three thousand Sectors in total in the prime days of the ancient civilization, but after the civil war of ancient Cultivators, the rise of demons, and the establishment and collapse of the Star Ocean Republic, the war grew more and more intense, and the magical equipment was more and more destructive. Countless Sectors were destroyed in wars. Even their coordinates had been soaked in the river of time.

"Today, the Imperium of True Human Beings and the Alliance of the Holy Covenant combined have only controlled slightly more than a thousand Sectors close to the center of the cosmos. The other Sectors have all become dead graves, scattered in the unknown places at the edge of the universe.

"Although they are dead, those worlds once boasted an atmosphere and a highly-developed civilization after all, if not perfect industrial infrastructure or relics that contain secret treasures. As long as they are found, it will be possible to re-establish an atmosphere and an ecological circle, restoring the former prosperity of those Sectors. At the very least, it is much more convenient and cost-efficient than to find a resource planet that is in fact not suitable for the survival of human beings and establish settlements on it despite the unfavorable conditions, isn't it?

"Since the age of the Star Ocean Imperium, people living at the center of the cosmos have been expanding, trying to recover the glory of the three thousand Sectors in the past. The Star Ocean Republic was certainly not an exception during our prime days. We once sent countless exploration fleets, which gathered abundant astral data and new sailing routes. That information was later taken away by Firefly. As a result, the expedition of the Imperium of True Human Beings had to start from scratch. It took you a thousand years and countless resources, but you've only managed to stretch the border of the Imperium out by a tiny bit.

"In the recent hundred years or so, because the cost of the external expedition was too high, and the efficiency when 'star children' were sent to explore planet by planet was too low, the Imperium had in fact dropped external expeditions.

"But… But that's only because you didn't have the accurate coordinates. It was certainly difficult to locate one habitable planet from ten billion planets. However, as long as you have the relatively accurate coordinates, and as soon as the vitality in a Sector is recovered, just think about it, how much profit and how many business opportunities will be in it? And how much money will be flooding in?"

Jin Yuyan stared at him again, his eyes as glittering as crystal coins, and his voice also sounded like clicking coins. "How many Sectors… have you located?"

"Not too many, and not too few. About a hundred."

Li Yao said, "The coordinates of those Sectors were stored in the database of the Institute of External Exploration of the Star Ocean Republic and brought away by us when we evacuated. Our original thought was to escape to the edge of the cosmos, settle on a few stable Sectors, reestablish our country in a thousand years, and then find a way to counterattack the center of the cosmos.

"It was a shame that when we found those Sectors according to the coordinates, we realized that they had been ruined in war a long time ago, their civilizations gone. They were all worlds full of rust and deserts like the Land of Sins.

"The resources we carried were too few for us to modify or reestablish such forsaken worlds. On the other hand, we were scared that the hunters of the Imperium might find us after we settled. In that case, we would've been screwed!

"In a panic, we looked for those Sectors one after another, updating their detailed information and marking them on the maps, but we eventually gave up settling in those places and continued fleeing. In the end, when we wanted to turn back after the resources were low, there was already no time.

"So, Director Jin, you realize where I'm getting at, right? We're like explorers who have found countless treasures but who are too hungry to open them. If we can't come up with a solution, we will be starved outside of the treasures that are brimming with milk and honey!"

Jin Yuyan finally understood it. "So, you are aware of the locations of a hundred abandoned Sectors, but you are short of the first resources, or the… initial funding, to develop them, right?"

"Exactly. Initial funding is an accurate description. This is why I am just a simple-headed warrior and you are a business genius!"

Li Yao slapped his thigh in excitement and said with even greater temptation, "You should know that those Sectors were very prosperous and developed once. Many of them were the famous natural paradises in the age of the ancient Cultivators. Too many unimaginably great seniors and experts used to train themselves there. Some other Sectors were the important production and research bases during the thirty thousand years of the Demon Beast Empire, and laboratories and weapon factories could be found anywhere. Even the relics of the primeval age could be easily spotted too! It was a shame that we were short of the initial funding in order to develop them on a large scale. There was nothing we could do about the relics that had been sealed for ten thousand years except for drooling.

"After much deliberation, if we did not intend to starve while holding the gold bowl, we could only return to the center of the cosmos and look for someone to cooperate with. That was why Li Linghai entered my eyesight.

"At first, I thought she was a leader of the reformists, and that was why I was honest with her. I didn't know that the great fiend Blackstar the Great was lurking behind her!

"Even if we offered all the Sectors that Firefly had found during the thousand years to Wuying Qi, he would not let go of us. With that in mind, my only option was to run. It was not until I escaped to the peripheral worlds that I finally realized that you, director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and one of the best businessmen of the Imperium of True Human Beings who began his career in space trades and development of asteroids, are a much more suitable partner than Wuying Qi and Li Linghai are!

"Come on, Old Jin, enough with your hesitation. Drop your bias. Both the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators work for money, right? Think about it, if you discover a new Sector, you will be able to establish a hundred trade routes. The profits of reestablishing a Sector include the sales of properties, the development of the mines and farmlands, the immigration fees, etc. Tsk, tsk, tsk. It's too much for me to calculate. Would you like to run an analysis yourself?"

Jin Yuyan was obviously breathing faster.

Even though he was trying to suppress his heartbeat, he was not controlling his own body after all, and the little details still betrayed him without him knowing it.

"Is it true?"

He asked a meaningless question.

"I know it is hard to convince other people when I make such a statement without any evidence."

Li Yao opened his hands and said calmly, "However, if you could think from a different perspective, why are you not convinced when Li Linghai and Wuying Qi have believed it?"

Slightly stunned, Jin Yuyan was deep in thought.

"Do you think that Li Linghai and Wuying Qi were so stupid that they were fooled by me easily, and they did not run any investigation on my statement?"

Li Yao said, "My cooperation with Li Linghai during the past half of the year had been quite pleasant until we fell out after I accidentally noticed the existence of Wuying Qi. Therefore, it was their problem, not mine. If I had lied to them, I would've been killed by them a long time ago!"

Jin Yuyan took a deep, deep breath.

"I still feel that you're lying, but at least, the ratio of your lies has dropped from 10% to 1%. My instincts tell me that the routes of space trades that you talked about are a real thing, and it is indeed possible for me to make a great fortune if I cooperate with you."

Jin Yuyan held his forehead and said, "It's truly a very weird feeling, but whether or not you are speaking the truth, in the situation today, I don't seem to have any option except to trust you without any reservation."

Chapter 2534: The Soul Transfer Technique!

Li Yao smiled brilliantly. "Exactly. You and I are both left with no choice except to open our minds and cooperate with each other."

"Then, you will have to help me win back the Seven Seas Grand Market and the control of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors first!"

Looking at his somewhat clumsy body, Jin Yuyan sighed and said, "It's absolutely impossible for me to compete with them with my current look."

"That's not a problem!"

Li Yao agreed quickly. "Like I said just now, your wealth plus my strength, we will be invincible!"

"Also, despite your lousy performance just now and your ridiculous lies, I would rather believe that it was your disguise. You institutionally pretended to be shameless and incapable in order to ease my wariness."

Jin Yuyan looked at Li Yao. "Didn't you?"

Li Yao: "… Of course, it was totally my disguise. You've seen through me after all!"

"You are clearly an unparalleled expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage, and yet you have completely hidden your gravitas and your dominance. You do not have the vibe of someone superior at all but vividly strike me as a shameless scoundrel. It is evident that your computational ability is very high!"

Jin Yuyan went on, "Since your computational ability is so high, you must've calculated that you and me are far from enough to challenge Blackstar the Great and the four Kurfürsten at the same time. So, may I boldly assume that you have powerful reinforcements out there and even a whole plan for victory?"

"Of course I have reinforcements and plans. Everything is within my calculation. However, the enemy cannot be destroyed only with calculation. I still need your help. I need the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to play a greater role in the renaissance of the Imperium!"

Li Yao said, "However, we need to figure out what has happened to you first. How were you tricked into this body by Qi Yuanbao and your wife? Also, what's the situation of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors today? Which managers have possibly been convinced by Qi Yuanbao and your wife to take Blackstar the Great's side, and which ones are too tied to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to surrender? I believe you must know the answers as the director of the organization, right?"

Jin Yuyan was stunned for a long time. Rubbing his rough face in disgust, he said in a hoarse voice, "Rising from a tiny group of merchants to the largest business group in the Imperium today, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors has certainly encountered many storms. We also expanded and protected our business routes with violence and took down those who jeopardized our business interests countless times.

"Killers and assassins played an important role in the process. Black Flag, Bloody Skull, Life Claimer, and Lamp of Death, the four most powerful groups of assassins in the Imperium, are all deeply associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. The super assassins who lead the four assassin groups are also our partners whom we have worked with for more than a hundred years. They are the shareholders of many agencies under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. There is a deep trust between us.

"Of the 'four gods of death', the one I trusted most was Qi Yuanbao, leader of 'Life Claimer', who is known as 'Hanged Man'."

"Wait—"

Li Yao asked in surprise, "Qi Yuanbao, the senior manager of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, is 'Hanged Man', one of the four gods of death? But I was told that 'Life Claimer' is an assassin group exclusively made of ghosts, and 'Hanged Man' is one of the strongest spectral Cultivators in the Imperium!

"Was the bald man in leather coal who tried to kill you with daggers outside of the Commodity Market not Qi Yuanbao?"

"Yes, he was exactly Qi Yuanbao, one of the strongest ghosts in the Imperium."

Jin Yuyan explained, "That is exactly why he is horrifying. The general ghosts, however tough, can only crawl into the artificial bodies and control the machines to move. However, Qi Yuanbao has practiced a weird art named 'Body Puppet Technique'. He soaks the newly-deceased bodies in a mixture of special drugs for polishing, and after eighty-one days, the bodies will not decay anymore but remain vivid with temperature and air, like living people.

"His remaining soul could hide in those bodies, allowing him to regard the real bodies as his 'artificial bodies'. In such a way, he can easily turn into any random person. He is unpredictable and beyond recognition.

"Except for me and my wife, few people know his secret. Everybody only pays attention to the rigid mechanic bodies when they are wary of his assassination. As a result, he often takes advantage of their weakness and kills the enemies in bodies of flesh and blood."

"Body Puppet Technique. So to speak, the bald man I fought a moment ago was not a living person at all but a dead body that had been specifically refined?"

Li Yao felt that his head was dizzy. There were indeed countless peculiar tricks in the vast space. He did not know that there was such a weird technique.

"Yes, that was only one of the 'body puppets' that Qi Yuanbao often used. I don't know how many similar body puppets he has secretly refined in secret."

Jin Yuyan sighed and said, "As one of the five senior managers, his position is not much lower than mine in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. It is impossible for me to monitor his every move."

Li Yao said in deep thought, "The so-called 'Body Puppet Technique' sounds like it involves the combination of the soul and the body. It's rather similar to the 'Soul Transfer Technique' that you mentioned a while earlier. So, it was also Qi Yuanbao who suggested you transfer your body into this… strong woman?"

"Exactly."

Jin Yuyan said frustratedly, "One and a half months ago, Qi Yuanbao came to me and claimed that there was unquestionable proof that the four Kurfürst families were planning an assassination against me, and their trump card was one of the closest people around me. They had bought over one of the 'four gods of death', who would stab me in the back at any moment!

"Qi Yuanbao was a senior manager responsible for the security in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. He was in charge of my safety too. Naturally, I could not ignore his intelligence.

"Also, I confirmed the intelligence through other sources many times. There wasn't any problem at all. All the pieces of evidence suggested that the four Kurfürst families were trying to assassinate me, and they had very likely bought over one of the 'four gods of death'.

"The problem was that the four gods of death were seniors who had worked with me for almost a hundred years. They had extensive connections within the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and they had raised a bunch of disciples that were adept at killing.

"I could not accuse any of them without valid proof, which would only dishearten the partners and incite dissatisfaction. Wouldn't I be asking to be killed if I pissed off a bunch of assassins for no good reason?

"However, it would always be a hidden problem if it was left alone. I was not a battle-type Immortal Cultivator. Being the target of one of the best assassins of the Imperium was certainly not a good feeling. I almost had a nervous breakdown before the assassin showed up.

"Seeing that I was upset and panicked, Qi Yuanbao suggested that I should use a stand-in to allure the assassin to show up."

Li Yao nodded his head. "It is quite normal for a big shot like you to prepare a couple of stand-ins."

"Yes. I had seven stand-ins waiting for my call at any moment. They had all received plastic surgeries and looked identical to me. Even their gaits, tones, and habitual movements had been trained. Sometimes, even I couldn't distinguish them from myself."

Jin Yuyan said, "However, such stand-ins could only fool the regular assassins. The super assassins like Black Flag King, Red Lady, Hanged Man, and Revenant From The Dead Sea had been working with me for a hundred years. How could they have been deceived?

"No false stand-ins could've possibly tricked them unless my own body was built into a very special stand-in!

"It was naturally very risky, but if the betrayed 'god of death' was not found out in time, I would be in equal danger. After weighing the pros and cons, I accepted Qi Yuanbao's proposal and conducted the 'Soul Transfer Technique' with a loyal female bodyguard. My soul entered her shell, and her soul entered mine, thereby producing the most perfect stand-in."

"Wait—"

Li Yao frowned and said, "Weren't you a bit too gullible? Did you not feel that this 'Soul Transfer Technique' reeked of the smell of schemes? It was a dangerous thing to let the soul leave the body. Should anything go wrong at such a moment, it was very possible that you could get killed. Did you really trust Qi Yuanbao so much?"

"Even if I didn't trust Qi Yuanbao, I at least trusted my wife Wuying Qinxin."

Jin Yuyan said gloomily, "The suggestion was made by Qi Yuanbao, but the 'Soul Transfer Technique' was performed under the supervision of my wife. My wife is of the same origin as Qi Yuanbao. She is also a most distinguished Meditation Healer who is adept at all the secret techniques in soul training. What was there to be suspicious about if she was monitoring every procedure in person?"

"What should I say?"

Li Yao glanced at Jin Yuyan and hesitated for a long time. He then finally spat it out. "Not that I'm blaming you, but aren't you a bit too… innocent in life although you are a victorious dominator in business? Your wife is of the same origin as Qi Yuanbao. So, they must be from the same school. When they suddenly proposed such a plan that was full of the air of plots, why did you not grow wary at all or consider the possibility that they were having an affair and conspiring to murder you? Did you really not notice any anomaly in your daily life, not realizing that they were often hanging around stealthily?"

"What's wrong with your head? Have the most excellent Cultivators on Firefly become so dirty and underbred after being corrupted by darkness for a thousand years?"

Jin Yuyan burst into fury. "Who says that they are from the same school or that they are 'having an affair'? Your imagination is truly remarkable! They did hang around a lot, sometimes even in my own house openly, but wasn't it perfectly normal? By 'of the same origin', I meant that Qi Yuanbao and my wife Wuying Qinxin are biological siblings. They are even twins who were born with a gap of only several seconds. Qi Yuanbao is my brother-in-law. Who else should I have trusted if not them?"

Chapter 2535: Unfortunate Royal Descendants

Li Yao was stunned for a long time before he finally replied in embarrassment, "So, Qi Yuanbao and your wife are biological siblings. Nobody could've seen that coming."

Neither Qi Yuanbao, a senior manager of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, nor Wuying Qinxin, Jin Yuyan's wife, was a Mr. Nobody. Li Yao had naturally collected their files before, but none of the files mentioned the slightest connection between the two of them.

Therefore, it was perfectly reasonable that Li Yao had certain inappropriate deductions.

"It's their most deeply hidden secret. Outsiders don't know anything about it at all."

Jin Yuyan sniffed and said, "My father-in-law was named 'Wuying Yuanshan'. A hundred and fifty years ago, he was the Sector Master of 'Spiritual Mountain Sector', a rather prosperous world at the edge of the Imperium. Also, he made a lot of friends and accumulated tremendous wealth, which vaguely turned him into the leader of the 'Wuying group'."

"The Wuying group?"

Li Yao immediately recalled that there was indeed a "Wuying group" in the business world of the Imperium of True Human Beings.

Five hundred years ago, since the royal power declined and the four families rose, the previous members of the royal family had shriveled. Part of the members lingered in the capital, turning into the ragged Imperial Guards that did not have any morale or training. Some other members of the royal family left the capital and found a way out in various parts of the universe with their family belongings.

The four families had been controlling those members of the royal family strictly in politics. They were only allowed to be the Sector Masters of the peripheral worlds of the Imperium but forbidden to touch the power at the center of the cosmos again.

However, even the slimmest camel was larger than a horse. The Wuying family had governed the center of the cosmos for five hundred years after all. Neither their wealth nor their power could've thawed in the blink of an eye. Besides, the four families still needed the emperor from the Wuying family to reign the Imperium. Naturally, they could not push the Wuying family too hard. Should it raise the intense resistance of the Wuying family, and the two parties got into a huge fight, it would be other people who would step in and take the advantage.

Therefore, the four families and the members of the royal family somehow reached an unspoken deal. The Wuying family was not to reveal the ambition for the supreme power, but it was not a problem for them to go to the edge of the Imperium and be extravagant merchants, which was even supported by the four families in secret.

Every man sought after either power or fortune. Now that the Wuying family had lost hope to rise to power, if they did not want to live the rest of their life like swine, they could only devote themselves to the pursuit of money.

They carried the wealth of the people that they exploited during the first five hundred years. The abundance of the capital that they brought from the center of the cosmos was naturally incomparable for the small families locally born and raised at the edge of the Imperium. Also, thanks to the secret support of the four families, they had connections, visions, strategies, and technologies that the bumpkins of the peripheral worlds had never seen or heard before. Very soon, the bumpkins were crushed in competitions, and they controlled many central industries at the edge of the Imperium, making a huge fortune through it.

In the hundreds of years that followed, the capitals with the royal family as the background, managed by those with the "Wuying" surname, were known as the Wuying group.

"My father-in-law was once a leader of the 'Wuying group'. He was an ambitious and visionary man. In his prime days, he even thought to focus all the capitals of the 'Wuying group' in order to accomplish an unprecedented achievement!"

Jin Yuyan sighed and said, "It was a pity that he was too determined and confident to forget the reason why the four families supported the 'Wuying group' in the beginning. Did those people really want them to make a fortune? Of course not. They were just hoping that a tiny bit of money would be enough to hold the royal family back and corrode their will.

"If the members of the royal family stayed at the edge of the Imperium, living an extravagant and obedient life, the four families would not bother to deal with them at all, but my father-in-law was planning to concentrate all the capitals for a 'great cause'. Hehe. He was asking to be killed!

"Then, it was the classic drama where revenge is a dish best served cold. My father-in-law 'Wuying Yuanshan' was killed in an assassination uncannily, and the whole family was immolated. Even the place of Sector Master was stolen by his subordinates. Naturally, his great dream was gone.

"However, although the assassins massacred my father-in-law's entire family, two youngest children somehow escaped. They were exactly my wife and my brother-in-law. Right, my brother-in-law did not exactly get away. He suffered a vicious punch from the enemy, whose power of corrosion and corruption haunted him for decades, eventually destroying his physical body and killing him. However, the decades of suffering was part of the reason why he could train himself into a 'super spectral Cultivator' in a weird form.

"All in all, after my wife and my brother-in-law escaped, they had a series of fortuitous incidents. In case the enemy continued hunting, they decided to hide their names and implement the revenge plan separately.

"My wife joined the business world with her advantage of inferring what was on people's mind as a Meditation Healer, turning into a rising star of the 'Wuying group' from another perspective, before she investigated the truth of the accident in the Spiritual Mountain Sector years ago. My brother-in-law, on the other hand, changed his name to Qi Yuanbao, nicknamed 'Hanged Man'. He became a notorious assassin and established his own force 'Life Claimer', preparing for their revenge.

"Later, my wife and I met in business…"

Li Yao nodded his head quickly. "Got it. Your marriage is some sort of political marriage!"

Jin Yuyan blushed and replied, "The marriage of people like us certainly can't be just about love. What caught her attention in me was that I had already become a rather influential person in the business world. She intended to avenge her family with my strength. So, she took the risks and confessed the truth to me.

"After much deliberation, I found the connections and reputation of my deceased father-in-law 'Wuying Yuanshan' in the Wuying group irrefutable, and I wanted to hold the strength in my hands. Therefore, we made a win-win choice.

"After that, my marriage with Wuying Qinxin was actually rather pleasant. Our cooperation with Qi Yuanbao was quite smooth too. Under the support of Wuying Qinxin and I in secret, Qi Yuanbao's 'Life Claimer' killed all the murderers who were directly involved in the massacre years ago. Then, naturally, the return of Wuying Yuanshan's daughter was announced within the Wuying group.

"In the meantime, with the help of the Wuying group and Qi Yuanbao's 'Life Claimer', I rose quickly within the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, defeating almost a hundred competitors, before I finally became the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the business leader at the edge of the Imperium!

"Of course, in order not to catch unnecessary attention or invoke the four families' thunderous attacks again, Qi Yuanbao never resumed his surname of 'Wuying', and nobody knew that he was Wuying Qinxin's biological brother. Otherwise, the power of the brother, the sister, and I was too dreadful. It would've been possible that we entered the blacklist of the four families a long time ago and got erased!

"You should understand it now, right? Wuying Qinxin and I are a couple who knew each other before we really grasped power. Later, we founded the most solid alliance of interests, helping each other to succeed. It must be noted that the real murderers of her family were exactly the four families. Who could've thought that she would betray me before the four families were destroyed?"

Li Yao thought quickly and inferred for a long time. In the end, he could only nod his head and said, "That's right. Under normal circumstances, she did have no reason to betray you at all until the four families were destroyed. If I were you, I certainly would've trusted my wife who had been with me for more than a hundred years.

"However, you couldn't have foreseen that Blackstar the Great was already revived, which was the biggest unexpected factor!

"Wuying Qinxin and Qi Yuanbao both belong to the royal family. They are the descendants of Wuying Qi. There are too many things that can be taken advantage of in their relationship. If Wuying Qi persuaded them, envisaging the rise of the royal family for them and tempting them with a title, I probably would've been convinced too."

"Who would not?"

Jin Yuyan's smile was even more bitter. "That's why I did not doubt it all when you said that Blackstar the Great had been revived. There was no reason why Wuying Qinxin or Qi Yuanbao would betray me other than that!"

"Speaking of which, Qi Yuanbao has never recovered his surname of 'Wuying' or revealed his relationship with Wuying Qinxin partly because of you, right?"

Li Yao looked at Jin Yuyan with a half-smile, "I think if Qi Yuanbao reveals his real identity and everybody learns that he is your brother-in-law, it will be impossible for you to be the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and your brother-in-law to be one of the senior managers at the same time. Other managers and shareholders will certainly be wary of your family."

Jin Yuyan coughed and said, "That's right. I would be lying if I say that I didn't try to control more shares of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and suppress the other three assassin groups through Qi Yuanbao. You know that the 'four gods of death' are disobedient people with remarkable skills, who also have so many unpredictable assassins at their disposal. I am not a battle-type Immortal Cultivator. If I don't have a few trump cards, how can I expect to check them?"

"Therefore, you regarded your brother-in-law as the greatest trump card."

Li Yao said, "But as it turned out, the trump card fell into the hands of Blackstar the Great in the end. You've suffered such a setback that you were almost killed. Even if you have luckily escaped, there is still no telling if you can recover your real identity and let other people admit that you are Jin Yuyan!"

Jin Yuyan was indeed the most good-tempered expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage that Li Yao had ever seen. It seemed that "peace makes fortune" had been melted into his blood, bones, and soul. He was utterly unaffected despite all of Li Yao's mockery but simply sighed and said, "It's already too late to regret right now. We have basically figured out the background of everything, but a lot of details about the chaos and the assassination a moment ago are still baffling me. We need to sort them out too."

Chapter 2536: On Their Own!

"That's exactly what I'm planning to do too. There are many suspicious points about the whole thing. As long as the questions are answered, it will be possible for us to reverse the outcome."

Li Yao activated his portable crystal processor and marked the experts that he sensed just now on the hologram of the Seven Seas Grand Market, recreating the scene of the assassination.

"When you rode here on the bulletproof limo just now, these were the suspicious people near the Commodity Market. They were all bodyguards and assassins whose capabilities were above average."

Pointing at the shining red spots on the hologram, Li Yao said, "Director Jin, please take a look at it. Which of them were bodyguards, and which ones were assassins from the outside?"

Narrowing his eyes, Jin Yuyan observed for a while and pointed quickly. "After learning that there might be a traitor among the four gods of death, Qi Yuanbao and I did not let other people know. While we created a 'real stand-in' through the 'Soul Transfer Technique', we also pretended that the defense was just like before, hoping that the traitor would reveal himself.

"As it turned out, since three days ago, a dozen batches of suspicious people had snuck into the Seven Seas Grand Market and got discovered by us. However, in order not to alarm the enemy, we simply indulged them while they snuck and infiltrated, but we watched them closely.

"Look, here, here and the gunner hiding here—they were all the assassins sent by the four families. Perhaps they had received rigorous assassination training from the four families, but when it came to stealth and assassination, who could've escaped the attention of Black Flag King, Red Lady, Hanged Man, and Revenant From The Dead Sea?

"In the end, we planned a 'Heavenly Dragnet Operation', hoping to fish up all the flies, fleas, and mosquitoes that had snuck into the Seven Seas Grand Market before the final battle with the fleets of the four families!"

Analyzing the location of the assassins of the four families quickly, Li Yao asked, "So, the rest of them were all your people? So, you had locked onto all the assassins. No wonder you were bold enough to show up in public. Where were the four gods of death? Please point them out."

"Here, here, and here."

Jin Yuyan pointed at the red spots and said, "It was part of the plan that I showed up in public in the Commodity Market every other day in order to tempt the assassins of the four families to attack.

"In fact, I wasn't afraid of the assassins of the four families very much. The key was the 'god of death' that had been bought over. It was like a thorn on my heart. If I could not pluck it out, it would've been impossible for me to go to sleep.

"Therefore, the deployment of the four gods of death was also meant for them to monitor each other. If one of them attacked me, other people would discover him immediately. Even my brother-in-law Qi Yuanbao was not an exception. In fact, he was the one who offered it. He spoke to me sincerely that he must be exposed to the eyes of the other three gods of death too, in order to clear his suspicion as much as possible."

"Of course."

Li Yao sniffed and said, "Even if Wuying Qi intended to kill you, it shouldn't have been now but after the battle of Seven Seas was won and the four families' coalition fleet was crushed. So, Qi Yuanbao was naturally free to express his loyalty.

"After I raised a riot in the Commodity Market, the super gunner who shot you must've been 'Red Lady', and she was here, right?"

"Exactly, that was her."

Jin Yuyan said somewhat frustratedly, "Since decades ago, after deciding that we had to compete with the four families until the end, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had realized that we did not stand any chance in head-on battles at all, and head-on battles were not necessarily required either. Therefore, we paid special attention to special operations, assassination, sabotage, and other concepts in unrestricted warfare. The elites of the four assassin groups were also trained to become special troops.

"Red Lady, leader of Bloody Skull, used to be one of the best gunners in the Imperium. She later became the chief tutor of gunmanship in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Leading a special squad of gunners, she was hiding among the skyscrapers and protecting my safety from the high stance. I did not expect that she would be the one who betrayed me and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!"

"So, this should be what happened—"

Li Yao said, "After I intentionally detonated the smoke grenades and stun grenades near the Commodity Market, the assassins of the four families were all exposed. The Red Lady could not afford to wait any longer but had to assassinate you while the opportunity was not bad. The moment she attacked, Black Flag King, Hanged Man, and Revenant From The Dead Sea sensed her. Among them, Black Flag King and Revenant From The Dead Sea immediately reacted and went after her after realizing that she was a traitor, and she fled in a hurry too. As a result, the three gods of death got away from our sight, leaving 'Hanged Man', the last god of death, on the spot next to you. Everybody knew that he was responsible to protect you from nearby, and it was normal that he did not chase after the traitor."

"Exactly."

Jin Yuyan nodded his head. "That's what happened."

"However, the bullet that Red Lady shot out was disrupted by me and shouldn't have blown up your head—neither yours nor your stand-in's."

Li Yao said, "Then, there would only be one explanation to the uncanny explosion of your stand-in's head. Her brain—well, or should I say your brain, had been planted with certain minor explosion devices earlier, which could be detonated remotely by other people."

"Yes."

Jin Yuyan said gloomily, "If what you said is true, Blackstar the Great must've hoped that I could die at the most appropriate moment without a single second of delay.

"The most convenient approach to achieve the purpose was to plant certain remotely-controlled crystal bombs in my head.

"However, I was an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage after all. Even though I was not battle-type, my control and senses over my body had reached the highest. Planting a tiny crystal bomb in my head, mixing venom that would release slowly in my food over a long period, or other similar approaches would've been easily detected by me. Then, they would be discovered.

"However, if what controlled my body was not my own soul but the soul of a relatively weak warrior, and with the rejection reaction between the soul and the new body, it would be much easier to play tricks under such circumstances!

"To ensure the safety of the 'Soul Transfer Technique' and the status of my physical body, my personal bodyguard had to receive modifications from Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin every day. It was more than easy to play tricks during the procedures.

"With a great war coming near, there were millions of affairs I had to attend to every day. Naturally, it was impossible for me to keep an eye on the brother and the sister every second, which turns out to be a horrible mistake!"

Jin Yuyan smashed his fists. Finally, viciousness and sullenness leaked out of his peaceful face.

Li Yao listened quietly and thought hard. A moment later, he said cautiously, "Even if Director Jin's speculation is not the whole truth, it can't be far away from it. There's just one thing that baffles me. Even if the enemy did plant microbombs in the head of your physical body, why did they choose to detonate it at such a moment?

"You had been the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for decades. Your connections, prestige, and control over the Business Alliance must've been incomparable for Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin, right? After you died, whether or not the murderer was captured, and no matter how they were to stabilize the situation, people's confidence in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors would certainly waver, casting a shadow on the upcoming battle.

"Blackstar the Great does not look like such an impatient man. Why would he want you dead at such a critical moment?"

Jin Yuyan was slightly stunned by the question. He thought for a long time.

The more he thought, the brighter his eyes became. In the end, he even put on a smile.
