"We have a chance now."

Jin Yuyan said delightedly.

"How so?"

Li Yao hurried to ask.

"The reason is simple. If it were Blackstar the Great who gave the order in person, he certainly wouldn't have hoped that I died now, which was not in his best interests. Like you said just now, it was his purpose to let my strength be drained in the battle of Seas before I was killed!"

Jin Yuyan said, "However, are the interests of Wuying Qi, as the 'decision-maker', and the interests of Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin, as two 'executors', absolutely the same? Wuying Qi does not want me to die right now, but what about Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin? There are a lot of changeable factors in all this!"

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao asked, "What changeable factors?"

"The possibility that I may change my belief and surrender to Blackstar the Great completely as an obedient dog under his feet!"

Jin Yuyan sneered, "Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin betrayed me only because they were greedy about the control of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Or rather, Wuying Qi bought them over at a high price because he thought that they could replace me and control the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"However, what if I am still willing to cooperate after learning the truth that Wuying Qi is residing inside Li Linghai's body, or even to kneel down and lick Blackstar the Great's toes?

"Yes, under most circumstances, it's absolutely impossible. I will not trust Wuying Qi, and Wuying Qi can hardly trust me 100% too.

"But it is undetermined after all. Whoever masters the Imperium in the end, they will need a financial minister! If I am willing to surrender to Blackstar the Great 100%, there will be no place for Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin. Or rather, after learning the truth, I may burst into fury and launch crazy retaliation against them.

"For the two of them, only my death could've completely eliminated the uncertainty and ensured their control of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors so that Wuying Qi's promises such as titles and lands could be fulfilled. Even though my death might've resulted in the instability of the army, the opportunity was still too great to be missed out!

"That is to say, the assassination was not a direct order from Wuying Qi, but Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin's own doing without asking for permission!"

Li Yao rose abruptly, his eyes glittering. "It's possible that Blackstar the Great is unaware of what has happened in the Seven Seas Grand Market yet. We still have a chance!"

Chapter 2537: Another Sneak-In?

"Yes, Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin must've decided to kill me a long time ago. However, if they did it when it was peaceful and quiet, not only would it raise the suspicion of other managers and shareholders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, but it would also infuriate Blackstar the Great. It would've been too risky."

Jin Yuyan said calmly. When he mentioned his wife, he seemed to be talking about a stranger that he didn't even know. "However, Red Lady's assassination gave them the best opportunity to assign the blame of my death to the four families and take over everything I left behind justifiably. That was why Qi Yuanbao took the chances despite the risk.

"If it hadn't been for the unexpected factor that was you, it was very possible that he would have succeeded. As long as he got the situation under control and led the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to defeat the coalition fleet of the four families, I don't think Blackstar the Great would complain much later or care about how exactly I died.

"After all, it's just like what the Immortal Cultivators say—victors enjoy impunity!"

"However, Qi Yuanbao's critical attack did not work out, and you are still alive."

Curling his finger, Li Yao tapped his thigh softly and said, "So to speak, Qi Yuanbao's attack was rather hasty. It was possible that he hadn't been prepared for everything, and he couldn't have dared to report what happened in the Seven Seas Grand Market to Wuying Qi at all. Otherwise, when Wuying Qi realized that he was more of a saboteur than a contributor and had put his enterprise in unnecessary risks, he would've certainly killed him.

"Director Jin, what do you think Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin will do next?"

"They will probably convene a temporary meeting of managers and shareholders, where they will try to win the support of the managers and the shareholders so that they will temporarily take over the supreme power of the Business Alliance as well as the leadership of the garrisons and special squads under the Business Alliance."

Jin Yuyan said, "There are five senior managers in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, more than forty managers of different levels, and more than a hundred very influential shareholders. Behind the managers and shareholders are the warlords and Sector Masters from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. Some are even the middle class of the four Kurfürst families who cannot rise any higher.

"Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin have to win the support of those people if they want to succeed. Considering that the coalition fleet of the four families would launch a destructive strike at the Seven Seas Grand Market in a couple of days, the warlords and Sector Masters, as well as the middle class and the bottom class within the four families, are anxiously deciding which side they should take too. They are vacillating between destroying the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors according to the Kurfürsten's will, burning their money bags and the hope to rise in the meantime, and rising up ruthlessly to fight the big shots of their families who have been s*itting on their necks all the time!

"Such a thing cannot be postponed forever. If the chaos in the Seven Seas Grand Market lasts more than twenty-four hours, many people in the coalition fleet of the four families will certainly change their attitudes and abandon the friendships of more than a hundred years.

"Therefore, Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin have to get all the permissions in one day and become the new leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!"

"Then…"

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao asked, "Do you think the possibility is high that they can convince the many managers and shareholders? Or rather, since they are obviously not by themselves now that they have made such a risky attempt, how many people do you think are their potential allies or conspirators within the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?"

"Not taking us into consideration, the possibility that Qi Yuanbao is successfully elected as an acting director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is very high."

Jin Yuyan said, "First of all, the capitals of the 'Wuying group' and the big shots behind them will undoubtedly support him and Wuying Qinxin. They may have even reached a secret alliance earlier and been bought over by Blackstar the Great. Secondly, the outside world is unaware of the truth and thinks that I've been assassinated by the four families. So, the subordinates loyal to me and the resources at my disposal will fall into the hands of Wuying Qinxin and be used to support Qi Yuanbao. Thirdly, with a war coming near, lack of leadership means suicide. Since the coalition fleet of the four families is about to flood into the Seven Seas Space Zone, as long as Qi Yuanbao acts tough enough, nobody will compete with him at such a moment. Even the senior managers who are not particularly fond of him will certainly consider the big picture."

"That is to say…"

Li Yao said, "We have to reveal their scheme in twenty-four hours, when Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin convene a temporary meeting of managers and shareholders, in order to stop them?"

"It's a pity that nobody will believe us."

Looking at his thick and stupid body, Jin Yuyan smiled bitterly again, with a hoarse voice that was mixed with sorrow and helplessness. "My body right now is a woman from inside to outside. Even my soul has slightly mutated because of the 'Soul Transfer Technique'. The soul signature that I make is completely different from the past. I cannot even open the safes that I locked, the checks that I signed, or the Cosmos Rings that I sealed myself before. How can I convince other people that I am really Jin Yuyan?

"As for you, 'Vulture Li Yao', it is even more impossible for you to be exposed. You are the greatest traitor that every reformist knows. All the assassin and mercenary groups under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors are aware of the bounties for you. Too many people are whetting their sabers ready to claim your head. The moment you reveal your real identity, you will be drowned in a storm of bullets.

"Think about it. The news from the capital is that you've been bought over by the four families and betrayed the reformists. Now, you suddenly appeared in the Seven Seas Grand Market, right after Red Lady betrayed the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and killed me. How are you going to explain everything? If you were an outsider who doesn't know anything about the situation, would you believe that the woman next to 'Vulture Li Yao' who looks like a chimpanzee is Jin Yuyan, or any of the bulls*it about the 'Soul Transfer Technique'?"

Li Yao scratched his head. "It is indeed hard to explain."

"Explanation means the confession of truth, which brings us to an even more critical problem. It's impossible for you to tell the truth!"

Jin Yuyan continued, "In order to reveal Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin's scheme, you will have to confess your real identity and your background. Then, you have to let everybody know that you are a Cultivator from Firefly that has fled for a thousand years.

"Regretfully, not all the Immortal Cultivators consider money, capital, and profit as their belief like me. As long as there is a 1000% profit, you can ask me to strike an alliance with the Cultivators that is sworn with blood.

"Most of the Immortal Cultivators' disgust, hatred, and wariness of the Cultivators are deep to their bones. Your real identity is even more sensitive than 'a traitor bought over by the four families'. Besides, you destroyed 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky', resulting in the heavy loss of investment for many people in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Many promising researchers were killed in the incident too.

"Don't blame me for not reminding you. The five senior managers, the dozens of regular managers, and the hundreds of shareholders, large and small, had investments in 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky'. Many of them even sponsored 'slaughter streams' personally. After the mess you caused, they were all vomiting blood because of the huge deficits.

"Even if they do not care that you are a Cultivator, you cannot expect them to be fond of you in the slightest. Let me ask you, how are you going to convince them?"

Li Yao scratched his head quickly. "Well…"

"One other thing, Qi Yuanbao knows that I'm not dead and that you are out there. With his cunningness, it shouldn't be hard for him to guess who you are."

Jin Yuyan further increased the difficulty. "I don't know if Blackstar the Great has told part of your real identity to Qi Yuanbao in secret, but I guess he has, because there are few places in the vast universe that a Cultivator like you can go. Blackstar the Great must've foreseen that you would come to seek my cooperation.

"Then, it is not entirely possible that he revealed your identity to Qi Yuanbao in advance for him to take precautions.

"I know Qi Yuanbao's style very well. In terms of head-on combat ability, he is naturally no match for you, but he never attacks the enemy in head-on battles. He never even let the enemy discover him.

"If I were him, I would've dispatched a large group to chase the two of us, strictly forbidding them to talk to me.

"Should you encounter such a squad, would you kill them or not? If you don't, you will be worn out by them. If you kill them, it will be even less possible for you to explain yourself, and it will only prove that you've been bought over by the four families!"

"All the problems boil down to one thing. In order to reveal Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin's scheme, we have to prove that you are the real Jin Yuyan!"

Frowning, Li Yao asked, "Is there no item or way that can prove your identity?"

"There are the surgical records and data about the Soul Transfer Technique."

Jin Yuyan said, "However, all the data are locked in my office, which is the most heavily guarded place in the Seven Seas Grand Market. Even a wisp of smoke cannot sneak in!"

"Why does it sound so familiar to me?"

Li Yao picked his ear and said, "So, the key for us to reverse the situation is the data about your operation of the 'Soul Transfer Technique', and all the data are kept in a place that is defended by the state-of-the-art magical equipment and countless assassins and guards?"

"Exactly. A lot of business secrets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors of the highest level are also stored in the place. Naturally, it is under the most rigorous protection."

Jin Yuyan said, "Theoretically, it can never be compromised or broken in. Why are you smiling so weirdly?"

"Nothing, I'm just curious…"

Li Yao rubbed his cheeks. "Since the data are Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin's greatest weakness, can't they destroy the data in advance?"

"They can't."

Jin Yuyan said, "To destroy the central data, they must have the highest permission of the director, and to get the highest permission, they have to convene a temporary meeting of managers and shareholders and win the votes of more than two-thirds of the participants. Therefore, the data cannot be destroyed by them, at least not in the next twenty-four hours!"

Chapter 2538: Truths and Lies

While talking, Jin Yuyan drew the structure of the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market as well as the defense map around his office. The defensive measures and the distribution of the guards turned into a waterfall of data and poured down.

Li Yao studied and gasped. Grimacing, he said, "Isn't your office too rigorously defended? Also, why are the ventilation tubes and the sewers so narrow and twisted, with the sharp daggers of mystic rays flying all the time? According to the structure, nobody can break in without three days and three nights!"

"But we absolutely don't have three days and three nights."

Jin Yuyan said solemnly, "Once the meeting of managers and shareholders comes to an end and Qi Yuanbao wins the approval of the big shots, the result will not be changed. After he becomes the 'victor', nobody will care about how exactly he has made his way to the top or how I died. They won't even care about whether or not I died. I will completely become the past, and Qi Yuanbao will be the future!"

"Is that so?"

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao continued studying the tubes and gaps that were a hundred times more complicated than a maze, before he asked casually, "Isn't it too cruel?"

"We are Immortal Cultivators. Immortal Cultivators are always cruel."

Jin Yuyan said, "You must understand one thing. Other people elected me as the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors not because I was delightful, not because I was dutiful, and certainly not because I was frank and forthright, but because I could bring more interests to them and defeat the coalition fleet of the four families. Nothing more!

"Then, as long as Qi Yuanbao shows an equal amount of capability, proving that he can also bring more interests to the shareholders and the big shots behind the shareholders and that he can defeat the coalition fleet of the four families, that will be enough. Nobody will care about whether his hands are dirty or how many schemes he has performed!

"Getting to the bottom of it, people of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors are not interested in serving justice or telling right from wrong at all. There is nothing but interests in their eyes!"

Li Yao's eyes were gradually frozen. Stunned for a long time, he asked, "That is to say, even if most directors and shareholders know that you are really Jin Yuyan, as long as they think you cannot bring enough interests to them, they will abandon you and support Qi Yuanbao without any hesitation?"

Gloomily, Jin Yuyan said, "That's only natural. They are all businessmen. As long as the profits are high enough, they can do business with anyone."

Li Yao's eyes were getting brighter and brighter. "From a different perspective, if a certain someone can bring them enough profits and demonstrate enough strength to defeat the four families, even if he is neither Jin Yuyan nor Qi Yuanbao, those managers and shareholders will still support him and do business with him instead without hesitation?"

Slightly stunned, Jin Yuyan thought for a moment and said, "Theoretically, yes. However, Qi Yuanbao and I have been working with the managers and shareholders for a hundred years to convince them of our capabilities. How can a 'certain someone' who emerges out of nowhere convert them?

"The only solution is that you confess your identity and do business with those people as a representative of the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic. Of course, the profits will be inestimable, and anybody can be tempted.

"But like I said just now, not all the Immortal Cultivators are as rational as me. Most Immortal Cultivators are absolutely incompatible with the Cultivators. Besides, you also destroyed 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky', resulting in their heavy losses. Furthermore, one tiny Firefly is far from enough to resolve the threat of the coalition fleet of the four families at all.

"Therefore, no matter what a great talker you are, you cannot persuade them easily. I really don't understand what your 'certain someone' is trying to do. What have you come up with?"

"Nothing. I just want to take a gamble."

Li Yao licked his frozen lips, with a cunning and cold smile. "First of all, the defense of your office is indeed too rigorous. Like you said, it's impossible to sneak in without three days and three nights. Breaking in openly, on the other hand, will cause a huge mess and kill many subordinates that are loyal to you. The misunderstanding will only get more severe.

"Secondly, after the fuss that Qi Yuanbao made, the confidence of the Seven Seas Grand Market must've hit the rock bottom. Even if we seize the control of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors again, the sagging morale can barely be boosted in the upcoming 'battle of Seven Seas'. We will be sure losers if we join the battle like this.

"Therefore, I believe that a gamble is highly necessary. We will take back the control of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the most simple and straightforward ways, and we will make all the warlords and Sector Masters at the edge of the Imperium, and even most of the bottom-class and middle-class Immortal Cultivators within the four families, believe that our capabilities will soar soon, allowing us to crush the four families and the 'false reformists' in the capital easily. They will wager their everything on us and work with us determinedly, making us the pillars as well as the new leaders of the reformists!"

"How is it possible?"

Jin Yuyan looked at Li Yao in disbelief. "All the managers and shareholders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors are the most cunning and paranoid foxes. How can they easily believe your bulls*it and even make us replace Li Linghai as the leaders of the reformists?"

"It's very simple, because what I am going to tell them will not be bulls*it but the truth."

Li Yao said frankly, "I always believe that truth is the most powerful of all. False things are always false. A lie needs the disguise of another hundred lies, and it will still be exposed in the end. So, I will only speak the truth from the beginning to the end. Now, I only need a chance; a chance for me to speak in front of all the managers and shareholders.

"In order to get the chance, we will have to…"

Li Yao elaborated on his chance in great detail for ten minutes.

Jin Yuyan looked at him as if he were the most zealous lunatic and the sleaziest conman.

"You truly have the potential to be an excellent businessman. It's been a long time since I last saw such a shameless, sordid man who could deceive himself without changing his face and who was so good at making a fortune with nothing."

Jin Yuyan said with mixed feelings, "If you had come to the Imperium a hundred years ago, it's possible that the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors today would be you instead of me!"

"Thank you for your compliment. In order to accomplish the plan, we will have to contact some people and prepare an ambush."

Li Yao accepted the praise without changing his expression. "Director Jin, you've been doing business in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for more than a hundred years. You must have made a few good friends who are willing to give you a chance under the most unbelievable circumstances however suspicious they are, right?

"I don't need them to believe that you are you and support you immediately. It will be fine as long as they give me a chance to talk for five minutes!"

"There are some."

Jin Yuyan sighed and said, "However, Wuying Qinxin is well aware of my friendships with those people. She must've monitored the Spiritual Nexus, the communication channels, and the residences of those people closely. However we reach out to those people, the messages will be intercepted."

"It's alright."

Li Yao smiled and said unhurriedly, "I've carried a powerful communication transverter with me, through which we can send the signals to a tiny super-remote communication base that I deployed at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone earlier. Then, we will be able to be connected to the Spiritual Nexus.

"Not far away from the Seven Seas Space Zone, I have some other pals and a… very advanced center of super crystal processors. The information we intend to deliver will be encrypted and disguised by the center of super crystal processors first before it is transferred back to the Seven Seas Grand Market. Rest assured, nobody can possibly intercept or interfere with it!"

While talking, Li Yao fell on one of his knees and retrieved abundant components from his Cosmos Rings before starting to assemble them quickly.

While assembling, he activated the secret communication channel between him and Li Jialing. "Jialing, what's going on outside? Nothing. I'm just checking up on you. Also, I have a tiny mission for you…"

…

In the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market, standing on the planet that was riddled with holes, the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was a splendid and glamorous building like a gold pyramid.

His hands in his back, Qi Yuanbao stood on the sightseeing balcony on the top floor of the plaza, surrounded by special glass that was as clear as water. There was not the slightest trace of steel structure, as if the glass were embedded in the air without any support.

As far as the eyes could reach, the whole planet looked like a glittering beehive. It had long been deformed by the power of human beings and money, brimming with skyscrapers and complicated traffic tubes. Countless people were coming and going like busy ants or worker bees, struggling to survive.

Qi Yuanbao opened all the windows, simply allowing the rushing wind to cut his face. The stink of iron and copper in the wind smelled as pleasant as honey for him.

He liked the scent. He liked all the scents as well as everything that reminded him that he was still a "living person".

Recalling the thrilling, blood-boiling assassination half a day ago, he felt that his heart was still racing crazily. Even his hands were shivering beyond his control.

The feelings let him know that he was still a living person, not a ghost that was haunted by vengeance and lust for power.

Soft footsteps echoed from his back. He did not need to turn back to know that it was his twin sister, Wuying Qinxin.

During the past hundred years, he had been very jealous of his sister because she got to live as a human being, unlike him who had to hide in the shells of other people through the "Body Puppet Technique", until he was unable to tell who he was exactly in the end.

However, right now, he had been used to and even grown fond of the form, and he considered himself to be some sort of existence that was above mankind.

He was not a human being, not even an Immortal Cultivator, but something entirely different and much more superior.

Perhaps it was exactly because of such arrogance that he defied Blackstar the Great and took action in advance, killing Jin Yuyan at an inappropriate time.

It was a pity that something went wrong during the operation.

But he was absolutely capable of resolving the minor error that was named… Vulture Li Yao.

Chapter 2539: Qi Yuanbao's Ambitions!

"What's the situation outside?"

Qi Yuanbao turned back and looked at Wuying Qinxin. "Are the managers and shareholders here? Also, have Red Lady and 'that guy' been caught?"

"Due to the emergency, all the managers and shareholders in the Seven Seas Grand Market have been gathered here. The shareholders who are not in the Sevens Seas Grand Market are also joining the meeting through representatives.'

Jin Yuyan's "widow" Wuying Qinxin looked as perfect as if she were a statue made of marble. She was so perfect that she did not even seem real. Even the vague sorrow covering her eyes and face was like a carefully-designed mask.

Nobody could see through what the woman wanted, not even her biological brother Qi Yuanbao.

Wuying Qinxin paused for a moment and went on, "Red Lady and the assassins of Bloody Skull have been surrounded in a narrow corner in the 27th district. We have locked down all the tubes from the 27th district to the outside world and dispatched three crystal suit legions to besiege them. There are also two Colossi aiming at and suppressing them from space. Even though Red Lady is an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage, it's impossible for her to get away.

"However… there are still no updates about 'that guy'. He seems to have vanished into thin air.

"Ah Bao, I do not feel good. You and I are both clear that it is not important whether the Red Lady is killed or escapes. 'That man' is the key. Not that I'm blaming you, but your attack was indeed a bit hasty."

"Why, sister, do you think I was too reckless?"

Qi Yuanbao's eyebrows and lips were rising at the same time as he looked at Wuying Qinxin with a feigned smile. "Or did your heart soften at the critical moment, and you had true feelings for Jin Yuyan?"

Wuying Qinxin responded with silence. Sighing gloomily, she said, "I just feel that such risks were unnecessary. We could've taken care of him after the battle of Seven Seas."

"Such risks were unnecessary?"

Qi Yuanbao's eyes popped out, and he grinned in a way even more hideous than ghosts. "With everything coming to this point, have you still not recognized the situation or who you are,?

"A hundred and fifty years ago, our father 'Wuying Yuanshan' was a dog domesticated by the four families and a chess piece who could not control himself. When he was needed, he was praised to be the business leader of the 'Wuying group'. By the time his value was fully exploited, and he showed the slightest ambitions, those people changed their attitude without any hesitation and massacred his entire family! That's exactly the ending for a dog and a chess piece!

"In the hundred years that followed, you and I had been struggling in the dark, bloody sea of stars for a slim chance of survival. After the hard work, we seem to boast minor positions and power today, but we are never the masters of our fate. Those things are just candles in the wind and towers on the sand, and they can be obliterated instantly!

"You should be very clear that Jin Yuyan married you because of the hidden connections behind you. He wanted to make use of or even absorb the wealth of the 'Wuying group' through you. The only reason why he supported me was that he hoped that I could help him suppress the other three assassin groups and swallow the entire Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"In other words, we are still chess pieces and dogs that will be cooked when there are no more hares to hunt. Nothing has changed after a hundred years!

"Even if Blackstar the Great has been reborn and promises us high positions and hereditary titles, so what? Do you think that our ancestor really cares for his descendants? Hehe. Don't be silly. We are still just chess pieces and dogs in his eyes!

"To be the real masters of our own fate, we have to boast irreplaceable value and unparalleled strength! If we wait until the battle of Seven Seas is over like you said, there will be too many unexpected factors. Who knows whether or not Jin Yuyan would suddenly change his mind and surrender to the Blackstar the Great? In that case, will there be anything that the two of us can do?

"Even if Jin Yuyan is determined not to surrender, who knows whether or not Blackstar the Great will send someone from the capital to regroup the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in our place? Don't forget that countless members of the royal family are still lingering in the capital. They belong to the system of the Imperial Guards and have always been incompatible with the Wuying descendants at the edge of the Imperium who focus on business!"

Wuying Qi was rather surprised. She asked subconsciously, "It's… It's impossible. Why would His Majesty do that?"

"Don't forget who he is. That is Blackstar the Great! Who knows what's on his mind exactly?"

Qi Yuanbao sneered, "After all, you and I have been working in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for a hundred years. What if we have been too corrupted by the air of 'freedom' to be controlled easily in his eyes? If I were him, chances are that I would play a few tricks so that the two of us would die with Jin Yuyan, before I pick a few trusted subordinates from the capital to take over the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that is in a mess. Is that what you want?"

Wuying Qinxin was stunned for a long time, and her face gradually turned pale.

"The struggles in the past hundred years have taught me the most important lesson. When an opportunity comes, seize it. When it's time to take risks, do it without hesitation!"

Qi Yuanbao said, "As long as we seize the supreme power of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors thunderously and win the battle of Seven Seas, allowing the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to expand into the unquestionable dominator at the edge of the Imperium, then, even 'His Majesty' at the capital will have no reason to extend his hands to our place. By then, we will be able to play the game of balance between the capital and the four families, rising from chess pieces and dogs into players!"

Wuying Qinxin narrowed her eyes, which were turning cold.

"It seems that my sister has thought it through."

Qi Yuanbao smiled and held Wuying Qinxin's hand. Drawing circles in his sister's hand, he said in a low voice, "He is just a random man, right? After we seize the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, you can have however many men you want. Why do you care about a Jin Yuyan? In the past hundred years, he had plenty of affairs outside, and you were certainly not idle when you were lonely either. Why are you acting like you are a chaste woman now?

"Remember, after our cause succeeds, you can have however many men like Jin Yuyan you want, but you only have one twin brother. The universe is a vast place, but we only have each other now, don't we?"

"You… are right."

Wuying Qinxin finally made up her mind and said, "However, you still need to track Jin Yuyan and the guy, who is likely to be 'Vulture Li Yao', who rescued him. You've seen the results of the guy's battles in the past too. He is certainly not easy to deal with and may cause serious trouble to our plan."

"Rest assured. 'Vulture Li Yao' is indeed tough, but why do I have to fight him the hard way?"

With weird brilliance beaming out of his eyes, Qi Yuanbao said casually, "As a matter of fact, I've already guessed where he is right now."

…

Deep inside the complicated tubes between the internal district and external district of the Seven Seas Grand Market, a dark shadow that was covered in a special stealth suit was wriggling inside the tubes.

The suit was very characteristic and looked like a cluster of clear jelly, helping the guy squeeze into all kinds of gaps without alarming anything. Also, it could secrete grease that looked like a shell on the surface, blocking the dispersion of temperature, breath, heartbeat, or smell.

Suddenly—

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Dozens of green scanning rays darted from the depths of the tubes and pierced at the cluster of black jelly precisely.

An amazing thing happened. Right before the mystic rays hit the target, the jelly turned from black into transparency. Even the shape of the human covered inside was entirely gone.

The mystic rays passed through the jelly and shot out from the back after complicated computations without being affected or deviated at all. The guy lurking inside the jelly was not detected either.

The green mystic rays crossed and scanned for a long time, only to discover no anomalies. They gradually dimmed.

The jelly turned from transparency into black again. The invader inside was more or less relieved and continued slithering forward, crawling into the tubes skillfully.

None of the more than ten traps and defense lines behind could stop the invader. He snuck into the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market despite all the dangers. Taking a brief rest, he continued crawling toward Jin Yuyan's office.

…

Inside Qi Yuanbao's office, a hundred 3D light beams were displaying every corner in the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market, particularly the route through which the invader broke in.

The ventilation tube in which the invader was wriggling forward vigorously was closely monitored too.

The keenest sensors hidden on the internal wall of the ventilation tubes were recording the slightest shiver of the ventilation tubes. When all the data of shivers were analyzed comprehensively, even the most brilliant invader would be immediately exposed.

The defense system was designed by Qi Yuanbao, the head of security, in person.

As a super assassin for more than a hundred years, he naturally knew how to deal with the stealthy invaders best.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

From another dozens of 3D light beams, it could be seen that countless Exos who had been armed to their teeth were quickly surrounding the tube that the enemy was sneaking inside.

As long as the invader was beleaguered and caught, it would be another proof that he had malicious plans. There would be no way that he could prove his innocence at all.

"Hehehehe. So much for 'Vulture Li Yao'. He's just a pig that has walked into the trap on his own!"

Qi Yuanbao crossed his fingers, truly like a player who was sure about his victory. "Getting to the bottom of it, he is just another simple-headed warrior with more brawn than brain, and he will be killed by me more than easily!"

Chapter 2540: A Good Show!

Qi Yuanbao rose abruptly and put on the snow-white leather coat again. Not bothering to look at the invader on the light beam who had been entirely surrounded, he strode out of his office.

At the door of his office, the floor slightly rose, separating a round floating disc, which had arc-shaped ribbons shining at the edge and was revolving slowly.

Stepping on the floating disc, Qi Yuanbao moved quickly in the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and soon reached the lift that was specifically reserved for the senior managers.

After inputting his fingerprint, voiceprint, and iris data, Qi Yuanbao gained the permissions of the lift and descended from the 97th floor to the 33rd floor.

It was the largest conference room of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors with the most advanced facilities, and it had never been used easily.

At this moment, it was also a battlefield where his fate would be decided!

Taking a deep breath, Qi Yuanbao opened the door of the lift and flew out on the floating disc.

The vast conference room took up more than ten floors up and down. It was a gigantic room that was as magnificent as a palace and as deep as a cave, enough to accommodate ten thousand people simultaneously for a meeting.

The state-of-the-art remote projection system also allowed for the managers or shareholders in different Sectors a hundred thousand lightyears away to present their encrypted and disguised holograms in the place so that the spokespersons of the interests of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in a hundred Sectors could be gathered.

At this moment, a thousand managers and shareholders had been gathered in the conference room. In order to stabilize the market, a thousand business partners who were in deep cooperation with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were invited to join the meeting too.

The participants who joined the meeting via remote projections were three times more than the actual participants.

Qi Yuanbao narrowed his eyes and looked around with his cold and gloomy eyes.

As he expected, besides the managers and shareholders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors itself, the influential warlords and Sector Masters at the edge of the Imperium had all sent their spokespersons to investigate the situation. Even many of the middle class in the four families were hiding amid the holograms.

Their real bodies were perhaps marching toward the Seven Seas Space Zone on starships, but their projections or spokespersons were still eager to learn how to preserve their interests.

If Qi Yuanbao could show them his strength and convince them of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' inevitable victory, it was possible that they would change their attitudes in the upcoming battle of the Seven Seas.

It was exactly Qi Yuanbao's purpose.

By the time Qi Yuanbao reached the conference room, the conference room was already as obstreperous as a grocery market.

Many people had learned the death of Jin Yuyan from Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin. With a great war coming near, such news was no different than an earthquake.

The middle class of the four families who had been secretly working with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were all as anxious as ants on a burning pan, scratching their heads and not knowing what to do.

They wanted to get away from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, but they were reluctant to abandon the resources that they had invested in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Furthermore, they were worried that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors would expose all of them when it was about to fail, dragging them to hell together.

As for the warlords and Sector Masters who had devoted their everything to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and made up their minds to follow the reformists, they had fewer concerns but simply shouted, asking the leadership of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to come up with a plan to clean up the mess now that Jin Yuyan had died.

Qi Yuanbao intentionally kept those people waiting for another five minutes. The moment he showed up, everybody's scorching eyes fell on him, making him vaguely the new center of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

Qi Yuanbao faced everybody's eyes confidently and casually, as if he had everything under control, but secretly, he looked at Wuying Qinxin and received an affirmative reply—the "Wuying group", which was quite influential in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, would support him.

That was enough.

Qi Yuanbao smiled and slowly flew to the center of the grand conference room. Pressing his hands together, he declared, "Please don't be anxious, everyone. The sky has not collapsed yet, and as long as we work together, it will never collapse!"

The crowd was silent for ten seconds before noises got the better of it again. All the investors and people whose interests were concerned had too many questions they wanted to ask.

"Quiet, everyone. If you could just be slightly more patient and listen to me for three minutes!"

Qi Yuanbao filled his voice with spiritual energy, making the whole conference room shiver under his voice. Suppressing everyone's suspicion and exclamation for now, he said unhurriedly, "I don't want to lie to you. Half a day ago, a tragedy happened outside of the Commodity Market. Director Jin of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was assassinated by the assassins of the shameless four families. He is now unfortunately dead.

"Yes, an accident at such a critical moment when a final battle is about to begin is a huge blow for the market and the morale of our armed forces. However, the situation is not so terrible that it is beyond rescue. Or rather, we already have the situation under control. Everything is in our hands.

"The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is an organization made of the people who worship freedom more than anything else in the first place. We have the most advanced and steady architecture. What pushes us forward is the torrent of capitalism, which will not dry out because of the fall of one leader. With that in mind, what's there to be worried about?

"I believe that if Director Jin were still alive, he would've wished to see us continue believing in free capitals and fighting against the brutal, ravenous aristocrats!

"Everybody, everybody, we have preliminarily found the truth of Director Jin's death. The four Kurfürst families bought over 'Bloody Skull', and it was done by 'Red Lady', the leader of 'Bloody Skull', in person. The relevant files will be sent to your terminals immediately. You will be able to tell if it is true after examining it."

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Tremendous data was sent to everyone's crystal processor. All the proofs corroborated with each other. They couldn't have been more convincing.

The managers, shareholders, and investors nodded their heads, unable to find any loophole.

"After the assassination, most of the members in Bloody Skull were already suppressed by us, and only part of the most ferocious members fled with 'Red Lady'. They are still resisting, but there is no need to worry. Almost a third of the armed forces in the entire Seven Seas Grand Market is involved in the hunting. It is impossible for the Red Lady to escape."

Qi Yuanbao suddenly changed the topic. "You must be very curious. If we have only sent one-third of the armed forces to hunt Director Jin's killer, where are the remaining two thirds?

"It's very simple. 'Red Lady' is not the mastermind behind the whole scheme. We even have reason to believe that even Director Jin's assassination was just a disguise for an even greater plot.

"An enemy who is ten times more dreadful than 'Red Lady' has snuck into the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market, trying to cause greater damage and mayhem before the arrival of the four families' coalition fleet and make us fail!

"The enemy is exactly—Vulture Li Yao!"

Shua!

Hardly had Qi Yuanbao's desperate voice died down when the light beam around him displayed the gigantic picture of a grimacing Li Yao.

It was shot in public when Li Yao was still the "No. 1 warrior" of the reformists.

The gigantic picture made everybody gasp hard in disbelief.

"There's no need to feel surprised, everyone. The news that Vulture Li Yao betrayed the reformists and joined the four Kurfürsten's side came from the capital. I believe that you all have your own sources of intelligence, and they have reported the same, haven't they?"

Qi Yuanbao smiled with his hands on his back. "Vulture Li Yao has only defected recently. Naturally, he is anxious to make a great accomplishment in order to consolidate his position and win the approval of his new masters.

"Assassinating Director Jin alone is not necessarily enough to shake the foundation of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, because any of the five senior managers, after being authorized in this meeting, can replace Director Jin and take over his responsibilities.

"However, if most of our defense is distracted by the riot after Director Jin's death, and he takes the chance to sneak into our most central database, destroying all the trade data, just think about it, what consequences will be caused?

"Of course, our trade data have backups, and we have more than one center of super crystal processors. However, the destruction of the central data will disrupt our trades for three to five days. All the information about capitals, logistics, shares, options, and bonds will be messed up and paralyzed!

"As a result, the whole market will freak out, and all the other forces will lose confidence in us, even thinking that we can't make ends meet anymore, since we cannot support such an enormous network of capitals and logistics!

"If the coalition fleet of the four families takes the opportunity to attack us, wouldn't it be our doomsday?"

The horrible future that Qi Yuanbao described cast all the managers, shareholders, and investors into extreme shock.

Everybody looked at each other in bewilderment, finding it hard to believe Qi Yuanbao's alarmism. One of the white-haired, prestigious managers said solemnly, "Manager Qi, this is a very important matter. Do you have any proof for your conclusion?"

Qi Yuanbao smiled casually and clapped his hands, saying, "Of course I do. Why else could I have been bold enough to talk about the nonsense in front of so many investors and partners?

"Allow me to present to you a great show!"

As he clapped his hands, more light beams were turned on. All of them were displaying the complicated tubes and gaps between the internal district and the external district not far away from the headquarters.

Through the combination of the pictures, it could be clearly seen that the ignorant invader was still wriggling wholeheartedly, and the defenders had surrounded him from all directions, pressing toward him without a sound.

"Take action now."

Holding his temples, Qi Yuanbao spoke to the micro communicator, sneering, "Capture 'Vulture Li Yao'!"

Chapter 2541: Battle Hymn Rises Again!

After Qi Yuanbao gave the order, all the light beams seemed to be displaying ten times faster.

The defenders covered their bodies with their crystal suits without leaving a single gap. All their cannons and flying sword launchers were activated. The machine guns were revolving quickly too.

The battle puppets rolled out from the projection holes on the two sides of the heavy crystal tanks. Colorful lines appeared on their surfaces as they were dismembered and assembled into battle forms.

The armed shuttles were rushing by from the midair. Their earsplitting noises were even making the whole conference room clatter. From below the cross-shaped wings of every armed shuttle, dozens of crimson rays shot out at a certain point on the artificial sky, illuminating the simulated blue sky and white clouds into redness!

Furthermore, accompanied by the deafening explosions, three iron giants dozens of meters tall arose and moved toward the artificial sky in a triangle. They were the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' own Colossi.

The invader who was still struggling to move forward also sensed the gathering of a great army outside. He seemed to be scared out of his wits and was meaning to escape to the external district in the same way he came, before he ran to space.

However, there was no time.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The Exos, battle puppets, crystal tanks, and armed shuttles aimed at the artificial sky around the invader and opened fire at the same time, almost turning the whole sky into a melted ocean of iron. One second later, the whole artificial sky collapsed, and even the invader was falling too!

Trying his best, the invader intended to escape through the hole that the defenders had blown out.

However, the defenders had calculated his every possible route of escape. They blocked his retreat with the most overwhelming firepower. Every attempt to escape made by the invader ended in failure, and he was forced to get back to where he was in the beginning.

He was like a fly that had been caged into a glass jar, only getting his own head broken wherever he crashed into.

"Do you see, everyone? After a hundred years of construction and training, the Seven Seas Grand Market today is not a place that anybody can enter or leave freely!"

Qi Yuanbao smiled. "If you want, you can even go to the window and observe the battlefield on your own. The battlefield is only no more than thirty kilometers from us. You can see the rising smoke and the barrage from the window.

"No, no, no. There's no need to worry about your safety. For the battle of Seven Seas, all the experts of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and even the edge of the Imperium have been gathered in the Seven Seas Grand Market. We have the most excellent killers, the most brutal mercenaries, and the most fearless gladiators. Of course, we also have the most advanced battle puppet system. Even if there may still be a gap between our fleet and the four families', the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is invincible when it comes to the quality and experience of individuals!

"We have run detailed analyses on Vulture Li Yao's public battle videos. Even a guy as crazy as him can't deal the slightest damage to our headquarters. You are all absolutely safe!

"This is also a great opportunity to show you our strength that has been hidden for a hundred years. Vulture Li Yao can't sabotage the Seven Seas Grand Market, and the four families' fleet can't disrupt our businesses and yours! As long as you stay on our side unwaveringly, your investments will be returned ten times and a hundred times more!

On the light beams behind Qi Yuanbao, dozens of glamorous fireballs were burning, like a stage made of scorching flames.

With that as the background, he opened his arms to all the managers, shareholders, investors, and partners of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Thick veins were protruding on his forehead, as if he had put on a horrible mask, while he demanded aggressively, "You've seen it with your own eyes. Do you still have any reason to hesitate? The four families made use of 'Vulture Li Yao' and resorted to such vicious methods exactly because they knew that they were too disorganized and had too low a morale for them to defeat us in a space battlefield!

"Unite! As long as we are united enough, we can conquer all the enemies and grab the radiance of all the stars in the universe in our hands!

"Now, in light of the unfortunate death of the former director Jin Yuyan and the urgent situation, I request the meeting to authorize me with the permissions of the acting director so that I can command all the armed forces of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors on the continent and in space to win the upcoming battle of Seven Seas!"

Qi Yuanbao's deafening voice swept across the entire conference room like rumbling thunders.

The other four senior managers whispered to each other solemnly. Although they were still rather suspicious of him, they did not object to him or decline his request immediately.

The members of the "Wuying group" among the regular managers and shareholders also started cheering for him and instigating more people to support him.

The noises grew louder and louder, gradually congregating into a flood of shock and exclamation.

Qi Yuanbao listened gloatingly at first, confident of his victory.

But the more he listened, the more inappropriate he felt. Why did everybody look so weird? Also, the gunshots and explosions behind him were dying down. What was going on? Did Vulture Li Yao escape?

Qi Yuanbao turned around and watched, only to discover that the "intense" battle on the surveillance light beams had come to an end.

The invader did not escape or resist. Instead, he raised his hands and surrendered!

Also, he seemed to be scared that the defenders would kill him, and he had shot out black mystic rays from his backpack, writing two words that were dozens of meters in length and width on the sky:

"I surrender!"

The exclamation mark at the end was thick and long, covering almost a hundred square meters. With the sight of the Immortal Cultivators, they could see it from dozens of kilometers away.

Many managers and shareholders ran to the windows to check. They immediately exclaimed in disbelief, because they really saw the two gargantuan words hanging on the artificial sky.

Qi Yuanbao's forehead was immediately covered in a cold sweat.

The truth of Jin Yuyan's assassination was a top secret. He and Wuying Qinxin were almost the only informants.

The armed forces who went to hunt the invader knew nothing of it. It was impossible for him to demand the defenders to kill the enemy no matter what, which would've been too strange an order.

He had thought that Vulture Li Yao would definitely resist hard after being discovered, resulting in severe casualties. There would be no chance of communication and exposition at all. Even if they did talk, it would be impossible for Vulture Li Yao to explain his identity and background.

Therefore, in his deduction, the worst-case scenario was that Vulture Li Yao escaped, which would be even better, because everybody could only believe his side of the story then.

He did not expect that Vulture Li Yao would play such a move. The guy had surrendered without even activating his Colossus? It… It must be a plot!

However, before he shouted out his conclusion, Qi Yuanbao had discovered that his trouble had only just begun.

On the dozens of light beams, the invader was still standing dutifully with his hands raised high, but the crystal suit on his body suddenly fell apart and was tossed away. His combat suit was also torn apart by spiritual energy, revealing a… furry body that looked like a cheetah and a pair of bright golden eyes.

It was a hybrid with the genes of demons—a "half-demon"!

"This is—"

Dumbfounded, Qi Yuanbao was lost for words.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Countless people looked at him suspiciously, especially the other four senior managers who were on par with him. Their eyes were filled with curiosity and wariness, as if they were questioning him, Is this really "Vulture Li Yao"?

Vulture Li Yao was an out-and-out pure human being, at least without any apparent genes of demons. It was a fact that everybody knew.

Even if he needed a new appearance to sneak into the Seven Seas Grand Market, it seemed unnecessary for him to pretend to be a half-demon. The Imperium was not entirely devoid of half-demons and demons, but those eccentrics were very eye-catching and certainly not the best camouflage.

Then, on what grounds did Qi Yuanbao believe that the invader was "Vulture Li Yao"?

Then, the black words above the head of the half-demon wriggled and changed, delivering a new piece of information:

"Stay away from the windows, and cover your ears with spiritual energy."

What was the meaning of that?

Everybody in the grand conference room looked at each in confusion, not knowing to whom the half-demon was talking. The armed forces that surrounded him obviously had no windows around. It was actually them, the managers, shareholders, and investors in the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors dozens of kilometers away, who were surrounded by transparent windows.

Could it be—

"Don't be nervous. Just relax."

The black words above the half-demon change again. "I only want to talk with you about that business. Three, two, one!"

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Before anyone realized what was going on, it was like ten thousand stun bombs had exploded around the headquarters at the same time. The glass wall around the conference room immediately fell apart. The hardest tempered glass turned into broken pieces that looked like sand, splashing out. The people closest to the windows were even blown away for more than ten meters by the soundwaves. The holograms remotely projected were also torn into twisted ripples, unable to be condensed again for a long time.

The most violent sound waves were like a torrent made of thunders and flames, blasting the entire conference room as well as everyone's eardrums and heart.

It was a primitive, brutal, and barbaric battle hymn. Every note seemed to have fluttered out after an axe slashed into a bone. The notes were then mixed and orchestrated again, with the bombardment of the giant cannons on starships as the background, sounding ten times more daunting than before!

Everybody's eardrums and heart seemed to be brushed by highly-explosive bombs again, again, and again.

They vaguely felt that the battle hymn sounded familiar, as if they had heard it somewhere a long, long time ago, but their boiling brains and their exploding hearts forbade them from further recalling their memories.

Crazy wind, mixed with the scent of iron and smoke, flowed into the conference room overwhelmingly, making everybody's soul shiver.

Dumbfounded, they could only watch the burning steel god standing at the center of the soundwaves outside of the empty window frame, the super Colossus named "Arsonist", as if they had fallen into a nightmare!

Chapter 2542: The Unspeakable Identity!

The Colossus that was as dark as a swirl, as brutal as a fiend, and as deep as a black hole was destined to be the most devastating part in the nightmare of the observers.

It was simply standing between the sky and the earth, not even in a battle posture, but the most daunting vibe was already flooding out.

Countless black flames were rising to the sky, congregating into pillars of smoke that were almost concrete and sprawling in all directions. The blue sky and the white clouds were eclipsed, and the whole headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was enshrouded in a hazy mist.

If one were to observe closely, they would find that the mist was actually made of countless twisted fiends, as if the black Colossus was commanding a great army and surrounding the heart of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

One person and one Colossus had surrounded the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!

It wasn't that the managers, shareholders, and investors of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had never seen any Colossus before. As a matter of fact, because of its unique advantage, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had collected plenty of Colossi during the hundred years. It was one of the temptations they used to attract the great warriors to work for them.

However, the Colossi of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were mostly the assembled products, which had been seriously damaged on the battlefield before. Some of them were even duplicates whose original components had been replaced by mass-produced components. Despite the quantity, they were not nearly as good as the Colossi under the control of the four Kurfürst families in terms of quality. It often took two to three miscellaneous Colossi of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to compete with an advanced Colossus of the four Kurfürst families.

The black Colossus, on the other hand, was an ultimate Colossus of the ultimate Colossi based on the style, the air, and the soaring spiritual energy. Even the experts who had traveled in the Imperium for a hundred years had never seen such ultimate weapons!

It seemed that the one Colossus was enough to pierce through the entire Seven Seas Grand Market and butcher each and every one of them. Even though they knew that it was only their illusion, their nerve endings were still quivering beyond their control, and they couldn't calm themselves down at all.

That was the real god of destruction and the unparalleled weapon that could even slay Pangu and Nuwa!

The black Colossus floated outside of the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors quietly, intentionally waiting for the defenders to surround him.

However, even if the defenders had reinforced in a haste, and several Colossi had arrived, how were they bold enough to attack the aggressive super Colossus easily?

Compared to the glamorous, brutal, and powerful Colossus, the Colossi of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were like ragged garbage that was riddled with holes.

The battle hymn from the chest of the black Colossus was even more violent. Finally, somebody remembered its name from the resounding rhythm. Their faces immediately turned pale as if they had just suffered a lightning strike. Their lips shivering, they stuttered, "It's… It's impossible… It's absolutely impossible!"

Qi Yuanbao was so scared that his brain cells were all frozen. He perceived two facts during the moment.

Firstly, Vulture Li Yao had predicted his every move. Li Yao did not break into the ventilation tube in person at all but merely sent an unimportant subordinate, intentionally letting the guy be discovered so that all his available forces and his own attention would be distracted!

One third of the armed forces under his control were still hunting Red Lady and the assassins of Bloody Skull in the external district, and the other two thirds were distracted by the decoy. When the place was utterly undefended, Li Yao could simply barge in openly.

He thought that Li Yao wanted to steal the critical data stealthily, but as it turned out, Li Yao had marched to the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors without caring anything through the simplest move of distraction!

Secondly, Vulture Li Yao, the shameless liar and the ferocious fiend, had been hiding his strength the whole time!

The capability that Li Yao was demonstrating at this moment was more than three times stronger than when he was a fighter for the reformists in the "Gold Vulture". Plus the Colossus that could be called the worst nightmare, even if he hadn't played any trick of distraction, he still could've barged in openly.

Nobody could've stopped him. Nobody could've stopped the fiendish Colossus or Vulture Li Yao!

At this moment, Qi Yuanbao finally understood why Wuying Qi and Li Linghai reminded him again and again that he must inform the capital the moment he discovered the traces of Vulture Li Yao or even only heard anything about the monster, and that he must now act alone against Li Yao.

Qi Yuanbao had always been confident about himself. He thought that the dozens of mysterious soul techniques that he had practiced and the "Body Puppet Technique", which allowed him to assume hundreds of different appearances, were enough for him to defeat any enemy in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Not paying heed to Wuying Qi and Li Linghai's warning, he was consumed by the fire of ambition.

Little did he expect that the blow could come so soon. He was about to slide into the abyss of doom before he got to taste the feeling of victory.

Qi Yuanbao shook hard. His eyes were so red that blood was almost dripping. Holding back his burning soul, he looked at Wuying Qinxin, only to discover that his sister was equally dumbfounded and lost about what to do.

No!

Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul roared like a ghost from hell. I haven't lost yet! I still have chances! However strong he is, he is all alone after all. Can he defeat all the soldiers here? Besides, he has a fatal weakness, which is his identity! He daren't expose his real identity at all. Then, nobody will believe what he says. Can he take down each and every person in this place?

While he was thinking, the black Colossus standing in the midair finally raised the right leg and made the first step.

BOOM!

The black Colossus stepped on the air, and ripples that looked like the fire of inferno spread out in a circle quickly, sabotaging the skyscrapers within ten kilometers again. This time, not just the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, but all the plazas nearby had broken glass, which seemed to be a storm of diamonds!

"Excuse me. Did I scare anyone?"

Li Yao's gloomy chuckle echoed from the black Colossus. "Don't be nervous. Just relax. I'm not here to kill you… at least not for now."

Stepping in the air, the black Colossus "walked" to the building!

Everybody's heart was almost frozen. While tens of thousands of Exos and multiple times more battle puppets had been gathered in the place, all the people and machines had been too deeply awed by Li Yao's vibe to wave their blades or pull their triggers.

"Don't… Don't believe him, everyone. Every word he says is a lie!"

Qi Yuanbao made up his mind and shouted at the loudest of his voice, "He has betrayed the reformists and is now a dog of the four Kurfürst families! Every word he says is a lie to dissolve our unity and morale!"

While he was talking, the black Colossus had already "walked" to the broken window of the building.

Next, Li Yao made an unexpected movement. He simply opened the cabin on the chest of the Colossus and drifted to the center of the grand conference room!

He was not even wearing a crystal suit and was only covered in a half-solid black combat suit. However, thousands of black feathers were swirling around him slowly like an extremely weird tornado. It was not until a long time later that they were condensed behind his back into a black cape more than ten meters long, which was still dancing in the evilest and luxurious way!

Although the black Colossus was still floating outside of the building, and the iron tentacles were raging unpredictably like black dragons, the pressure on the spot was still more or less eased after he showed up with his real self. Many shareholders and investors who joined the meeting via remote projection grew curious too.

After all, their real bodies were not here. Even if Li Yao were ten times stronger and completely razed the Seven Seas Grand Market, nothing would happen to them at all.

They were simply curious about one thing. Why did Vulture Li Yao, the ace warrior of the reformists in the past, who was then announced to have defected to the four families uncannily, show up in such a way? What exactly did he want?

"Since the celebrities of the peripheral worlds of the Imperium have been gathered here, a lot of my trouble can be saved."

Li Yao smiled, with his hands on his back. The brutal auras burst out, making the cape of black feathers flit nonstop. "Let me reintroduce myself. I am Vulture Li Yao, and I'm here to talk about a 'minor' business with you."

"Vulture Li Yao, you have obviously defected to the four families' side. Not only did you kill Director Jin, but you also attempted to destroy the central trade data of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. You are bold enough to come here!"

Qi Yuanbao roared, "Everybody knows that 'decapitation' is your favorite. Are you planning to kill all the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors here? Then you can start with me!"

"Quiet!"

Li Yao glared at him and opened his hands, snatching at Qi Yuanbao casually. A gigantic black hand of spiritual energy immediately appeared out of nowhere in a scary wind, forcing Qi Yuanbao to scream and dodge in a hurry.

Li Yao, however, stopped after the snatch. He sniffed and said, "Killing you is as easy as butchering a dog for me. However, filthy swine like you are not qualified to stain my hands. Be quiet and back off. Don't be an eyesore!"

"You—"

Qi Yuanbao was one of the five senior managers of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors after all, and he had never been so humiliated in front of the managers, shareholders, investors, and business partners before. Blushing, he let out his fatal move without caring about anything. Raising his voice, he shouted, "Vulture Li Yao, who are you exactly? Nobody in the entire Imperium of True Human Beings has ever heard of your name before. You basically emerged out of nowhere, and yet you were so strong and had such a… weirdly-styled Colossus?

"Which sect and which family did you come from? Where did you advance into the Divinity Transformation Stage? Where did you get your super Colossus? I guarantee with my head that there was absolutely never someone like you in the Imperium of True Human Beings before. Are you a super spy that the Alliance of the Holy Covenant carefully trained in order to sabotage the Imperium? Or maybe, do you have other secret and unknown identities that you daren't reveal at all?

"Speak now! You don't have anything to say for yourself now, do you? Hahahaha. With such an obscure identity and background, you are not going to convince us of anything you may say!"

Chapter 2543: Black Storm Born!

After hearing that, a lot of the Immortal Cultivators nodded their heads quickly.

Those qualified to be present in this conference room were all the most sordid and cunning guys, who could come up with a couple of tricks in the blink of an eye. How could they have not been suspicious of someone who rose abruptly and brilliantly like Li Yao?

Before, Li Yao was endorsed by Queen Li Linghai, and he was simply a fighter. So, they could let go of Li Yao's background and origins and accept whatever Li Linghai claimed.

However, the situation of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and even the entire Imperium was at the most critical moment of change. They couldn't help but feel skeptical now that Li Yao had appeared out of nowhere again.

For a moment, many Immortal Cultivators were looking at Li Yao cautiously and warily.

The Immortal Cultivators whose holograms were in the Seven Seas Grand Market instead of their real bodies had even fewer reasons to be scared of Li Yao. They simply whispered to each other.

Li Yao narrowed his eyes with a gloomy face, as if he did have secrets that he would rather not talk about.

Qi Yuanbao grew even cockier after seeing that. He laughed. "Vulture Li Yao, just confess your identity if you can! If you find it impossible to open your mouth, you can kill me and keep my mouth shut alternatively! But even if you do kill me, you will never bend so many great heroes here!"

Li Yao rolled his eyes again and again, before he said coldly, "Qi Yuanbao, you seem very certain that there is something wrong with my identity?"

"Of course!"

Qi Yuanbao shrieked, "All the Immortal Cultivators here are experts above average. Everybody knows how many resources must be consumed and what a powerful force must be depended on in order to raise an experienced expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage! It is needless to say that even your Colossus is a rarely-seen product in the four Kurfürst families! The Imperium in the past hundred years couldn't have raised such an expert like you or witnessed such a Colossus! I'm even suspicious that you are not from the Imperium at all. Who are you? Speak! Speak now!"

"That's right…"

The other four senior managers talked to each other for a while, before an old senior manager said prudently, "Fellow Cultivator Li, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors only wants to do business in peace and does not want to wage war at any force, but you have to give us an explanation now that you have barged in recklessly today. How else is the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors going to be respected in the Imperium in the future?"

"Exactly!"

Qi Yuanbao declared brutally, "If you cannot give a satisfactory explanation, we will not let you do whatever you want even if we have to die with you!"

The atmosphere grew intense again.

The doors around the conference room were suddenly wide open, and countless defenders flooded in like an iron torrent.

The sky and ground outside were also brimming with crystal suits, armed shuttles, and all kinds of powerful attack magical equipment. Even several cannons that were designed for warships had been dragged onto the planet and aimed at the "Arsonist"!

Since the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was bold enough to challenge the four families, it certainly had remarkable strength. Not only did it have the "four gods of death", assassins in the Divinity Transformation Stage, but it also had plenty of battle-type Immortal Cultivators in the Nascent Soul Stage. There were thousands of experts in the Core Formation Stage too, who were also capable of fighting after putting on advanced crystal suits.

When there were too many ants, they could bite an elephant to death. It was a truth that worked for both the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators.

The grand conference room was filled with silence. Everybody held their breath and secretly touched their Cosmos Rings as they waited for Li Yao's reply.

"Hehe! Hehehehe! Hahahahahaha!"

Li Yao opened his fingers and stabbed them into his messy black hair while he let out the crazy, fearless laughter.

In his laughter, the black flames of spiritual energy around him burst into and turned into black air currents that were visible to the naked eye.

The black tornado that could swallow everything took shape again!

The black storm immediately enshrouded the whole conference room, making it hard for the Immortal Cultivators to keep steady.

Qi Yuanbao suffered the worst. Faced with Li Yao's overwhelming and brutal vibe, every muscle on his face was shivering crazily under the blow. He could barely keep his eyes open.

"Vulture Li Yao is never a stealthy man, and I hate the hilarious tricks such as pseudonyms or camouflages most. Besides, with my power and my armed force today, I can easily whip the galaxy and savage the sea of stars. Why do you think I am afraid to speak it? Just open your eyes, prick your years, and keep your mind from being blown. Listen up!"

Li Yao's voice was like an astounding thunder in the black storm that bombarded every seat in the grand conference room, which seemed to be only one-hundredth of its previous size under the suppression of his spiritual energy. The Immortal Cultivators in the room found it barely possible to catch their breath.

Even the remote participants whose real bodies were thousands of lightyears away also felt the haunting fear, which was stabbing their spines heavily like needles out of the light beam.

All the Immortal Cultivators seemed to have fallen into a nightmare. In shock, they watched Li Yao raise his right hand high in the black storm, where a black Cosmos Ring glittered and dumped a delicate scepter. The scepter was then grabbed by Li Yao.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The scepter seemed to have entailed infinite strength and information. After being grabbed by Li Yao heavily, light, shadow, and information immediately flowed out among his fingers into countless soul marks. It looked like many tiny figures were crouching around Li Yao and worshipping him.

"They are the… soul marks of the captains and experts of the Black Wind Fleet who left the capital for the edge of the cosmos more than a hundred years ago!"

"It seems true. They are truly the air of the experts of the five worlds including the Black Wind Sector. I know a few of the experts!"

"So many experts of the five worlds have sent the soul marks that represent their submission to Vulture Li Yao. That's… That's impossible!"

"This scepter… Is the scepter in his hands the legendary—"

A lot of the Immortal Cultivators jumped to their feet.

Years ago, the series of events where the Black Wind Fleet blackmailed the government and escaped from the Imperium after getting tremendous resources, leading to a serious economic crisis, was deeply associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, if not planned by it behind the curtain. Di Feiwen, the deputy commander of the Black Wind Fleet, was one of the high-level managers of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors too.

Therefore, there were naturally many managers and shareholders in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors who knew the experts of the Black Wind Fleet as well as the scepter.

Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin were awed by the scepter that Li Yao suddenly took out and the soul marks that he activated too. It was like Li Yao had slapped their faces a hundred times with his black hands. Even the fire of his soul was almost blown out.

The sister and brother looked at each other in bewilderment. What else could they have done except for holding back their tears?

Li Yao's showoff, however, had not ended yet. After tremendous soul marks flowed out of the scepter, the structures of starships were running out too. For a moment, it seemed that a hundred golden warships that were as magnificent as mountains were surrounding him.

The Immortal Cultivators of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were all seasoned sea wolves who had been spending their lifetimes in space voyages. They could tell after the first glance that they were the structures of the main-force warships of the Black Wind Fleet, even including the flagship Black Swirl, in the old days!

Such structures were not exactly secrets, but even the starships of the same model would always be specifically modified according to the requests of the commander and the nature of the mission.

The modifications that the Black Wind Fleet did before embarking on the expedition were partly done with the help of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Many people recognized the unique details that nobody except for the real controller of the fleet could've fabricated.

"Has the Black Wind Fleet, which has wandered at the edge of the cosmos for a hundred years, fallen into this guy's hands?"

"No wonder he is so blatant and dominating. He is—"

Before their exclamations died out, a third torrent of information flushed out of Li Yao's scepter. They were the star maps of many Sectors at the edge of the cosmos far away from the Imperium. They were the keys to treasuries that all the Immortal Cultivators dreamt about!

"Ah!"

The eyes of all the Immortal Cultivators were occupied by the dense star maps. With greed all over their faces, they couldn't help but moan in thirst.

However, the star maps that represented sailing routes, markets, profits, relics, secret treasures, and slaves only flashed for half a second before they were retreated by Li Yao.

Many people subconsciously reached for them, only to catch the broken light and shadow in the end.

The overwhelming black storm gradually weakened. The black flames of spiritual energy turned into delicate, splendid feathers that fell to Li Yao's shoulders and back, covering him in a glamorous black coat. Every feather also had glittering gold stars at the edge, which decorated the black coat with a complicated gold stripe, further highlighting Li Yao's mysteriousness and domination.

"Now, is there any blind idiot who still can't tell who I am?"

Two tiny red spots were still vaguely glittering inside Li Yao's eyes that were as dark as ink, making him look indescribably cold.

Wherever his gloomy eyes reached, the Immortal Cultivators lowered their heads, not having the courage to look back at him.

He looked around the grand conference room, and when all the Immortal Cultivators had lowered their heads under his eyes, he grinned and touched the black scepter, saying casually, "Exactly.
