I am exactly the Black Wind King, Black Storm Born, the Immortal Cultivator of the Immortal Cultivators, the Commander of the Black Wind Fleet, the Conqueror of the Edge of the Cosmos, the Destroyer of the Star Ocean Republic, the Nightmare Butcher of the Immortal Cultivators, the Dominator and Protector of the Black Wind Sector, the Purple Fire Sector, the Red Spider Sector, the Hard Rock Sector, and the Wild Wolf Sector!

"Qi Yuanbao, you are rather smart for a pig. It's true that my name has never been heard in the Imperium in the past hundred years. However, I'm back today, with a Black Wind Fleet that is ten times stronger than a hundred years ago! A hundred years from today, every Immortal Cultivator in the Imperium of True Human Beings will bend before me, hear my magnificent feats, and press forward to claim more glories following my saber! Haha, hahahahahaha!"

Li Yao laughed crazily.

Chapter 2544: The Black Wind King's Story

While Li Yao laughed arrogantly, most of the Immortal Cultivators were still dwelling on what he said just now. They were either considering the consequences now that the "Black Wind Fleet" had been added to the game as an unexpected factor, or thinking how to make friends with Li Yao and share the profits at the edge of the cosmos, or whispering to each other while trying to locate loopholes in Li Yao's words.

Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin, on the other hand, found it absolutely impossible to laugh. They lost the ability to think too. Like two puppets whose strings had been cut off, they were overwhelmed and paralyzed.

Li Yao's eyes grew sharper and sharper and darted into Qi Yuanbao's body like hooks. He grinned hideously. "Qi Yuanbao, are you satisfied now that you've learned my identity? I'm rather curious about one thing. Why have you been so hostile to me since the beginning, fabricating nonexistent schemes, slandering me, and even mobilizing so many people to surround me? Can you bear the consequences if the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the Black Wind Fleet are engaged in a war in the end? Or rather, is it not me but you who have really betrayed the reformists and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and you are intentionally making the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors fight the Black Wind Fleet through conspiracies?"

Everybody was rather shocked after hearing that. All the Immortal Cultivators couldn't help but picture a total war between the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the Black Wind Fleet. The Black Wind Fleet's combat ability was among the most distinguished even in the entire Imperium. Even if it might not be as good as Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet, the gap couldn't have been huge. Also, since they had been fighting at the borderland all the time, they were even more tough and ruthless than Lei Chenghu's troops.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors at this moment was faced with the enormous military pressure from the four families' coalition fleet. How could they afford to lose an ally and to have an additional formidable enemy?

Whether Qi Yuanbao meant it or not, his recklessness was highly inconsiderate and nearly caused a catastrophe to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!

"Fellow Cultivator Li—no, Black Wind King, please don't be angry!"

Sweating hard, the other four senior managers shouted at the same time, "We absolutely do not mean to be hostile to the Black Wind Fleet. As forces growing up at the edge of the Imperium, we are natural allies, and we should be united against the four families!"

"I thought so too. That's why I came to the Seven Seas Grand Market to talk about business with you in good faith, or rather, to give you a hand."

Li Yao sniffed and looked at Qi Yuanbao with the corner of his eyes. "However, one of your five senior managers does not seem to welcome me to be here. He is even scared of my arrival. Why is that?"

"About that…"

The other four senior managers frowned and looked at Qi Yuanbao doubtfully at the same time.

The other managers, shareholders, and investors did not hide their dissatisfaction and suspicion about Qi Yuanbao either.

His face pale, Qi Yuanbao drifted backward for more than ten meters before he realized that all of the exits had been blocked by crystal suits and battle puppets. Managing to stand steadily, he waved his arms like someone struggling not to be drowned and shouted crazily, "It's impossible! You can't be the Black Wind King! Isn't Heiye Ming the commander of the Black Wind Fleet? Also, you sent a message before long that you were dealing with barbarians at the edge of the cosmos. How did you suddenly march back to the Imperium?

"Don't be fooled by him! This 'Vulture Li Yao' is best known for his shamelessness and cunningness. You cannot believe any of the words he says. So much as you believe one of them, we will be pushed into the hopeless abyss of doom!"

Qi Yuanbao felt so wronged that he was almost cutting his chest to show his heart to all the Immortal Cultivators.

"Hehehehe. Heiye Ming has become history since a long time ago. Right now, I am the commander of the Black Wind Fleet, and I'm also the Black Wind King that is acknowledged by all the experts of the Black Wind Sector, the Purple Fire Sector, the Red Spider Sector, the Hard Rock Sector, and the Wild Wolf Sector!"

His hands in his back, Li Yao said casually, "I know that many people here know the experts from the five Sectors but might have never heard my name before. It's reasonable that you have suspicions like this Manager Qi does.

"It's true that Vulture Li Yao was just a Mr. Nobody a hundred years ago that nobody knew.

"However, after the Black Wind Fleet left the Imperium and went to explore the sea of stars, when most of the experts fell into dormancy, the battle squad that I belonged to was given an important reconnaissance mission.

"When we explored a mysterious relic, all the other people in my squad died, and I was the only one who luckily survived. Also, I picked up a technique named 'Star Absorption Art' by accident, which granted me the ability to swallow other people's spiritual energy!

"In the coming hundred years, most of the members of the Black Wind Fleet were sleeping in the hibernation capsules. They were like fat, succulent meat waiting to be feasted upon by me.

"You do not need to know the specific process. All in all, after I reached the highest level, I took down—no, our beloved former commander Heiye Ming died in an accident. Since I was respected and strong enough, everybody elected me to be the new Black Wind King to lead the Black Wind Fleet to continue the journey. This is about everything that happened. How about it? Is there anybody here who is not convinced?"

Li Yao crossed his fingers, his knuckles cracking, while he eyed all the Immortal Cultivators in the conference room provocatively.

All the Immortal Cultivators swallowed hard, picturing how "Vulture Li Yao" seized power through schemes and bloody coups. They were even less courageous to say or do anything.

Qi Yuanbao, however, knew that he had been cornered, and it wouldn't help at all no matter how he begged for mercy. He could only go on and shout, "What about the barbarians? The messages you sent back stating that you discovered quite a few worlds of barbarians at the edge of the cosmos, and it would take at least a couple of years to complete the conquest!"

"It's just a strategic deception to trick the government. You bought that bulls*it? How can an Immortal Cultivator be as naïve as you?"

Li Yao scorned and looked at Qi Yuanbao with his nostrils. "Yes, it's true we found a few worlds of barbarians at the edge of the cosmos. However, with the thunderous might of the Black Wind Fleet, our army was naturally unstoppable and invincible.

"Among our enemies, the queen of the barbarians was the only one who was tough and caused us some minor trouble, but after I joined the battle in person and fought three hundred rounds with her, she was still subdued by me and followed all my instructions.

"Now that even their queen had submitted to me, how could the other barbarians resist? In less than one year, the few worlds of barbarians all surrendered. They were officially included in the Imperium's territory and became my land.

"As for why we did not tell the truth to the court, it's very simple. If the court had learned that the war was over and the Imperium now had new territory, wouldn't there have been infinite additional trouble? Perhaps other fleets would've been sent to claim the victory, or experts from the four Kurfürst families would've infiltrated my land. They might have even asked His Majesty to issue a decree that mobilizes the Black Wind Fleet from the edge of the cosmos to another battlefield, right?

"You must've been through a lot of such tricks, everybody. The Black Wind Fleet had floated in space for a hundred years before we finally found a place to live in. How could I have allowed anybody to take it away?

"Therefore, on one hand, I delayed the court from sending anybody to my place with the excuse that the war was intense, and on the other hand, I have returned to the center of the cosmos —despite the risks—to investigate the situation in the Imperium after a hundred years before making any plans.

"Before, I was only thinking to bribe the four families and the few important ministers in the court with a fortune so that they could speak for the Black Wind Fleet and permit us to live and rest at the edge of the cosmos. But after I saw what I saw in the homeland of the Imperium, I couldn't help but have new plans!"

The few senior managers looked at each other in bewilderment and asked, "What plans?"

Li Yao declared formally, one word after another, "Same as everybody here—Honor the Majesty, Settle the Rebels, and Renew the Imperium!"

"Alright…"

The Immortal Cultivators all took a long breath in relief. Whether or not Li Yao was telling the truth, he was at least not on the four Kurfürst families' side, which was the best news.

"Black Wind King, we just heard you saying that you were… the Butcher of the Cultivators and the Destroyer of the Star Ocean Republic."

The senior manager in the leader continued cautiously, "What do you mean by that? Are they only your… honorary titles, or do they have deeper meanings?"

"Honorary titles? No, no, no, I am a serene and practical man, and I hate the pretentious honorable titles most!"

Li Yao declared proudly, "I forgot to tell you that the Black Wind Fleet caught up to Firefly, which had fled a thousand years ago, twenty years ago, fishing up all the Cultivators of the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic. The last Cultivator on Firefly was slain by me in person!"

"What?"

Four of the five senior managers rose to their feet. "You have really destroyed Firefly?"

Emotionlessly, Li Yao retrieved plenty of jade chips and various kinds of medals of the "rightful government of the Star Ocean Republic", tossing them at the four managers. "Those jade chips were all the products of the highest standard of the Star Ocean Republic. The ways they were polished and their storage structure are entirely different from the generic models of the Imperium. I believe you can certainly tell the difference, right? Inside the jade chips are parts of the voyage logs of Firefly and the structures of the critical parts. It is barely possible to fabricate such details too.

"As for those medals, hehe, they would've been pursued and fought over by all the experts at the center of the cosmos a thousand years ago, but today, they are just my toys.

"Perhaps it is a great event that Firefly was destroyed, but in my eyes, those fleeing Cultivators were nothing but cockroaches and rats. Is it anything to be proud of if you step on a cockroach or a rat and kill them? Do I really need to lie about such trivia?"

Chapter 2545: Brave but Brainless

In the Imperium of True Human Beings' hierarchy of accomplishments, annihilating the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic was a remarkable achievement only secondary to destroying the Alliance of the Holy Covenant. They did not expect that Li Yao would talk about it in such a casual way as if he really did not care about it.

The senior managers, managers, shareholders, and partners of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were all knowledgeable and cunning people who had traveled a hundred years in the erratic sea of stars, but they did not know what to say when faced with such a "weirdo" like Li Yao.

It was too important an issue. The four senior managers, having no time to bother with any courtesy, took a few jade chips respectively and studied them with all their attention for a long time, before they looked at each other and nodded their heads. Their conclusion was the same. Those jade chips were indeed the magical equipment of the Star Ocean Republic from a thousand years ago, and they all carried the seal of the so-called "rightful government", which was impossible for other people to duplicate. Also, the voyage logs and the confidential files inside looked convincing too. If they were not true, it would've been barely possible to make them up in such a detailed way.

Observing them coldly, Li Yao retrieved a handful of jade chips, marrow crystals, and medals from his Cosmos Ring and threw them at the four senior managers, before he said coldly, "With a great battle coming near, it is indeed necessary to be more vigilant. I know that I have shown up with a lot of mysteries too. It's right of you to be more careful.

"Those jade chips are the items that the Black Wind Fleet has been using for a hundred years, with the signatures of the experts of the Black Wind Fleet on them. Right, there's also a secret letter from Di Feiwen, the deputy commander of the Black Wind Fleet. He is also a manager of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. I believe that there must be tokens that only you can understand in his letter, right?

"Rest assured, Di Feiwen is not dead yet. He is now in full charge of the construction of the 'New Black Wind Dominion' as the financial minister. His life is much more comfortable than yours. He should've stated in the letter that I am a friend and a partner that you can absolutely trust, and whatever secret agreements you reached with Heiye Ming a hundred years ago, you can negotiate with me about them as well!"

"A secret letter from Di Feiwen?"

The four senior managers were refreshed. They hurried to study the letter together.

Di Feiwen had been away from the Imperium for a hundred years. He was in fact a senior manager of the older generation. The juniors like Qi Yuanbao who emerged later actually filled up the vacancies of people like Di Feiwen.

At that time, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had not expanded to what it was today. Helping the Black Wind Fleet to do the big job required huge determination and stakes.

Since Di Feiwen was qualified to plan and implement such a great gamble, it was not hard to imagine his status in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Now that Li Yao had returned with the news of the Black Wind Fleet, it meant that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' investment a hundred years ago was about to be rewarded. How could they not be serious?

The few senior managers perused Di Feiwen's jade chip for a long time. After unlocking it with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' secret technique, they discovered that Di Feiwen's letter was rather ambiguous without many details. He only stated that he was safe and sound and had a thriving career. He also asked his fellows in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to absolutely trust every word that Vulture Li Yao, the new Black Wind King, said, assuring that they would make a huge fortune out of it.

The letter was simple, but Di Feiwen had left "Hidden Mark" between the lines that could only be perceived by the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. It was a very special technique. By touching the "Hidden Mark", it would be able to sense the body and mental conditions of the person who left the mark.

The four senior managers touched the letter one by one, all agreeing that Di Feiwen was physically and mentally healthy and steady when he wrote the letter, without suffering any torture or coercion.

When they studied the jade chips, medals, and voyage logs that Li Yao presented, they found that the items were all authentic without any error on the details at all.

At this moment, the four senior managers finally took a long breath in relief and were more or less convinced by Li Yao's crazy story.

They pushed the jade chips and the letter to Qi Yuanbao, apparently hinting Qi Yuanbao to study them too.

Qi Yuanbao's eyes were bloodshot. He looked at Li Yao's jade chips as if they were a pile of squeaking crystal bombs that were about to explode. He was reluctant to extend his hands no matter what, and he simply floated in the midair lethargically like a zombie, making people think that he indeed chose a great nickname as "Hanged Man"!

Seeing the expressions on the few senior managers' faces, the managers, shareholders, investors, and business partners around were all shocked, knowing that Li Yao's identity as the "Black Wind King" had probably been proved.

In fact, the more quick-witted ones among them were already convinced without looking at the proof.

That was because Li Yao bluntly confessed his secret a moment ago. He claimed that he had been a Mr. Nobody in the beginning and only rose abruptly after picking up a technique named "Star Absorption Art", which granted him the ability to swallow other people's spiritual energy to strengthen himself!

Such a technique where one strengthened oneself by harming other people would significantly undermine the peace and solidarity of the Immortal Cultivators, which made it a taboo for the Immortal Cultivators. Dongfang Wang, the previous Prime Minister of the Imperium, fell exactly because of that.

Of course, Dongfang Wang had been dissecting living people and even absorbing them into dry bodies to transplant their spiritual roots or gold cores. Such evil arts of "cannibal training" were not the same as swallowing spiritual energy.

There were many techniques similar to the "Star Absorption Art" among the Immortal Cultivators; some more effective than others. A lot of people had been training in secret too. Most of the techniques would not cause the death of the swallowed. But still, few people would admit that they had been doing such things.

Out of their expectation, Li Yao had confessed it brusquely, not taking it seriously at all.

It left two impressions on the many Immortal Cultivators:

Firstly, he must've been speaking the truth, because nobody would voluntarily demean themselves by confessing such things.

Secondly, the man was just a naïve magnate who had suddenly grown rich. He only succeeded because he found an extraordinary art in a certain relic luckily and because most of the experts in the Black Wind Fleet were hibernating. Despite his unparalleled combat ability, he did not have enough training on his mind at all!

All in all, their first impression was not wrong. It was just a pure warrior with more brawn than brain. His combat ability might be very high, but so what? He would be counting money for them even if he were sold by them!

At the very least, as long as they held back his arrogance on the surface, they did not need to worry about being deceived by him if they were to cooperate with him.

As for whether or not the "Star Absorption Art" was a taboo, the Immortal Cultivators all wished that it was. Should they end on bad terms in the future, it would be an excellent weapon to lambast him!

For a moment, the many Immortal Cultivators were all wearing a mysterious smile. They were all convinced by what Li Yao said, regarding him as the real… Black Wind King, the Immortal Cultivator of Immortal Cultivators, and the worst nightmare for the Cultivators!

"Black Wind King, please forgive our prudence. After all, you should know that a great accident just happened in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors today, and we lost our leader."

The old senior manager respectfully gave all the jade chips and medals back to Li Yao, before he asked carefully, "However, we still do not understand what the Black Wind Fleet has been through in the past hundred years, or what has happened between you and the queen since you came to the Imperium."

"It's very simple. I meant to cooperate with the queen sincerely in the cause to renew the Imperium while we made a business deal in the meantime."

Li Yao sniffed angrily. "But I did not expect that the queen's appetite was bigger than I thought. She intended to swallow me as a whole since I was all by myself.

"Hehe, now that I was bold enough to return to the Imperium on my own, I certainly had my own reliance, and how could I have succumbed so easily? After I escaped from the capital, it occurred to me that the powerful people in the capital were not on the same boat as the people who rose from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium after all. I should've listened to Di Feiwen and come to talk with Jin Yuyan and the rest of you sooner!"

The few senior managers looked at each other and asked, "May we ask what business you want to talk with Director Jin about?"

"I do not 'want' to talk with him; the deal has already been made. It's about the reconstruction of the abandoned worlds at the edge of the cosmos!"

Li Yao said, "We collected a lot of confidential files from Firefly during the campaign to eliminate the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic. As it turned out, Firefly found dozens of abandoned Sectors in the past hundred years. Now, the coordinates of those worlds are in the hands of the Black Wind Fleet. In fact, most of the habitable planets have undestroyed atmospheres and acceptable industrial infrastructures. They can be easily rebuilt with a tiny bit of initial capital and labor.

"Furthermore, according to the files of prospection on Firefly, some abandoned worlds even had primeval relics below the ground. If any of them is found luckily, the cost to build ten Sectors will be covered!

"Also, like I said just now, I have just conquered a few remote worlds of barbarians. Although those worlds are barren, they do have comfortable sunlight and atmospheres. The aggressive barbarians are qualified warriors too. There are also many mother lodes deep below the ground that are beyond the abilities of the barbarians to excavate, but things will be different with the advanced technologies of the Imperium."

The more the Immortal Cultivators listened, the further they craned their necks.

Li Yao blinked his eyes and sniffed. "To be honest, I wasn't going to share the coordinates and the development rights with anyone. After all, the Black Wind Fleet carried tremendous colonization starships and population. With a few hundred years, we can digest as many barbaric worlds or abandoned worlds as possible.

"However, after I discovered the subtle situation in the Imperium, I changed my mind and began to consider making a deal with some capable partners with parts of the coordinates and development rights."

Chapter 2546: Nonexistent Agreement

The eyes of the senior manager were glittering, and he rubbed his mustache to cover his cramping lips. He asked, "What kind of deal are we talking about, Black Wind King?"

Glancing at the four senior managers, Li Yao smiled casually. "Do I need to say it out, everyone? If you cannot see what deal I'm trying to negotiate with you as the senior managers of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, I'll be starting to wonder if you are qualified and smart enough to accomplish the unprecedented deal."

The four senior managers were slightly stunned. Then, another senior manager who was younger hurried to say, "How can we not know what is on the Black Wind King's mind? Although you have discovered almost a hundred abandoned worlds at the edge of the Imperium, learning their coordinates and developing them profoundly are two wholly different things!

"You only have the colonization resources and population of five Sectors, which may not be sufficient even for the construction of the barbaric worlds, much less rebuilding even more Sectors.

"Therefore, the coordinates of those abandoned Sectors, for the Black Wind King, are just ducks hanging high in the sky, greasy, fragrant, but unreachable!"

"Exactly!"

The second senior manager jumped out, with a look of "I've totally seen you through" on his face. With a cunning smile, he said, "We are all smart people. Why don't we stop beating around the bush? I assume that the Black Wind King never really intended to rebuild those abandoned worlds in hundreds of years through hard work, did you? There will be too many variables during the hundreds of years. Besides, the experts of the Black Wind Fleet, with their tough and brutal nature, are the most excellent warriors, but they are not the dutiful and diligent pioneers!

"Instead of spending hundreds of years working on the uncertainties, it may be a better option to pack up those abandoned planets and sell them at a good price, earning more concrete benefits—the Black Wind King must've snuck back into the Imperium with such an intention, right? Only a critical business like that deserves you to come in person. If you are only here to investigate the latest situation of the Imperium, the mission could've been carried out by any random expert."

"However, the moment the Black Wind King returned to the Imperium, you were involved in mayhem and realized that the Imperium today was on the brink of falling apart if not complete destruction. Heroes like the Black Wind King were needed more than ever to save the day!"

The third senior manager said confidently, "If I were the Black Wind King, I would've been a hundred times more ambitious too. The abandoned worlds at the edge of the cosmos may be good, but how can they be compared to the Black Wind King's homeland?

"Trading the coordinates and development rights of the abandoned worlds for the five worlds including the Black Wind Sector or even more developed worlds around… Or taking over the banner of the reformists and becoming the hero who revives the Imperium, to be remembered by all future generations… That's what the Black Wind King and the Black Wind Fleet want!"

"It is a pity—"

The old senior manager concluded, "—that the queen did not seem fond of ambitious heroes like the Black Wind King. Or rather, the reformists only have one banner, which is to be carried by one person ahead of all the other Immortal Cultivators. Therefore, a… minor misunderstanding happened between the two of you. Then, the Black Wind King came here to negotiate with the 'old friends' of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for cooperation!"

The four senior managers were all the most experienced and smart guys. Although Li Yao said only a few words, they had inferred his intentions in a reasonable and convincing way. The managers, shareholders, and investors around all nodded their heads in agreement.

Li Yao's eyes were popping out as he stared at the four senior managers without blinking.

It was not until the four senior managers were almost creeped out that he finally cackled and clapped his hands, raising his thumbs at the senior managers. "Haha, hahahaha! Impressive, very impressive! Do you know why I like the smart people of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors? Because you are all f*cking geniuses! Communicating with you does not need any effort. I do not even need to open my mouth for you to know what I am thinking. It's almost like you've all practiced mind reading before!

"Then, let's cut the crap. Do you think the business is acceptable?"

The senior managers looked at each other and then at the other managers and shareholders on the seats around. Seeing that everybody was fascinated and deep in thought, they knew that the participants were already tempted and were simply evaluating how much profits the Black Wind King had to offer.

"If I may ask the Black Wind King—"

The old senior manager asked cautiously, "The coordinates of how many abandoned worlds are stored in Firefly's database? Also, what are the specifications of the barbaric worlds that you conquered? What's the quantity of population, resources, and money needed for the first period of development, and how long will it take for them to be returned? Also, if we are going to establish new sailing routes between the homeland and the edge of the cosmos, are there suitable jump points? What about the fuel consumption on the way? We cannot talk about the business without knowing the details first.

"Also, more importantly of all, if the Black Wind King is generously offering the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors such a great gift, what do you want from us in return?

"What I want is very simple. Like what you said just now, what I want first is the five worlds including the Black Wind Sector. As the hometown of many experts in the Black Wind Fleet, they are of paramount significance and must not fall to the hands of other people."

Li Yao said coldly and demandingly, "Also, after we do crush the four Kurfürst families and reestablish the Imperium, I will have the returns that are due for me. I do not care about being remembered by the future generations, but it won't be an outrageous request to expand the scale of the Black Wind Fleet by a couple of times, right?"

The senior managers shook their heads at the same time. "No, not at all."

"Hmm…"

Satisfied, Li Yao nodded his head. "In exchange, since I am not interested in the trivia about the reconstruction of the abandoned worlds or the operation and colonization, is it really a problem to entrust all the affairs to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors? I don't care about the specific details. All in all, I have only one price, for everyone to benefit from this! I have no family or friends after returning to the Imperium after a hundred years. Now that we have met each other today by the arrangement of fate, everybody here will be my best friend. Since you are my best friends, we should eat meat and make fortunes together without leaving anyone behind, right?"

Everyone's eyes were glittering like burning holes because of his declaration.

"Speaking of which, since we are best friends, reciprocity should naturally be honored."

Li Yao narrowed his eyes, his voice filled with the most intense stink of blood. "The distribution of the duck is for the next step. For now, we have to stick together and defeat the four Kurfürst families first! Otherwise, we will not be cutting the duck but will be cut as ducks before getting split and swallowed by other people!

"I do not worry about it too much. After all, I have my Black Wind Fleet. Even if the four Kurfürsten were to learn about what I did, I'm afraid that they would have to turn a blind eye to it and show me some respect. But I don't think everybody here can escape the disaster. Hehehehe!"

His crazy laughter made everybody look awful again.

"Since we have joined the cause to renew the Imperium, we have put aside our own ending, and we will certainly fight with our best. Please rest assured about that, Black Wind King."

The old senior manager was still not letting it go. "However, if you could reveal some of the details of the enormous business you talked about, Black Wind King, I'm sure that it will significantly boost the morale."

"Is that so?"

Li Yao frowned and said rather impatiently, "That's what Jin Yuyan said too. He pestered me for three days and three nights until my head was dizzy before we finalized the details into a secret agreement. You'll know them after you take out the agreement and read it."

"Well—"

The old senior manager was stunned for a moment. Then he said with a bitter smile, "Black Wind King, you should know that our Director Jin has unfortunately perished. Where can we find the secret agreement that he signed with you?"

"It's simple. Jin Yuyan had been paranoid and worried that somebody was going to murder him. In case of accidents, he told me the place where the secret agreement was kept as well as the retrieval code."

Li Yao said, "He stored the secret agreement in your central trade database, with four retrieval codes and three other safety measures, namely… Huh, Manager Qi, your face is so awful that you almost look like a hanged man. Is there a problem?"

Qi Yuanbao was too shocked by what Li Yao said to control himself. He cried subconsciously, "Lies! Lies! You are telling lies! This whole thing is highly illogical! How could our Director Jin have told an outsider the place where the signed secret agreement was kept and the way to retrieve it? Why didn't he tell other directors or his wife? That is simply too weird!"

"Fair enough. Now that you put it in such a way, it is indeed weird and illogical!"

Li Yao said quickly, "Then, in order to prove my innocence, why don't you retrieve the document that I talked about and see for yourselves? The truth will immediately be out."

"Well—"

Qi Yuanbao was more than angry. "How can the secret documents of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors be read by any random outsider?"

"It doesn't matter. I'm not interested in your secret documents at all."

Li Yao smiled and said, "I'm aware that under normal circumstances, only your director is qualified to open the central trade database. However, in emergencies, five senior managers can open it too, if they unlock it together.

"Then, I would like the five of you to check whether the document that I talked about a moment ago is a secret agreement… or something else."

Chapter 2547: Inescapable Soul!

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The other four senior managers, as well as all the other managers, shareholders, and investors, stared at Qi Yuanbao.

Wuying Qinxin stood nearby, overwhelmed. The big shots that belonged to the "Wuying group" were rendered speechless too.

"Manager Qi."

The old senior manager said, "What do you think of the Black Wind King's suggestion?"

"I-I… He's lying! Trust me, he has told the greatest lie!"

Qi Yuanbao couldn't have felt more wronged. Bashing his chest, he said, "He is not the Black Wind King at all. He's fake! Fake!"

"We have checked the jade chips, medals, warship structures, and star maps that the Black Wind King brought over. Everything is authentic without the slightest flaw."

The old senior manager said peacefully, "If you think otherwise, you are free to check them yourself. Speaking of which, you said so yourself a moment ago, that the Black Wind King was too strong and the Colossus was too finely-made to be products of the Imperium today, didn't you? Isn't it another proof of the Black Wind King's identity?"

"Well…"

Lost for words, Qi Yuanbao blushed and said, "Perhaps it was not the Black Wind Fleet that destroyed Firefly, but Firefly that destroyed the Black Wind Fleet! That's how he got the items! He… He's not an Immortal Cultivator but a Cultivator!"

Everybody was amused by what he said.

Holding his arms, Li Yao sniffed. "Manager Qi, I did not know that you were so imaginative. How strong was the Black Wind Fleet, and how strong was Firefly? Even if the Black Wind Fleet lost most of the starships in a cosmic storm and had only a tiny branch fleet left, Firefly still couldn't have resisted it! I am a Cultivator? Haha, hahahaha. It's truly the joke of the year!"

"Yes, I'm saying that you are a Cultivator!"

Like a lunatic, Qi Yuanbao pointed at Li Yao's nose and demanded, "If you want to prove that you are not a Cultivator, just kill a random person!"

"That's not a problem. Bring your family here, and I'll mince each and every one of them."

Li Yao's eyes were suddenly even colder than ice. Like the god of slaughter who had just crawled out of the deepest level of hell, he said, one word after another, "However, why don't you retrieve the document and take a look at it first? I can kill your family after that. You seem very reluctant to let other people see the document. Why?"

"Yes, Manager Qi, it's just a document that only the five of us can check. It won't harm anybody. Why are you so nervous?"

The old manager appeared more and more suspicious as he stared at Qi Yuanbao. "I didn't want to talk about it in front of so many managers and shareholders, but you are really acting weird today. Is there anything clandestine about the document? Even if there is, are the four of us not qualified to learn about it either?"

"Of course not. Trust me! You have to trust me!"

Qi Yuanbao was like a desperate dog that had been pushed into a corner. His forehead was covered with beads of sweat and his body was shivering hard as he cried hoarsely, "He's not the Black Wind King at all and not even an Immortal Cultivator, but an out-and-out Cultivator! Also, the incident in 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' was done by him too! I guarantee with my head that he was the perpetrator. It was he who ruined the work of so many investors!"

Everybody was shocked at what he said!

Li Yao, however, raised his neck and burst into laughter. "I've never seen anyone as insane and crazy as you. You are biting like a rabid dog now that you are doomed, aren't you? Blaming the destruction of 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' on me? Why don't you say that Emperor Shenwu was assassinated by me?

"I stand by what he said. If you accuse me of being a Cultivator or even the culprit who destroyed 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky', I can defend myself later. But first things first, why do you insist on not retrieving the document? Is the document not the secret agreement between Jin Yuyan and me, but one between you and… the four Kurfürst families?"

The indictment was like a thunder on a sunny day, electrocuting everyone's soul.

The other four senior managers immediately grew hostile. Making gestures, they hinted to the soldiers who were loyal to them to scatter and watch over those loyal to Qi Yuanbao.

The soldiers loyal to Qi Yuanbao, and even the former members of "Life Claimer", the assassin group, were mostly not involved in Qi Yuanbao's scheme. They had no idea what was going on at all when other people took precautions against them.

Seeing that their leader was stuttering impassively, as if he had truly done something wrong, how could they have any morale to fight?

"Manager Qi, the Black Wind King does have a point."

The old senior manager said solemnly, "It won't take much time to condense our soul signatures and retrieve a document. Whatever you want to say, let's wait until we read the document, shall we?"

Qi Yuanbao looked at everybody, as well as Wuying Qinxin who was hiding in a corner, and then at Li Yao hatefully. He nodded his head heavily. "Alright. I don't know anything about any document. Why shouldn't we?

"However, there are too many people here, and the secret may be leaked. Let's go to a more confidential room to check it!"

"About that…"

The old senior manager looked at Li Yao.

Li Yao made a "be my guest" gesture.

The five senior managers discussed for a while and then boarded on the same giant floating disc, descending to the space below the grand conference room.

Right when a round gate was opened on the floor like a blossoming flower, and the five senior managers were about to enter, an abrupt change took place!

Qi Yuanbao suddenly roared and turned into a streak of brightness, dashing out of the window as fast as lightning!

Even though somebody guessed that he would do something, they did not expect that he would escape so ruthlessly. The defenders of the Seven Seas Grand Market were under the command of Qi Yuanbao in the first place. Therefore, the soldiers looked at each other in bewilderment, wondering if they should attack their superior. As a result, Qi Yuanbao dashed out of the window after only a few leaps.

Exclamations echoed in the grand conference room. Qi Yuanbao's escape proved his betrayal and Li Yao's identity as "Black Wind King".

Li Yao had already predicted Qi Yuanbao's move. With a sniff, he blinked to the window, his body turning into ferocious black smoke, and he snatched Qi Yuanbao's back.

Shua!

A gigantic black hand appeared out of nowhere and grabbed Qi Yuanbao, with such an enormous strength that his whole back was splitting apart. Even the bones were clearly visible.

Qi Yuanbao grunted, and his body fell from the altitude of hundreds of meters.

However, an invisible strength leaked out of his pores into thousands of streams, continuing to flee.

Even though the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had been surrounded by thousands of soldiers, few detected the existence of Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul. Even the Immortal Cultivators with the keenest senses only felt a gentle breeze passing by.

Qi Yuanbao was about to escape when Li Yao sniffed and snapped his fingers, remotely controlling the "Arsonist" to turn around and blow out deafening sound waves at where Qi Yuanbao fled in panic.

Bam!

As if ten thousand bells had been hit at the same time, the soundwaves congregated into visible blasts and raged out like a flood.

All the Immortal Cultivators were dizzy, their ears humming and bleeding.

Crystal suits, shuttles, and battle puppets were all sparking, many units temporarily malfunctioned.

In the overlapping soundwaves, Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul let out desperate screams and finally revealed his real appearance. He struggled in pain like a cluster of hazy, unpredictable mist.

"Qi Yuanbao—Hanged Man!"

Not far away, two angry roars echoed at the same time!

They were exactly Black Flag King and Revenant From The Dead Sea, the other two super assassins on par with Qi Yuanbao. They locked onto Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul at the same time, one from the left and the other from the right!

If Qi Yuanbao still had a heart, it would've been falling apart because of fear.

Even though his remaining soul couldn't have been more solid after being tempered with secret techniques hundreds of times, it was on the verge of dissolution too.

Black Flag King and Revenant From The Dead Sea were both super assassins in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Even though they might be slightly weaker than him, the gap was not very huge.

Besides, there was still a brutal monster that was Li Yao, who was eyeing Qi Yuanbao coldly from his back, occupying most of Qi Yuanbao's senses and computational ability.

After one moment of carelessness, his soul felt a sudden pain after a bullet that had been carved with special rune arrays to deal with ghosts pierced through it.

Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss!

Qi Yuanbao completely lost the strength to condense his soul, turning from a slightly fuzzy shape of a human into a monster that was waving the tentacles crazily. The tentacles were also breaking and falling apart one after another. He was about to be completely obliterated.

Li Yao waved his hands and released a rusted, crippled artificial body from his Cosmos Ring before he threw it at Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul.

Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul was like someone being drowned who had found a straw to cling to. Having no time to bother if the artificial body was a trap, the streams of smoke from his soul crawled into it in a hurry.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

The moment all the remaining soul entered the artificial body, Li Yao condensed a rune casually, and dazzling electric arcs immediately emanated from the artificial body. Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul was screaming in excruciating pain because of the electrocution.

"Just stay inside and behave!"

Li Yao remotely dragged the artificial body that was surrounded by electric arcs to himself, before he spoke to Qi Yuanbao in a soft voice, "Don't waste your time struggling, and don't say anything about 'His Majesty'. If 'His Majesty' learns that you have been so reckless and sabotaged his thousand-year plan, do you think you can possibly be kept alive?"

Chapter 2548: Be Loyal to Me!

Dark smoke popped up from the rusted artificial body. That was the shiver of Qi Yuanbao's remaining soul in fear.

"As a matter of fact, you are now basically screwed."

Li Yao said unhurriedly, "Use your nonexistent brain to think whose side you should take, and who you should wish to win in the end. 'His Majesty' must detest you. If he learns what you and your sister have done, it will be merciful of him if he tears the two of you apart alive.

"The four Kurfürst families certainly do not have any place reserved for you and your sister either, considering that so many assassins from the four families have been killed. Most of them were the central members of the main bloodline in the four families.

"Basically, you are now ruined and doomed. The universe may be big, but there is no place for you and your sister now!

"However, in your dilemma, there is still a narrow, zigzagging way out and an expert that deserves the loyalty of you and your sister."

Inside the artificial body, Qi Yuanbao's eyes became more and more widened.

"Congratulations. Your guess is correct. The only way for your sister and you to survive is to pledge loyalty to the Black Wind King, Vulture Li Yao!"

Li Yao grinned hideously. "There's no need to feel strange. Technically, there is nothing personal between you and me. Even though you did set Jin Yuyan up with plots, he is nothing more than my business partner. As long as the business is made, I don't care whether he is dead or alive!

"Listen, everything I said just now is true. I am really the Black Wind King, and I'm sincerely hoping to cooperate with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. However, one can never be too careful. I have only just returned to the Imperium after all, and I do not know the situation inside the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors very well. I'm in need of a few 'good friends' who are familiar with it to help me deal with the issues on the Business Alliance's side and keep an eye on those cunning merchants, including Jin Yuyan himself!

"I will not earn or lose anything whether you and your sister are dead or alive. Therefore, as long as you submit to me and show your worth, I will try my best to keep you alive!"

"Well…"

From the metal throat that had been sealed for a long time, a bitter voice echoed.

"Don't act like you are a chaste woman. Have your sister and you never committed any betrayals before?"

Li Yao sniffed and said, "Just think about it carefully. If you are unwilling to do that, I will not force you to. However, Jin Yuyan is not dead yet. Your soul signature is not necessary to open the central trade database. Are you and your sister ready to embrace the fury of Jin Yuyan and other investors?

"As the saying goes, stopping somebody from making money is like killing their parents. The plot of yours almost burnt billions of free coins of the investors to the ground. Tsk, tsk, tsk. That's even more outrageous than killing all their ancestors. How do you think your sister and you will end?"

Qi Yuanbao shuddered hard, with every ragged component on his body clattering. He lowered his head and his voice. "My king…"

"That's right. Those who suit their actions to the times are wise. Very soon, you will realize that submitting to 'Vulture Li Yao, the Black Wind King' is not something to be ashamed of, but the ultimate glory for the experts at the center of the cosmos!"

Li Yao opened his hands and patted Qi Yuanbao's iron shell, saying casually, "Now, go back and open the central trade database with the other four senior managers. Confess everything you have done dutifully, and I will ensure that you are not killed!"

Fear and hesitation bloomed out of Qi Yuanbao's manmade eyes.

"Opportunities knock only once. It's all up to your own attitude now."

Li Yao said coldly, "Think carefully. Jin Yuyan is not dead in the first place. Even without the confession of you and your sister, the truth will still be revealed very soon. I'm merely hoping to boost the confidence and morale of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors as quickly as possible.

"You haven't been with me long enough to know that I am a man of my words, so I don't blame you. However, now that I have promised to spare your life, nobody will take it from you.

"On the very contrary, I think highly of you as a great talent. Being a senior manager of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors doesn't deserve your gifts. The new Imperium after the war will be a new country where all the old stuff must be reinvigorated. At the center of the cosmos or in the New Black Wind Dominion at the edge of the cosmos, people like you deserve greater stages for you to carry out your ambitions."

The fear and suspicion in Qi Yuanbao's eyes gradually turned into intense greed and thirst.

"Of course, there is no free lunch in the world. What you can get from me depends on what you can give me."

Expressionless, Li Yao pressed Qi Yuanbao's iron skull hard with coldness beaming out of his eyes. In squeaky noises, a deep mark of his palm was left on Qi Yuanbao's head, as if it were his stamp.

He then tossed Qi Yuanbao out casually and said, "Go now. Live or die, you may choose for yourself now!"

Dragging the incomplete, rusted artificial body, Qi Yuanbao floated in the midair, stunned for a long time.

He looked around, only to discover that he was surrounded by his former comrades who were now hostile and vigilant. His identity as a traitor and a spy had been spread out. There was nothing he could do to turn it around.

Not far away, next to the window of the headquarters, Wuying Qinxin, his sister, seemed to be watching him.

His sister's identity hadn't been exposed yet, and she was still free to move around.

However, there was not much time left for her precious freedom now.

Therefore, his sister looked rather frustrated and seemed about to leap off from the height.

Subconsciously, Qi Yuanbao touched the palm mark that Li Yao had just left. Making up his mind, he flew back to the headquarters voluntarily to turn himself in!

Looking at his shivering back, Li Yao smiled and made a gesture at a sniper's spot on the top of a skyscraper not far away.

…

In the sniper's spot on the top of the skyscraper, the person on one of her knees and holding a heavy anti-ghost sniper rifle in her hand was exactly Red Lady, the super assassin who betrayed and joined the four Kurfürst families earlier.

At this moment, Red Lady was still in the rigid posture on one of her knees, soaked in sweat with an extremely pale face.

If one were to observe more carefully, they would notice that a red light was swirling quickly in feeble hums right above the back of her head, ready to pierce through her cerebrum and cerebellum to eliminate her soul anytime.

"S-Senior…"

Red Lady stammered in fear, "Qi Yuanbao has surrendered. I have accomplished my mission. Could… Could you spare my life and let me go?"

Hiss! Hiss! Hiss! Hiss!

The red light swirling above the back of her head split into thousands of red threads that crawled into her ears and cackled gloomily. "Of course, but you should understand who your new master is now, shouldn't you?"

After a shiver, Red Lady replied obediently, "It's… It's 'Vulture Li Yao, the Black Wind King'!"

"Very good. You are smart. Smart people always live longer."

The red threads flowed out of Red Lady's ears again and focused on the intangible shadow of the mental devil. "Neither the four families nor the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors has any prospects. The future of the Imperium is destined to be the Black Wind King's. Bear that in mind, and you will live longer and better!"

"S-Senior."

Sensing that the guy was about to vanish, Red Lady hurried to ask, "May I ask your name, senior?"

"About that…"

The mental devil had completely vanished, but its voice was still popping up inside Red Lady's brain. "If I am going to kill you someday, I'll let you know it."

Hardly had the voice gone when thousands of ants seemed to be biting Red Lady's brain. She fell and cried from the excruciating pain while holding her head; the fear of the mental devil impressed on her forever.

When she raised her head again, there was not any red brightness around anymore. The fiend that had haunted her since half a day ago had finally left.

…

Jin Yuyan was not killed!

He had transplanted his own soul into the body of a female bodyguard through the "Soul Transfer Technique"!

Everything was a scheme that he made with "Vulture Li Yao, the Black Wind King" in order to attract and catch all the spies and traitors hiding within the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, eliminating all the uncertainties for the upcoming battle of Seven Seas!

The astounding news raised surging tides inside the grand conference room.

However, everybody saw with their own eyes that the five senior managers retrieve the surgical data of the "Soul Transfer Technique" from the central trade database. Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin also pleaded guilty and confessed to everything dutifully.

Red Lady, a super assassin who had joined the four families, had shown up on her own and confessed everything too. She also volunteered to give out the locations of quite a few secret bureaus of the four families in the Seven Seas Grand Market to alleviate her punishment.

With the solid evidence, it was impossible for the managers, shareholders, investors, and partners to not believe it.

Based on the convincing evidence, when Jin Yuyan slowly drifted to the center of the grand conference room in the thick and hideous body of the female bodyguard, it was not too unacceptable.

"Everybody, I am Jin Yuyan, and this is my brand-new body."

Jin Yuyan opened his arms so that everybody could see his latest appearance clearly. "As you have seen just now, the whole thing is a scheme that the Black Wind King and I made. Despite the minor error during the implementation, which resulted in the destruction of my real body, I believe that it was a price that had to be paid in order to defeat the four Kurfürst families and to make the businesses of each and every one of us to thrive even more.

"Now, we have won. The clandestine plots of the four Kurfürst families have been completely shattered. Everything is under our control.

"Now, let's welcome 'Vulture Li Yao, the Black Wind King', the most honorable guest of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, with our warmest applause!"

Chapter 2549: The Universe Is Great but Not as Great as Me!

When Li Yao stepped into the conference room of the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors again, everybody looked at him in a way completely different manner from just now.

In their complicated eyes, there was the fear for the Black Wind King, the greed about the edge of the cosmos, and the curiosity of the Black Wind Fleet. It must be noted that Li Yao was not just representing himself but also a most brutal fleet that was hiding in nobody knew where right now!

Although they were all the rulers of the peripheral worlds of the Imperium, the Black Wind King was vastly different from the general warlords and Sector Masters. He was a battle-tested general and had the strength of five Sectors.

At such a subtle, critical moment, the heavy chess piece that was the Black Wind Fleet would certainly tip the scales of victory.

Therefore, everybody was silent while they waited for Li Yao to speak.

Li Yao looked around again and retrieved a few jade chips, saying, "General Jin is quite right. The riot that happened in the Seven Seas Grand Market today was but a scheme that he and I set up together. The victims of the scheme did not just include QI Yuanbao, Wuying Qinxin, and Red Lady, but also a lot of other people seated here.

"As the saying goes, when you pluck a carrot, you will inevitably splash the mud. You know very clearly whether or not you have been wavering or secretly talking to the four Kurfürst families. All the evidence is inside the few jade chips."

After hearing that, everybody exclaimed in shock.

Many people's faces changed greatly, as if they were sitting on a carpet of needles.

Some were hesitating and looking around.

Some other people were clenching their fists, with brutality beaming out of their eyes.

With a smile, Li Yao observed what everybody was doing and suddenly made a movement that surprised everyone. He unleashed his spiritual energy and crushed the few jade chips into pieces, before burning them up in flames. All the data were gone.

"I hate traitors more than anything else. I was going to get to the bottom of it and find out all the guys who were secretly conspiring with the four families, mincing them and letting them die the most miserable death!"

Li Yao said rather creepily. Patting his hands to get rid of the ashes of the jade chips, he suddenly changed his attitude. "However, Director Jin persuaded me not to blame you for what you had done. He said that the four Kurfürst families were powerful and had overwhelming advantages on the surface. It was perfectly normal that somebody sought a way of survival when they couldn't handle the enormous pressure.

"Many of you had been doing business with the four families since more than a hundred years ago. Some of you were even directly backed by the people from the four families. It was already impossible to draw a clear line between the two parties. If I got to the bottom of it, everybody would've been guilty. Even Jin Yuyan himself had done a lot of business and made quite a few friends with the four families. Haha, hahahaha!"

Li Yao cackled again, so unpleasantly that it sounded like a crow hovering above carrion.

"Why, am I not right?"

Li Yao's laughter came to an abrupt end. He glared at everybody. "Why are you not laughing, huh?"

"Hehe… Hehehehe…"

The many managers, shareholders, and investors were all sweating hard. Looking at the aggressive Exos and battle puppets around, they hurried to echo in uneasiness.

"That's right. Laughing is better than crying, and certainly much better than screaming after being skinned by me and hopping around with your bleeding body."

Li Yao continued, "All in all, Director Jin convinced me. Whatever your relationship with the four Kurfürst families was until today, and whether or not you were involved in any plot or scheme, I'll let bygones be bygones. Nobody will bring it up again!

"However, the battle of the Seven Seas is about to begin. The cause of the reformists is in danger. It is time you make a clear decision about your stance.

"The business of gaining advantage from both sides can't last. I know that standing behind you are the Sector Masters, warlords, and experts from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium, if not the important persons in the four Kurfürst families. As their spokespersons, you are discussing in the grand conference room of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors how to resist the coalition fleet of the four families, while your masters are rushing to this place on fierce starships, determined to completely destroy it here. Can there be a more absurd thing in the entire universe?

"It's time to make up your mind, everybody. You have only two options.

"You can side with the four Kurfürst families and completely destroy the Seven Seas Grand Market, the enterprise of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists, the free coins and various resources that you have accumulated through hard work for a hundred years, and the hope for you to change your fate and climb the infinite peaks!

"Yes, it is possible that your life will be safe for now, but you will forever lose the chance to compete with the four Kurfürsten. You and your offspring are destined to be the dogs of the four Kurfürsten that are only qualified to feed on their leftovers.

"We are all very clear that there won't be a second Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. There won't be a second great opportunity for you to change your fate. You will crouch in a corner for all eternity like dogs whose legs have been broken, moaning and crying for help!

"Oh, I almost forgot, even if you want to be dogs, it may be impossible for you to accomplish your wish. Who knows how the four Kurfürsten will treat the people like you who had second thoughts before after they stabilize the situation and take back the capital? Is it really troublesome to start a great purge?

"Hehe, those of you who came from the four families, may I remind you to be cautious, because people always loathe traitors a hundred times more than they loathe enemies.

"Director Jin and I are lenient enough to forget the past, but if the petty leaders of the four Kurfürst families learn what you did here, do you think they will just drop it?"

After hearing that, many people looked even more awful.

"If that is not the result you're looking forward to, you have only the second option now!"

Li Yao suddenly raised his voice, which swept across the entire room like a black storm and rumbling thunder again. He opened his arms, as if he were holding the shoulders of all the Immortal Cultivators. "You can stand on the side of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the reformists, and me! We will fight together with the magnificent Black Wind Fleet to strike the arrogance of the four families and shatter their evil plan to destroy the Seven Seas Grand Market. Then, we will launch a thunderous counterattack to overthrow the four Kurfürsten, carving up the population, resources, and market at the center of the cosmos. Then, you will share the many worlds at the edge of the cosmos with me!

"Eventually, all of us will be the masters of our fate. We will be the most lawless and dominating Immortal Cultivators who control the Imperium and the entire universe!

"The universe is great, but not as great as me. If you do not have such determination but prefer to be an obedient dog, why did you bother to become an Immortal Cultivator in the first place?!"

Li Yao's roar shattered the eardrums and the defense line in the heads of countless Immortal Cultivators.

The investors and partners from the four families had mostly chosen to be present through holograms. Their real bodies were indeed marching at the Seven Seas Space Zone on their respective starships in the middle of an enormous, aggressive coalition fleet exactly like Li Yao said.

It was time to make a final decision!

"Black Wind King—"

Jin Yuyan seized the opportunity to ask, "That being said, where is your Black Wind Fleet exactly, and how good is it after drifting for a hundred years?"

Everybody pricked their ears for the question.

That was right. Li Yao's bragging was not important. What mattered was the Black Wind Fleet!

"The Black Wind Fleet is naturally at where it should be. A saber is the sharpest when it is about to be unsheathed."

Li Yao smiled mysteriously. "As for the strength, you can rest assured about that. Although the Black Wind Fleet floated in the infinite sea of stars for a hundred years, we barely encountered any gory battle. The attrition was not high.

"On the contrary, after crushing Firefly, we received abundant secret treasures of the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic. We also split and reorganized Firefly into a new brand fleet. Many elite warriors on Firefly were captured and educated into Immortal Cultivators, with a much higher combat ability than before.

"Also, the barbaric worlds have also provided us with incessant soldiers. Those fearless barbaric warriors are the best infantry. They are all rubbing their hands impatiently, desperate for a great battle.

"I assure you that the Black Wind Fleet today is multiple times stronger than a hundred years ago. It is definitely a cutthroat dagger!"

"Then—"

His eyes glittering, Jin Yuyan asked, "When will the Black Wind Fleet arrive?"

"It will depend on your performance."

Li Yao sniffed and said, "Getting to the bottom of it, this is your war first and then mine. If you are united and fight together, and the Immortal Cultivators in the coalition fleet of the four families cooperate with you, showing a chance of victory, the Black Wind Fleet will naturally march out at the most critical moment!

"However, if you are all vacillating and hesitative, hoping to take advantage of both sides, why would I bother to help you at such great risks?"

"Fair enough."

Jin Yuyan said, "All in all, as long as we fight hard, the Black Wind Fleet will certainly show up?"

"Of course it will!"

Li Yao declared resolution, "You and I are interdependent on each other and share mutual interests. How can I benefit from it if I watch you be annihilated by the four families while doing nothing?

"The news of my arrival cannot be kept a secret for long. It will spread out to the entire universe. It is safe to say that the four families and I have already fallen out. If I do not cooperate with you to destroy the four families, should I just wait for them to divide and conquer, killing you first before killing me?

"So, just rest assured about that. In short, you can just charge forward boldly without worries, and the Black Wind Fleet will definitely cover you as your strongest support!"

Chapter 2550: The Black King's Palace!

Honestly, if any of the Immortal Cultivators believed Li Yao's bulls*it that they could charge forward valiantly while counting on Li Yao's cover, their brains would've been too seriously wrong for them to survive to this day.

However, strength was most worshipped by the Immortal Cultivators. Compared to the tiny fleet in their hands, the Black Wind Fleet was definitely an irresistible primordial beast.

Besides, as the Immortal Cultivators thought again and again, they believed that Li Yao was not entirely wrong. Two tigers could not live on the same mountain. It was impossible for an ambitious warlord from the periphery like "Black Wind King" to live in peace with the four Kurfürst families.

A war between the Black Wind King and the four Kurfürsten was inevitable. Wasn't the present the best opportunity?

Therefore, while the Black Wind King would not lead the charge for them, if they did fight together and reveal the weakness of the four families' coalition fleet, the Black Wind Fleet would certainly show up and finish the battle in a critical strike.

Li Yao did not give them any chance to think carefully. Raising his right arm high, he clenched his fist and bashed his chest hard, before he waved it out like a saber, a hammer, or a warship that was flying to the heart of the universe.

"Long live the Imperium!"

Taking a deep breath, he roared in a soul-stirring voice, "Our cause will succeed. A brand-new Imperium will arrive. Long live the Imperium, long live the Immortal Cultivators, long live the civilization of mankind!"

It had been a long time since the Immortal Cultivators heard such a sincere and heartfelt roar.

The voice was so pure, genuine, and even extreme that the body of the Black Wind King seemed to be expanding nonstop, further highlighting the selfishness and insignificance of their own souls. It was true. Were they Immortal Cultivators at all when compared to such a Black Wind King?!

"Long live the Imperium!"

"Long live the Immortal Cultivators!"

"Long live the civilization of mankind!"

Whether they meant it or not, all the Immortal Cultivators began to roar together with Li Yao.

The roars flooded out of the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors again like a torrent of thunders, filling the internal district immediately before overflowing to the external district and even the entire Seven Seas Space Zone.

Very soon, all the merchants, killers, mercenaries, and spies lingering in the Seven Seas Grand Market learned the astounding news. The Black Wind King, a capable warrior who was on par with Lei Chenghu but a hundred times more ambitious than him, had returned and declared unambiguously that he was going to resist the four families on the side of the reformists and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors again!

The shocking news was exactly a rapid cardiotonic for the panicked Seven Seas Grand Market.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors again had plenty of capitals. However, capitals had to be combined with violence in order to let out mysterious reactions and unleash a strength a hundred times more than before.

Before, although Lei Chenghu was on the reformists' side, "reformists" and "Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors again" were not exactly the same thing. Also, Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet and the best fleets of the four families were stalling each other, ready to reinforce the battle at the capital anytime. It was impossible for him to jump to the peripheral worlds of the Imperium easily.

Whether or not the garrison of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, together with the mercenaries and the assassin groups that had been temporarily recruited, could resist the overwhelming crush of the four families' coalition fleet, nobody was confident to give a positive answer.

To make things worse, even Director Jin Yuyan was assassinated before the final battle. Their confidence and morale had hit rock bottom.

However, as it turned out, Jin Yuyan was not dead at all but neatly resolved the four families' series of schemes. He had also invited such a powerful person as the Black Wind King to help with the battle!

A well-trained army was much better than a large army. The covetous Black Wind Fleet would certainly bring enormous pressure to the coalition fleet of the four families, adding more unexpected factors to the battle of Seven Seas.

The confidence and morale that had just hit rock bottom immediately bounced back, as shown by the futures index in the Commodity Market.

Before, when the news that Jin Yuyan had been assassinated was out, the whole market lost confidence in the free coin. Everybody was desperately exchanging the free coins they had into all kinds of goods and hoping to escape before the four families' coalition fleet arrived.

For a moment, the prices were soaring, and the free coin had been greatly devalued. The business order was about to be gone.

After the news that Jin Yuyan was not dead and the Black Wind King had arrived, the market was immediately calmed down. The exchange rate between the free coin and the crystal coin of the Imperium was soon stabilized, and it became even higher than before Jin Yuyan's assassination.

It meant that the free coin was more valuable than a day ago!

The free coin was confidence; the confidence of the Immortal Cultivators from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium and even within the four families in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors as well as the reformists.

The value of the free coin almost decided the ending of the battle of Seven Seas.

Very soon, even the soldiers who were stationed at the floating sentry posts at the border of the Seven Seas Space Zone were full of confidence about the final battle and the Black Wind King. Such a troop was definitely usable!

Jin Yuyan took the opportunity to secretly meet with the powerful warlords and magnates at the edge of the Imperium, reaching underground agreements one after another.

The middle-level commanders and the bottom-level officers of the four families were also important targets for him to attract. He promised them thousands of benefits and temptations on one condition—that those people would shift their sides during the battle and join the cause of brightness and justice.

Other than that, he was also busy restoring the order of the Seven Seas Grand Market and dealing with the guys like Qi Yuanbao, Wuying Qinxin, and Red Lady. However Jin Yuyan hated the few "traitors", it was impossible for him to execute them immediately. After all, they were not by themselves but had countless subordinates and allies, like "Bloody Skull" and "Life Claimer", which were trained by themselves in person. 99% of the members of the assassin groups were unaware of their betrayal or their scheme to assassinate Jin Yuyan.

With a war coming near, those people were the pillars to defend the Seven Seas Grand Market. What would those people think if their leader was executed at such a moment? What would the people of the "Wuying group", which was rather close to Wuying Qinxin, think?

Therefore, Jin Yuyan could only follow Li Yao's suggestion and keep Qi Yuanbao, Wuying Qinxin, and Red Lady in custody until the victory of the battle of Seven Seas.

As for the elite assassination squads that the four families sent to infiltrate the Seven Seas Grand Market, they were not as lucky.

Identified by Qi Yuanbao and Red Lady, those people were all captured, and all their secret bureaus and intelligence networks were unrooted too.

They were all the central members of the main bloodlines of the four families. It was barely possible for them to surrender.

Without batting an eye, Jin Yuyan had all the assassins from the four families executed before the Commodity Market, their stinky blood flowing all over the ground, as a gesture of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' determination to crush the four families.

Jin Yuyan was occupied for almost half a day before he finally finished the preliminary preparations for the final battle. He was able to catch his breath and went to Li Yao to discuss the operations in the next step and to seek the answers to the mysteries that had been puzzling him.

Li Yao did not stay in the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. He asked Jin Yuyan to spare a building that was about twenty floors high as the palace for the "Black Wind King".

He also retrieved countless components from the warehouse of the Seven Seas Grand Market, arming the tower that solely belonged to the Black Wind King. Even Jin Yuyan did not know what he was up to inside.

When Jin Yuyan walked into the "palace of the Black Wind King", the inside of the building had been completely deformed by Li Yao's modification.

The whole building was crowded with mysterious defensive arrays. All the floors were drowned in the hazy mist where one couldn't see one's own fingers. In the dark mist, countless raging shadows could be vaguely seen. Now and then, the hoots of fiends, the cries of ghosts, and other creepy noises were echoing.

Jin Yuyan felt that he had stepped into an absurd but horrible nightmare after he walked into the building. He could not sense any of the sounds or information from the outside world.

What a brilliant interferential array on the soul!

Jin Yuyan was secretly alarmed. He knew that what he saw and heard was mostly illusions. The defensive arrays that Li Yao had set up could even deceive the experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage like him. Although it was partly because his soul and his brain were not matched, it was still indicative enough of Li Yao's capabilities in magical equipment modification, deployment of defensive arrays, and soul attack besides fighting.

Where does this monster come from exactly? Not only does he have such a remarkable combat ability, but he is also adept at so many side tricks. This is really…

While Jin Yuyan was thinking, a glamorous and eye-catching black shadow slowly descended from the midair.

Every floor of this building was about three meters tall. However, the black shadow seemed to be dropping from a hundred meters high in the middle of thousands of crows, landing on Jin Yuyan's back without a sound.

Greatly alarmed, Jin Yuyan turned around abruptly, only to discover that thousands of black flames were ignited on the black shadow, tearing it apart and revealing Li Yao's real appearance.

Li Yao at this moment had a skin as pale as a dead man, which was even more eye-catching under the contrast of the black mist. He was only wearing a sloppy black robe that seemed to carry no decorations, but if one were to observe more carefully, they would notice that countless goriest scenes of slaughters had been sewed on the black robe. The astounding black seemed to be made of an ocean of blood that had curdled and dried.

Li Yao's eyes were as profound as the night sky and stained with blood. From the bloodstains, the gold brilliance that was a mix of dominance and brutality was vaguely blooming. He grinned and bare his sharp teeth, and his voice was echoing far and near from all directions at the same time, slithering up from the bottom of Jin Yuyan's feet like countless vipers all the way to the back of Jin Yuyan's ears. "Director Jin, you're here."
